# Registries (population, land, address, etc.): progress report, 2025-09-01 to 2026-09-06

*54 countries. Each row below is carried verbatim from that country's own progress report, which answers a fixed frame of indicators over the period; nothing is written here. A country not listed under an indicator has ***No evidence*** on it in its own report.*

**Progress values.** *Advanced* — a system entered service, a stage was completed or an instrument was made. *Stalled* — a stated target passed without delivery. *Regressed* — an instrument was withdrawn or neutralised, or a reported position worsened. *Mixed* — the indicator's instruments moved in different directions in the period; the clause after the comma names which moved which way. *No change* — the base holds a standing position and nothing in the period touched it. ***No evidence*** — the base holds nothing on this indicator at all. A value may carry a qualifying clause after a comma, as in *Advanced, regulations still pending*.

*The place reports do not share one window; the period above is the range they span.*

## Population register

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | A [national unique identification number is governed by decree](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/JO-FRANCAIS/2023/F2023060.pdf) and the [automated civil status register was created in 2014](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/JO-FRANCAIS/2014/F2014049.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The identifier the estate is built to use is [governed by an executive decree of 6 September 2023](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/JO-FRANCAIS/2023/F2023060.pdf).<br><br>The register beneath it was [created by article 25 bis of the 2014 law amending the 1970 ordinance on civil status](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/JO-FRANCAIS/2014/F2014049.pdf), and its [digitalisation was presented at the annual regional meeting of civil-registry directors general in July 2026](https://apai-crvs.uneca.org/sites/default/files/resourceuploads/day-2-decentralisation-algeria.pdf).<br><br>No population coverage figure, record count or issuance total for the identifier is published at any point, so the instruments are established and their reach is not.</details> | No change, a first stated position with no earlier account behind it |
| Angola | A presidential decree [created an interministerial commission to de-duplicate and update the adult-citizen database](https://novojornal.co.ao/politica/detalhe/pr-cria-comissao-liderada-pelo-ministro-de-estado-para-actualizar-dados-dos-cidadaos-maiores-com-prova-de-vida-que-passou-a-ser-obrigatoria-nos-periodos-pre-eleitorais-72374.html), carrying [a US$275m supplementary credit](https://www.novojornal.co.ao/economia/detalhe/pr-manda-abrir-credito-adicional-suplementar-de-275-milhoes-usd-no-oge2026-para-o-mat-e-duplica-dotacao-do-ministerio-70083.html). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-06-08 — [a presidential decree created an interministerial commission to stabilise civil-identification and adult-citizen database interoperability, biometrically de-duplicate, update residence and consolidate the electoral base](https://novojornal.co.ao/politica/detalhe/pr-cria-comissao-liderada-pelo-ministro-de-estado-para-actualizar-dados-dos-cidadaos-maiores-com-prova-de-vida-que-passou-a-ser-obrigatoria-nos-periodos-pre-eleitorais-72374.html). No technical standard, interface or completion date is on file.<br><br>2026-02-26 — the money came first: [US$275,000,000 was opened inside the 2026 budget for the proof-of-life update of the adult-citizen database, more than doubling the territorial administration ministry's allocation](https://www.novojornal.co.ao/economia/detalhe/pr-manda-abrir-credito-adicional-suplementar-de-275-milhoes-usd-no-oge2026-para-o-mat-e-duplica-dotacao-do-ministerio-70083.html), on reporting rather than a held gazetted order.<br><br>The register is the base of the electoral roll, which is why the proof-of-life operation reported under the electoral register is contested. A biometric de-duplication of the adult population, financed at this scale and governed by a commission rather than a statute, has no data-protection basis stated anywhere on this ledger.</details> | Advanced |
| Benin | The register [connects more than 80 institutions at about 200,000 biometric authentications a day](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202602/benin-reports-75-of-identification-services-accessed-through-digital-platforms), with 25m know-your-customer verifications in 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-02-02 — [the national register of natural persons connects more than 80 institutions at about 200,000 biometric authentications a day, and in 2025 at least 75% of identification services went digital, across 10.5m services and 25m electronic know-your-customer verifications](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202602/benin-reports-75-of-identification-services-accessed-through-digital-platforms), [reported alongside mass adoption of the digital identity](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/benin-advances-e-government-with-mass-digital-id-adoption).<br><br>It was already operating at the start of the window, so what moved is volume, and the volume is the largest of any system on this ledger.<br><br>Nothing published states the error rate, the failed-authentication rate or what happens to a person the register cannot match — which is the question the failed fingerprints found during the charity card campaign, reported under registration of the population, put directly.</details> | Advanced, in volume |
| Botswana | The [National Registration (Amendment) Act commenced in November 2025](https://www.botswanaspeaks.gov.bw/media/COMMITTEE%20OF%20SUPPPLY%20SPEECHES/MLHA%20-%20Committee%20of%20Supply%20Speech%20FINAL%20%20-%2024-02-2026.pdf), and a biometric card is funded within the wider identity programme. <details><summary>Full record</summary>1986-12-31 — [the National Registration Act establishes the register, its contents and the registration requirement](https://botswanalaws.com/consolidated-statutes/principle-legislation/national-registration).<br><br>2026-02-24 — [the amending Act of 2025 commenced in November 2025, and funding for an electronic and biometric identity card was folded into the broader digital identity initiative](https://www.botswanaspeaks.gov.bw/media/COMMITTEE%20OF%20SUPPPLY%20SPEECHES/MLHA%20-%20Committee%20of%20Supply%20Speech%20FINAL%20%20-%2024-02-2026.pdf).<br><br>No registered-population figure, deduplication rate or register-quality measure is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Burundi | The national identification file [has an administrative procedure page and no instituting instrument](https://isoko.gov.bi/?procedures=fichier-national-de-lidentification-carte-nationale-didentite); further texts remain to be adopted. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The official administrative procedure page for the Fichier National d'Identification and the national identity card states that further texts remain to be adopted to institute the file](https://isoko.gov.bi/?procedures=fichier-national-de-lidentification-carte-nationale-didentite).<br><br>A population register that issues a card without an instrument constituting it is the position this row records, and it is the reason every identity question in this report resolves to a document rather than to a database. The page itself carries no date and served an expired security certificate when it was captured, which is a small fact about how much of this estate is maintained.</details> | No change, the instituting texts remain unadopted |
| Central African Republic | No population register is held; what exists is [late registration catching up on people already born](https://oubanguimedias.com/2026/02/01/centrafrique-lancement-de-la-campagne-2026-denregistrement-tardif-des-enfants-sans-acte-de-naissance-dans-la-prefecture-de-bangui/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The base holds no national population register. The nearest thing is the civil-registry estate, whose archive holdings are [being diagnosed by a recruited expert before any digitisation](https://www.civipol.fr/fr/offre-d-emploi-expert/appel-candidature-pour-le-recrutement-dune-experte-archivage-national), and a [late registration campaign reporting 61,000 additional registrations in the capital prefecture](https://oubanguimedias.com/2026/02/01/centrafrique-lancement-de-la-campagne-2026-denregistrement-tardif-des-enfants-sans-acte-de-naissance-dans-la-prefecture-de-bangui/).<br><br>The one register-shaped statement on the record is that [the unique identification number is mandatory](https://www.afdb.org/sites/default/files/documents/projects-and-operations/central_african_republic-economic_and_social_reform_consolidation_support_programme_phase_i_pacres_i-_p-cf-k00-020-pcr-fevrier_2025.pdf), with nothing said about what it is drawn from or how many hold one.</details> | No change, a first stated position with no earlier account behind it |
| Chad | The [integrated population and secure-documents system](https://www.anats.td/) has run since January 2020 and assigns every enrolled resident a unique number. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The agency's site names Ordonnance n. 001/PR/2016 as its founding instrument and states that the system, after enrolment and deduplication, assigns every enrolled individual a unique national identification number across 29 centres ([agency](https://www.anats.td/)); its director-general dates the system's launch to January 2020 ([memorandum](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/05/07/tchad-loim-et-lanats-signent-un-memorandum-dentente-pour-renforcer-lidentification-des-populations/)).<br><br>The register is being extended to populations outside the ordinary enrolment path — the diaspora through embassy postings from September 2025 ([diaspora](https://presidence.td/tchad-rca-le-chef-de-letat-rencontre-la-communaute-tchadienne-de-bangui/24606/)) and Sudanese refugees at Abeche from November ([refugees](https://data.unhcr.org/en/documents/details/119829)).<br><br>No cumulative enrolment total or coverage rate is published by the agency.</details> | Advanced |
| Comoros | The [2023 law provides for a national civil-status and population register](https://extranet.who.int/cpcd/health-legislation/decret-ndeg23-103-pr-portant-promulgation-de-la-loi-ndeg23-016au-du-27-juillet); the [agency is a draft decree](https://lagazettedescomores.com/soci%C3%A9t%C3%A9/vers-un-d%C3%A9cret-d%E2%80%99application-pour-l%E2%80%99agence-nationale-de-l%E2%80%99%C3%A9tat-civil-.html). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [law promulgated on 25 September 2023 provides for computerised civil-status data, a national civil-status and population register and a civil-status agency, with registration centres sited at commune chefs-lieux](https://extranet.who.int/cpcd/health-legislation/decret-ndeg23-103-pr-portant-promulgation-de-la-loi-ndeg23-016au-du-27-juillet).<br><br>Three years on the [agency is a draft decree in island consultation and is not recorded as created](https://lagazettedescomores.com/soci%C3%A9t%C3%A9/vers-un-d%C3%A9cret-d%E2%80%99application-pour-l%E2%80%99agence-nationale-de-l%E2%80%99%C3%A9tat-civil-.html), which leaves the register without a body to hold it.<br><br>2025-09 - a [registration campaign was launched to reach unregistered people in remote areas](https://www.francophonie.org/le-projet-etat-civil-deploye-aux-comores-8175), widening the population such a register would have to carry. No count of records, coverage estimate or completeness figure is on file at either end of the window.</details> | No change, the register exists in law and the agency to keep it has not been created |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The identification office's director general [put holders of the national identification number at roughly 11 million, in a substantially larger population](https://digitalmag.ci/identite-numerique-la-cote-divoire-pays-connecte-mais-pas-encore-pleinement-identifie/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-30 - the [identification office's director general put holders of the national identification number at roughly 11 million](https://digitalmag.ci/identite-numerique-la-cote-divoire-pays-connecte-mais-pas-encore-pleinement-identifie/).<br><br>It is the first such figure the base holds, so no direction can be stated from it. It is also relayed through a commentary piece rather than established from an official publication, and it carries no as-of date. No enrolment series, coverage rate or target for the register has been published by the office, and the population denominator it would be read against is not stated in the same source.<br><br>2026-08-14 - the office [opened a civil-registry and identification agency inside a Yopougon shopping centre with a private mobility company, presented as bringing identification services closer to citizens](https://www.oneci.ci/actualites/1787127124): one site, with no service volume, opening hours or fee difference published.</details> | No change, a first published holder count with nothing earlier behind it |
| Djibouti | The [register of natural persons is the statutory spine of a modernised administration](https://www.lanation.dj/notre-vision-est-de-faire-de-la-direction-generale-de-la-population-et-de-la-famille-une-administration-entierement-modernisee-interconnectee-et-proche-des-citoyens-abdi-abdillahi-obsie/); no enrolment figure is published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register was [created by the 2019 law as the unique reference for identifying individuals, with a national identification number attached](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-n39-an-19-8eme-l-portant-identification-des-personnes-physiques-en-republique-de-djibouti-creation-du-numero-national-didentification-et-etablissement-dun-registre-national/), and the population directorate now [describes it as the spine of a modernised and interconnected administration](https://www.lanation.dj/notre-vision-est-de-faire-de-la-direction-generale-de-la-population-et-de-la-famille-une-administration-entierement-modernisee-interconnectee-et-proche-des-citoyens-abdi-abdillahi-obsie/).<br><br>What moved in the period is what runs on it: a [biometric card issued nationwide with mobile enrolment kits](https://www.lanation.dj/carte-nationale-didentite-biometrique-dans-les-coulisses-de-la-nouvelle-identite-nationale/) and a [mobile identity service launched in February 2026](https://interieur.gouv.dj/forum-sur-lidentite-numerique-et-lancement-du-projet-mobile-id/).<br><br>The register's own size, completeness and update rule are not published, and the [prior-authorisation regime for national-identifier processing has no supervisory body on record](https://www.techhiveadvisory.africa/insights/review-of-djiboutis-data-protection-law).</details> | Advanced |
| DR Congo | [Digitalisation of the Kinshasa population register began in November 2025](https://www.radiookapi.net/2025/11/04/actualite/politique/debut-du-projet-de-digitalisation-de-registre-de-la-population-de). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Digitalisation of the population register began in Kinshasa in November 2025](https://www.radiookapi.net/2025/11/04/actualite/politique/debut-du-projet-de-digitalisation-de-registre-de-la-population-de), under the [general population file instituted by Decret 22-07 of 2 March 2022](https://leganet.cd/Legislation/Droit%20Public/Identification/decret%2022.04.02.03.2022.html). [Communal agents were being trained on digitalised enumeration by August 2026](https://acp.cd/urbain/kinshasa-les-agents-communaux-de-kasa-vubu-outilles-a-la-digitalisation-de-recensement-administratif/).<br><br>No enrolment count, coverage figure or completion date is held for any commune.</details> | Advanced |
| Egypt | Registration completeness is high on the country's own figures, and a [citizens' data update form was piloted in one governorate](https://mcit.gov.eg/en/Media_Center/Press_Room/Press_Releases/82666). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register rests on [the 1994 civil status law and the civil status organisation at the interior ministry](https://mksegypt.org/ar/laws/23813), with [completeness put at 96.6% for live births and 95.3% for deaths](https://unstats.un.org/unsd/demographic-social/products/dyb/documents/metadata/2024/Egypt-2024-VT-Metadata.pdf).<br><br>2025-09-10 - the communications and supply ministries [launched a citizens' data update form in one governorate as the initial phase of a gradual nationwide expansion, described as an organising instrument for keeping citizen records current](https://mcit.gov.eg/en/Media_Center/Press_Room/Press_Releases/82666). No completion, record count or further governorate is recorded.<br><br>The 2027 census is [in preparation under a high committee the prime minister chairs](https://www.cabinet.gov.eg/News/Details/84892).</details> | Advanced |
| Equatorial Guinea | [Birth and death coverage above 90 per cent from administrative records](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/presentacion_oficial_del_segundo_informe_de_hechos_vitales), against a population still projected off the 2015 census. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Registration has improved sharply — from [46 per cent of children unregistered in 2019](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/discurso_de_la_representante_de_unicef_en_el_dia_del_ni%C3%B1o_africano_2021) to [coverage above 90 per cent for 2024](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/presentacion_oficial_del_segundo_informe_de_hechos_vitales).<br><br>The denominator has not: the [2025 compendium still projects population off the 2015 census](https://inege.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/GE-en-cifras-2025-Final.pdf). The [fifth population census has its legal basis in a 2025 decree and resolution and a scientific committee at work](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/inege_presenta_los_avances_estrategicos_para_el_v_censo_de_poblacion_y_el_i_censo_agropecuario).</details> | Advanced |
| Eritrea | Population registration runs on six separate regional registers with no central national register ([technical description](https://www.metas.ch/dam/sem/en/data/internationales/herkunftslaender/afrika/eri/eri-identitaets-und-zivilstandsdokumente-e.pdf.download.pdf/ERI-identitaets-und-zivilstandsdokumente-e.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>Eritrea's population registration has been digital at regional level since 2001: six separate registers, one per zoba, each recording an identity number, family and resident numbers, nationality and civil data, from which residence cards and family identity cards are printed as extracts. Birth registration feeds them through the local family register. There is no central national register ([technical description](https://www.metas.ch/dam/sem/en/data/internationales/herkunftslaender/afrika/eri/eri-identitaets-und-zivilstandsdokumente-e.pdf.download.pdf/ERI-identitaets-und-zivilstandsdokumente-e.pdf)).<br><br>A regional treaty body, reviewing Eritrea's fourth periodic report in April 2026, recognised the development of a five-year strategic civil registration and vital statistics plan for 2024-2028 ([concluding observations](https://www.acerwc.africa/sites/default/files/2026-07/503.26_Eritrea%20Concluding%20Observations%2022.07.2026.pdf)).<br><br>A country-of-origin report carries a dedicated section on the register and the cards drawn from it ([report](https://www.ecoi.net/en/file/local/2107005/General%20country%20of%20origin%20information%20report%20on%20Eritrea%20December%202023.pdf)).<br><br>The base holds no coverage rate and no statement that the six registers reconcile with one another.</details> | Advanced |
| Eswatini | A [compulsory civil register](https://eswatinilii.org/akn/sz/act/1983/5/eng@1998-12-01) has run since January 1984 under a 1983 statute, and nothing in the base shows it has been digitised. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register commenced on 6 January 1984 under a births, marriages and deaths registration Act, administered by a registrar in the home affairs ministry ([statute](https://eswatinilii.org/akn/sz/act/1983/5/eng@1998-12-01), [assessment](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202508/robust-dpi-inevitable-for-eswatinis-economic-jobs-boom-world-bank)).<br><br>It is a paper-based statute and nothing shows digitisation. The electronic identity planned for 2027 would therefore be built on a register whose current form is unrecorded, in the ministry whose card production stopped for five months and which is under investigation for a data breach.</details> | No change |
| Gabon | Two registers have been [opened as workshops and never built](https://www.gabonreview.com/identification-legale-et-digitale-le-gabon-enclenche-la-mise-en-oeuvre-de-trois-projets-strategiques/): a biometric register of natural persons and a national digital identity system. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The biometric register was seeded on the legacy civil registry and its implementation workshop opened in June 2025 with no build reported ([stocktake](https://www.gabonreview.com/identification-legale-et-digitale-le-gabon-enclenche-la-mise-en-oeuvre-de-trois-projets-strategiques/)).<br><br>The digital identity system's workshops opened at Ntoum under the donor-financed digital project, and the last mention is a governance-day framing of 16 September 2025 ([governance day](https://www.gabonreview.com/identite-digitale-le-gabon-franchit-un-cap-vers-une-gouvernance-inclusive-et-securisee/)).<br><br>Fourteen months of the window have passed with no build, pilot or delivery date published for either.</details> | No change |
| Gambia | [More than 1.4m cumulative civil registrations](https://apai-crvs.uneca.org/sites/default/files/resourceuploads/day-2-interoperability-gambia.pdf) by June 2026, a total rather than a coverage rate. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registrar general reported [more than 1.4m registrations under the electronic civil registration system by June 2026](https://apai-crvs.uneca.org/sites/default/files/resourceuploads/day-2-interoperability-gambia.pdf). It is a cumulative count of records, not a share of the population registered, and no coverage rate is published.<br><br>The legal frame is moving: a [Civil Registration Bill 2026 cleared first reading on 5 March 2026](https://www.voicegambia.com/2026/03/06/civil-registration-bill-2026-receives-first-reading-in-national-assembly/). The identity credential that would sit on the register [was first issued in July 2026](https://www.citinewsroom.com/2026/07/margins-delivers-first-gam-id-president-barrow-is-inaugural-recipient/).</details> | Advanced |
| Ghana | The mass-registration phase for those aged 15 and over [closed at about 20 million captured](https://gna.org.gh/2026/05/nia-begins-registration-of-children-aged-six-to-14-in-volta-oti-regions/), and registration of children aged 6 to 14 began in May 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-05-05 - registration of children aged 6 to 14 [began in two regions against a nationwide target of about 3.1 million children, with children's health-insurance records integrated into their card data and guardian records linked as an anti-fraud and tracing mechanism; the mass-registration phase for those aged 15 and over closed at about 20 million captured against a target of about 19 million](https://gna.org.gh/2026/05/nia-begins-registration-of-children-aged-six-to-14-in-volta-oti-regions/).<br><br>The authority publishes [live registration statistics, stated as updated at 19 May 2026](https://nia.gov.gh/nia-registration-statistics/), but the page is a counter rather than a dated report, so the base carries the fact of publication and not a figure from it.<br><br>What no source states is the register's coverage against population - the denominator - or how many of those captured hold an issued card.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea-Bissau | The [fourth census launched field enumeration](https://guinea-bissau.unfpa.org/en/news/historic-milestone-guinea-bissau-launches-4th-national-census-map-data-driven-future) in June 2026, the first fully digital count since 2009. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The institute [opened recruitment for about 5,000 census staff](https://ang.gw/recenseamento-geral-da-populacao-ine-gb-lanca-sua-plataforma-oficial-de-recrutamento/) in September 2025 and [launched field enumeration in June 2026](https://guinea-bissau.unfpa.org/en/news/historic-milestone-guinea-bissau-launches-4th-national-census-map-data-driven-future), the country's first fully digital census since 2009.<br><br>The continuous register beside it is the civil register, whose [first fully digital office opened at Santa Luzia](https://www.undp.org/sites/g/files/zskgke326/files/2026-04/_undp_g.b_newsletter_jan-mar_2026_eng_1.pdf). No population register linking the two is described.</details> | Advanced |
| Kenya | An [integrated population registration service](https://usajili.go.ke/integrated-population-registration-service) runs under the immigration and citizen services department, with nothing published about what it holds. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The population registration service is run by the State Department for Immigration and Citizen Services ([service](https://usajili.go.ke/integrated-population-registration-service)).<br><br>The held capture is the department's own service page, whose substantive content did not render to the fetch. No record count, participating-agency list, query volume or access rule is established.<br><br>That matters more here than elsewhere, because the base separately holds an electoral commission engagement with the registration bureau on identity verification and a planned civil-register link to police facial recognition, both of which would query this register.</details> | No change |
| Lesotho | The [National Identity Register is established by the 2011 Act](https://lesotholii.org/akn/ls/act/2011/9/eng@2011-03-21); the movement is a modernisation framework, not enrolment. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2011-03-21 — [section 3 of the Act establishes the register itself](https://lesotholii.org/akn/ls/act/2011/9/eng@2011-03-21).<br><br>2026-02-26 — [the 2026 work plan ties open-source platform development to the register](https://www.gov.ls/government/micsti-and-unicef-signed-digital-id-work-plan/).<br><br>2026-06-29 — [a consultation opened on a systems architecture design and modernisation framework for the registry](https://www.gov.ls/development/ministry-of-home-affairs-advances-digital-transformation-of-national-identity-system/).<br><br>No registered-population figure later than 2022, no deduplication rate and no register-quality measure is published.</details> | Advanced, on the platform rather than the register |
| Liberia | The [2011 Act](https://citizenshiprightsafrica.org/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/Liberia-NIR-Act-2011.pdf) makes the biometric system the population register in law; enrolment remains partial. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [Act](https://citizenshiprightsafrica.org/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/Liberia-NIR-Act-2011.pdf) creates the autonomous registry and gives it the biometric system, the card and the Social Security Number as the primary government identity number, which is the statutory basis for a population register.<br><br>Coverage has not kept pace. The registry put enrolment at [759,223 individuals, 14.6 per cent of an estimated 5.2 million](https://gnnliberia.com/nir-outlines-milestones-and-challenges-under-executive-order-147/), in June 2025 across 30 permanent centres, and the [presidential proclamation of September 2025](https://www.mofa.gov.lr/media/press-releases/president-boakai-declares-tuesday-september-16-national-identification-day) cited 720,000. The registry is not a complete population register and no statutory alternative exists.</details> | No change |
| Libya | The interior and labour ministries agreed a [unified national database of expatriate workers](https://www.lananews.com/ar/?p=499082) in August 2026, with a dedicated platform and no authorising instrument. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The two ministries agreed the database on 3 August 2026 and named a dedicated platform for it ([agreement](https://www.lananews.com/ar/?p=499082)).<br><br>No build or timetable is published, and the base holds no register of the citizen population against which to set it, so the only population-register movement on file covers non-citizens.</details> | Advanced |
| Madagascar | [Biometric enrolment opened in January 2026](https://prea.gov.mg/presidence/lancement-officiel-de-lenrolement-biometrique-izaho-tokana/) and a special registration law for unregistered adults cleared review in May. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The civil status law of 2018 is the founding instrument, mandating a unique identification number at birth ([law](https://extranet.who.int/cpcd/sites/default/files/public_file_repository/MDG_Madagascar_Loi-No-2018-027-relative-a-l_etat-civil_2018.pdf)).<br><br>Ceremonial launch of biometric enrolment for citizens aged 18 and over opened a district pilot in January 2026 ([launch](https://prea.gov.mg/presidence/lancement-officiel-de-lenrolement-biometrique-izaho-tokana/)).<br><br>A derogatory birth-registration law for adults without birth certificates, verified by local officials, was cleared for promulgation in May 2026 ([decision](https://www.hcc.gov.mg/?p=9952)). The register's completeness is not stated anywhere in the base, and the two routes into it — enrolment and derogatory registration — have no published reconciliation.</details> | Advanced |
| Malawi | [Marriage and divorce registration transferred to the registration bureau on 12 February 2026](https://www.nrb.gov.mw/wp-content/uploads/file-listing/NRB_Public_Statement_Transition_of_Marriage_Registration_12.02.2026.pdf), a third vital event under one roof. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register runs on the national registration system, [hosted in the national data centre since January 2026, with the bureau's own plan putting 99 per cent of adults biometrically registered](https://www.undp.org/sites/g/files/zskgke326/files/2026-01/malawi_nrb_strategic_plan_2025_web.pdf).<br><br>2026-02 - the [first phase of the national birth registration campaign synchronised 970,400 of 1.1 million targeted child records into the central database across five districts](https://www.nrb.gov.mw/news/nrb-completes-phase-one-of-national-birth-registration-campaign/).<br><br>2026-02 - [marriage and divorce registration transferred from the Registrar General to the bureau with effect from 12 February 2026, its director becoming Registrar of Marriages](https://www.nrb.gov.mw/wp-content/uploads/file-listing/NRB_Public_Statement_Transition_of_Marriage_Registration_12.02.2026.pdf). Births, marriages, divorces and identity now sit with one body; death registration coverage is still not held.</details> | Advanced |
| Mali | A [national digital directory of diaspora skills](https://www.maliweb.net/politique/diplomatie/washington-dc-lancement-du-repertoire-numerique-des-competences-de-la-diaspora-malienne-3119417.html) was launched in Washington in August 2026, with no data-protection basis stated. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The directory registers professional profiles across economic, administrative, academic, private-sector and emerging fields so that ministries can identify expertise against specific needs; it is run by the embassy to the United States and was launched on 22 August 2026 ([launch](https://www.maliweb.net/politique/diplomatie/washington-dc-lancement-du-repertoire-numerique-des-competences-de-la-diaspora-malienne-3119417.html)).<br><br>The minister described it as an accreting database whose usefulness depends on take-up and on profiles being kept current. Questions at the ceremony went to registration terms and to how the recorded data would be used, and no data-protection basis is stated.</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritania | A [unified digital consular registry](https://fr.saharamedias.net/le-ministere-mauritanien-des-affaires-etrangeres-lance-deux-plateformes-numeriques-pour-la-prestation-de-services-consulaires-en-arabie-saoudite-a-bahrein-et-aux-maldives/) went live for three countries in 2026, registering residence, distribution and permitted activities of citizens abroad. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry is live for the Riyadh mission's zone of competence, covering Saudi Arabia, Bahrain and the Maldives, and builds a digital register of national skills abroad ([registry](https://fr.saharamedias.net/le-ministere-mauritanien-des-affaires-etrangeres-lance-deux-plateformes-numeriques-pour-la-prestation-de-services-consulaires-en-arabie-saoudite-a-bahrein-et-aux-maldives/), [services](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/public-management/mauritania-launches-digital-consular-services-platform-for-citizens-abroad)).<br><br>The consular registration desk remains the competent authority, so the platform layers onto the registry rather than replacing it. No enrolment count for the diaspora it covers is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritius | The civil status register's [system is being replaced](https://csd.govmu.org/Documents/Communique/Communique%20-%20Revamping%20of%20CSD%20System%20New%20.pdf), announced September 2025, with the 2026 Finance Act amending around it. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register operates under the Civil Status Act of 1981 ([Act](https://lawsofmauritius.govmu.org/portal/viewlegislationdocument/web/?docnumber=&doctitle=Q2l2aWwgU3RhdHVzIEFjdA%3D%3D&doctype=act)).<br><br>The civil status division announced a revamping of its system in September 2025 ([communique](https://csd.govmu.org/Documents/Communique/Communique%20-%20Revamping%20of%20CSD%20System%20New%20.pdf)).<br><br>The Finance Act 2026 carries the statutory changes behind the budget's registry measures ([Act](https://mauritiusassembly.govmu.org/mauritiusassembly/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Act1426.pdf)). No completeness figure, record count or migration timetable for the replacement is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Morocco | [Law 72-18](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/mor204689.pdf) creates the national population register and the agency to run it; the [agency](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2024/12/04/world-bank-greenlights-70m-boost-for-morocco-s-innovative-social-protection-delivery-systems) has no establishment date, budget or operating record. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statute creates the population register, the unified social register and a national register agency, with article 45 deferring force to implementing texts ([statute](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/mor204689.pdf)).<br><br>The agency was named in December 2024 as a component of the identity and targeting project to be operationalised, and nothing since records it established ([agency](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2024/12/04/world-bank-greenlights-70m-boost-for-morocco-s-innovative-social-protection-delivery-systems)). So the register operates and the body the law puts in charge of it does not yet exist.</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | [The 2018 revision of the civil registration code is the founding act of the civil registration and vital statistics system](https://www.studocu.com/row/document/universidade-sao-tomas-de-mocambique/direito/lei-122018-de-4-de-dezembro-revisao-do-codigo-de-registo-civil/158371975); the base holds no separate continuously updated population register. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statutory foundation is [the 2018 revision of the civil registration code, which establishes the electronic civil registration and vital statistics system and governs the digitisation and notification duties of registry offices](https://www.studocu.com/row/document/universidade-sao-tomas-de-mocambique/direito/lei-122018-de-4-de-dezembro-revisao-do-codigo-de-registo-civil/158371975).<br><br>What the base does not hold is a population register distinct from civil registration: no separate continuously updated register, no coverage figure and no statement of how the civil register would serve that function. The distinction matters because a civil register records events and a population register records a current resident population, and the record here answers only the first.</details> | No change, and the base holds no register distinct from civil registration |
| Namibia | The [national population registration system](https://www.undp.org/namibia/blog/season-3-namibias-digital-identity-journey-vision-reality) has been operating since at least 2020 and is the register the September 2026 card is built on. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A 2021 baseline found obsolete software, siloed databases and little interoperability; the system now operates with remote biometric enrolment tested through hand-held devices ([system](https://www.undp.org/namibia/blog/season-3-namibias-digital-identity-journey-vision-reality), [case study](https://sdg.iisd.org/news/namibia-case-study-highlights-importance-of-data-policy-alignment-for-sdg-16-9)).<br><br>No enrolment count, coverage share or performance figure is published for it, so the register underneath the country's identity programme is described and not measured.</details> | No change |
| Niger | A [National Population Register](https://www.lesahel.org/tillaberi-reformes-de-letat-civil-et-de-lidentification-une-mission-de-sensibilisation-sillonne-la-region/) is in build assigning every citizen a unique identifier, with a sensitisation tour running ahead of a register that does not yet accept enrolments. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register is designed as a central secure database with a first enrolment phase opening at the capital and other regions following in 2027; officials on the tour cite the April 2026 ordonnance as the legal basis and describe the register as forthcoming ([register](https://www.lesahel.org/tillaberi-reformes-de-letat-civil-et-de-lidentification-une-mission-de-sensibilisation-sillonne-la-region/), [tour](https://www.lesahel.org/dosso-lancement-de-la-tournee-nationale-de-sensibilisation-sur-letat-civil/), [texts](https://anp.ne/lancement-a-tahoua-dune-tournee-nationale-de-vulgarisation-des-textes-juridiques-et-des-operations-denregistrement-intermediaire-au-registre-national-de-la-population/), [restructuring](https://www.lesahel.org/wuri-niger-restructure-une-opportunite-pour-une-identification-universelle-de-la-population-au-service-de-linclusion-et-du-un-puissant-facteur-de-developpement-inclusif-et-une-dimension/)).<br><br>No enrolment target, budget, supplier or start date is published, and a sensitisation campaign preceding a register that does not yet accept enrolments is the whole of what is dated here.<br><br>A separate diaspora and expert data bank was undertaken by the foreign minister in July 2026, with no enabling instrument, custodian, data-protection basis or timetable named ([diaspora](https://www.lesahel.org/cloture-de-la-2e-edition-du-forum-de-la-diaspora-a-niamey-des-recommandations-et-des-engagements-formules-pour-le-developpement-economique-et-social-du-pays/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Nigeria | A [diaspora health-workforce registry](https://fmino.gov.ng/fg-commends-nigerian-health-professionals-in-diaspora-for-strengthening-healthcare-delivery/) was launched standing on no stated legal basis. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It is described as newly launched, with no launch date, custodian agency, enrolment terms or data-protection basis stated in anything held: a register standing up ahead of its governance ([registry](https://fmino.gov.ng/fg-commends-nigerian-health-professionals-in-diaspora-for-strengthening-healthcare-delivery/)).<br><br>It is the pattern this unit repeats across the mortgage registry, the cooperative credential and the addressing framework, each announced before the instrument that would govern it.</details> | Advanced |
| Rwanda | All nine modules of the digital civil registration system were [reported complete](https://www.minaloc.gov.rw/news-detail/rwanda-completes-digitalisation-of-crvs-modules) in January 2026, ahead of schedule, and biometric enrolment reached 1.8 million submissions. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The local government ministry announced in January 2026 that all nine modules — the last two being guardianship and legitimisation — are digitalised, ahead of the government's own 2024 schedule ([announcement](https://www.minaloc.gov.rw/news-detail/rwanda-completes-digitalisation-of-crvs-modules)).<br><br>The biometric identity rollout reached over 1.8 million submissions against a 15 million target by 22 February 2026, reported directly to the Senate ([report](https://www.parliament.gov.rw/news-detail?cHash=349550b59d9f16c1cdbfe62d270d5c3c&tx_news_pi1%5Baction%5D=detail&tx_news_pi1%5Bcontroller%5D=News&tx_news_pi1%5Bnews%5D=58639)).<br><br>The integrated system launched in June moved registration authority from sector to cell level and connected it to the national population registry ([launch](https://www.minaloc.gov.rw/news-detail/rwanda-launches-new-integrated-civil-registration-system)).</details> | Advanced |
| Sao Tome and Principe | A [central civil and criminal identification service](https://dgrn.gov.st/CICC.php) keeps the national records, and a [free identity-card campaign closed in March 2026](https://www.instituto-camoes.pt/sobre/comunicacao/noticias/sao-tome-e-principe) feeding automatic voter registration. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registries directorate describes the centre as the central service charged with centralising, organising and keeping up to date at national level the records on civil and criminal identification, seated in the capital with jurisdiction over the whole territory ([service](https://dgrn.gov.st/CICC.php)).<br><br>A national campaign giving a free identity card to every citizen over 17 without a valid one ran until 15 March 2026, implemented by the directorate with technical and financial support from the electoral reform project, and the card is to become the base document for automatic and permanent voter registration ([campaign](https://www.instituto-camoes.pt/sobre/comunicacao/noticias/sao-tome-e-principe)).<br><br>No population coverage figure, issuance total or de-duplication statement is published for the register itself. The page describing the service carries no date and the year is inferred.</details> | Advanced |
| Senegal | A [biometric audit of the public-service headcount](https://lesoleil.sn/actualites/societe-fait-divers/audit-agents-fonction-publique-le-ministre-diante-visite-les-centres-denrolement-de-rufisque-et-thies/) began enrolment in June 2026 and a [biometric register of artisanal gold miners](https://www.wearetech.africa/fr/fils/actualites/gestion-publique/le-senegal-numerise-lorpaillage-pour-juguler-une-perte-annuelle-de-plus-de-614-millions) was agreed by convention, neither stating a data-protection provision. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The headcount audit runs physical and biometric enrolment from 23 June 2026. It is identity infrastructure whose beneficiary is the treasury, and no correction or grievance route is stated for an agent the audit fails to find ([audit](https://lesoleil.sn/actualites/societe-fait-divers/audit-agents-fonction-publique-le-ministre-diante-visite-les-centres-denrolement-de-rufisque-et-thies/)).<br><br>The miners' register was agreed by a convention signed on 30 July 2026 between the mines ministry and the deposits fund; no enrolment target, timetable, cost, host, retention period or regulator role is stated, and the reported design carries no data-protection provision ([register](https://www.wearetech.africa/fr/fils/actualites/gestion-publique/le-senegal-numerise-lorpaillage-pour-juguler-une-perte-annuelle-de-plus-de-614-millions)).<br><br>Two new biometric collections in two months, in a country whose identity directorate was breached in February and whose data-protection law is still under revision.</details> | Advanced |
| Seychelles | The [National Population Database](https://www.ics.gov.sc/about/department/civil-status-division) sits with the Civil Status Division and sustains the statistics bureau's population releases. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Division holds custody of the database and issues identity cards to all residents under the Civil Status Act and the Identity Card Act ([division](https://www.ics.gov.sc/about/department/civil-status-division)).<br><br>The statistics bureau's 2025 releases draw on those civil-status and immigration administrative records ([releases](https://www.nbs.gov.sc/downloads/36-data-acquisition-census/16-population-and-vital-statistics/176-2025)), giving 123,097 residents at 31 December 2025 and life expectancy of 77.0 years ([year-end](https://www.nbs.gov.sc/downloads/1924-end-of-year-population-2025/download)).<br><br>The land-system tender of 2024 named the database as one of the systems a new registry would interface with ([tender](https://www.pou.gov.sc/tenders/consultancies/1275-design-and-development-of-the-land-management-administration-information-system-lmais)), which is the only account held of it being read by anything else.</details> | No change |
| Sierra Leone | The [national biometric register](https://sierraloaded.sl/news/ncra-urges-identity-approach-sierra-leones/) covers about 93 per cent of the population, under the [2016 Act](https://www.parliament.gov.sl/uploads/acts/The%20Nationa%20Civil%20Registration%20Act,%202016.pdf) that created the authority. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Coverage stands at about 93 per cent with a unique identification number each, on a Tier III data centre with a live eKYC platform ([register](https://sierraloaded.sl/news/ncra-urges-identity-approach-sierra-leones/)).<br><br>The register's statutory basis is the National Civil Registration Act 2016, which vests it in an independent authority with a confidentiality duty ([Act](https://www.parliament.gov.sl/uploads/acts/The%20Nationa%20Civil%20Registration%20Act,%202016.pdf)).<br><br>Ninety-three per cent is a coverage figure and not a currency figure: no deaths-recorded rate, no update cycle and no de-duplication audit is published against it.</details> | No change |
| Somalia | [Law 46 of 2026](https://www.osagsomalia.com/media/documents/bulletins-contents/legislation/2026/sharciga_Dad-dhigista_iyo_Tirakoobka_Xaaladaha_Madaniga.pdf) gave civil registration a statute in April, and the [interior ministry was briefed on expansion](https://sonna.so/en/article/interior-minister-receives-update-on-progress-of-civil-registration-and-vital-statistics-system) in June. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Population Registration and Civil Status Statistics Law was published in the Official Bulletin on 5 April 2026, establishing the civil-registration and vital-statistics layer beside the national identity infrastructure ([law](https://www.osagsomalia.com/media/documents/bulletins-contents/legislation/2026/sharciga_Dad-dhigista_iyo_Tirakoobka_Xaaladaha_Madaniga.pdf)).<br><br>The registration directorate briefed the minister in June on expansion of services and implementation of the law ([briefing](https://sonna.so/en/article/interior-minister-receives-update-on-progress-of-civil-registration-and-vital-statistics-system)), following a coordination meeting in February ([meeting](https://sonna.so/en/article/ministry-of-interior-hosts-crvs-coordination-meeting-in-mogadishu)).<br><br>The policy behind this dates to January 2021, and the assessment that accompanied it found no operational national civil-registration office and 4 per cent of under-twos registered ([profile](https://somalia.unfpa.org/sites/default/files/pub-pdf/crvs_report.pdf)). No later coverage figure exists.</details> | Advanced |
| South Africa | Cabinet [approved the white paper anchoring the intelligent population register](https://www.sanews.gov.za/south-africa/schreiber-welcomes-approval-revised-white-paper-citizenship), and [2,202,368 blocked identity numbers were unblocked](https://www.gov.za/news/media-statements/home-affairs-launches-nationwide-campaign-resolve-blocked-id-numbers-01-jun) on a different count from the court-supervised one. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-04-08 — [Cabinet approved the white paper anchoring the conversion of the population register into an intelligent population register, on biometrics, interoperability and real-time integration](https://www.sanews.gov.za/south-africa/schreiber-welcomes-approval-revised-white-paper-citizenship), and [the core biometric technology is live for the citizenship portal while the digital identity front end is still being built](https://www.gov.za/news/speeches/minister-leon-schreiber-2026-state-nation-debate-17-feb-2026). The successor had been [announced in June with the green identity book to be de-recognised](https://www.sanews.gov.za/south-africa/sa-unveils-biometric-population-register-tighten-migration-controls) and [a biometric case-management system and 600 handheld readers tendered](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202607/south-africa-tenders-biometric-case-management-system-for-migration-enforcement).<br><br>The exclusions are reported on two counts that do not reconcile. [A national campaign unblocked 2,202,368 identity numbers blocked before November 2022](https://www.gov.za/news/media-statements/home-affairs-launches-nationwide-campaign-resolve-blocked-id-numbers-01-jun), while [the court-supervised cohort fell only from 384,189 to 377,060 in roughly seven months, with unserviceable addresses given as the obstacle and a deadline running to March 2027](https://iol.co.za/news/politics/2026-07-17-home-affairs-struggles-to-unblock-identity-numbers-due-to-unserviceable-addresses/).<br><br>Both are the department's own figures and no source states how the two populations relate.</details> | Mixed, a biometric successor anchored in Cabinet policy while the register's own exclusions clear on two incompatible counts |
| South Sudan | The [Register of South Sudanese Nationals](https://mojca.gov.ss/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/Nationality-Act-2011.pdf) is statutory, and [more than 4,000 cards](https://radioyei.org/news/local/yei-residents-begins-collecting-nationality-cards/) were distributed across Greater Yei in July 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Nationality Act 2011 establishes the directorate and requires it to maintain the register, with the Nationality Certificate as the credential ([Act](https://mojca.gov.ss/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/Nationality-Act-2011.pdf)).<br><br>Distribution of more than 4,000 processed national identity cards and passports began in Yei River County covering the four counties of Greater Yei, with European Union support ([distribution](https://radioyei.org/news/local/yei-residents-begins-collecting-nationality-cards/)).<br><br>No national coverage figure exists. A county-level distribution count is the most precise measure of the register the base holds.</details> | Advanced |
| Sudan | The [Civil Registry Act 2011](https://citizenshiprightsafrica.org/wp-content/uploads/2011/01/Sudan-Civil-Registration-Act-2011-EN.pdf) founds the register and its national number; coverage was [88.8 per cent in 2021](https://landinfo.no/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/Report-Sudan-Civil-Registration-ID-documents-and-passports-03032023.pdf) and has not been measured since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act repealed the 2001 statute and establishes the national civil registry and its unique national number, requiring the registry to supply data to competent state organs and coordinate with them ([Act](https://citizenshiprightsafrica.org/wp-content/uploads/2011/01/Sudan-Civil-Registration-Act-2011-EN.pdf)).<br><br>Sudanese authorities reported 35.5 million of 40 million inhabitants registered by 2021, up from 82 per cent in 2016 ([report](https://landinfo.no/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/Report-Sudan-Civil-Registration-ID-documents-and-passports-03032023.pdf)).<br><br>Issuance resumed in Port Sudan and Kassala over the window, and registry data was reported recovered to 13 April 2023 ([resumption](https://www.alnilin.com/13423167.htm)). No post-war coverage figure exists.</details> | Advanced |
| Tanzania | [Child birth registration rose from 55 per cent in 2020 to 68 per cent in 2025](http://www.rita.go.tz/news.php?n=483), against [a 13.7 million record backlog](https://www.nao.go.tz/uploads/Performance_Audit_Report_on_the_Identification__Registration_and_Issuance_of_Birth_and_Death_Certificates.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The responsible minister stated on civil registration day that child birth-registration coverage rose from 55 per cent in 2020 to 68 per cent in 2025, with a target of 100 per cent by 2030](http://www.rita.go.tz/news.php?n=483).<br><br>[An independent audit found only 29 per cent of the whole population birth-registered by December 2024, 43 per cent of the 4,742 registration centres reviewed not operating, and a backlog of about 13.7 million records](https://www.nao.go.tz/uploads/Performance_Audit_Report_on_the_Identification__Registration_and_Issuance_of_Birth_and_Death_Certificates.pdf).<br><br>[The registration agency reports 11.5 million certificates issued and 20 million people registered by November 2025](https://www.rita.go.tz/news.php?n=464).<br><br>Child coverage and whole-population coverage are different denominators, so the two headline figures measure different things rather than contradicting each other; the backlog is what stands between them.</details> | Advanced |
| Togo | Civil status went online in a [five-commune pilot](https://territoire.gouv.tg/digitalisation-de-letat-civil-le-togo-lance-officiellement-la-demande-en-ligne-des-copies-dactes/) in December 2025 and the communes were equipped in April 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The governing statute has permitted computerised registration since 2009: article 15 of [loi n 2009-010](http://jafbase.fr/docAfrique/Togo/Togo-%20Loi%202009%20sur%20l%27%C3%A9tat%20civil.pdf) gives computerised acts the same legal value as manually registered ones. It permits electronic registration; it does not establish that any office operated one.<br><br>The online service for requesting copies of civil-status acts launched on [19 December 2025](https://territoire.gouv.tg/digitalisation-de-letat-civil-le-togo-lance-officiellement-la-demande-en-ligne-des-copies-dactes/) in five communes only — Golfe 1, Agoe-Nyive 1, Ave 2, Lacs 1 and Zio 2 — to test operational effectiveness before rollout.<br><br>On [8 April 2026](https://territoire.gouv.tg/digitalisation-de-letat-civil-des-equipements-informatiques-remis-aux-communes-pilotes-pour-accelerer-le-processus/) each received two computers, a printer and an uninterruptible power supply, after the communes reported they had none. A review of the 2009 statute opened in August 2026.</details> | Advanced |
| Tunisia | [The citizen identifier register was created in 2020](https://legislation-securite.tn/latest-laws/decret-gouvernemental-n-2020-312-du-15-mai-2020-fixant-le-contenu-et-les-specifications-techniques-de-lidentifiant-unique-du-citoyen-et-les-regles-regissant-la-tenue-et-la-gestion-de-son-regi/); no figure for what it holds has been published since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[An implementing decree of May 2020 created and specified the register of the citizen unique identifier, together with its governance unit and oversight council](https://legislation-securite.tn/latest-laws/decret-gouvernemental-n-2020-312-du-15-mai-2020-fixant-le-contenu-et-les-specifications-techniques-de-lidentifiant-unique-du-citoyen-et-les-regles-regissant-la-tenue-et-la-gestion-de-son-regi/).<br><br>[The statistics institute published a new projection series to 2054 in May 2026, built on the 2024 census population of 11.97 million and on civil-registration data using a UN-standard Bayesian methodology](https://www.ins.tn/publication/flash-projection-de-la-population-2025-2054).<br><br>The register itself publishes nothing: no enrolment count, no coverage rate, and no statement of how it stands in relation to the civil register or to the unified population registry still being worked toward.</details> | No change |
| Uganda | [First alien identity cards were issued](https://nilepost.co.ug/news/365341/nira-starts-issuing-ids-to-foreign-nationals) with 1,875 enrolled in ten weeks, against a scope of which about 1.95m are refugees or asylum-seekers, and a US$100 fee. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Enrolment of 1,875 in ten weeks is under a tenth of a per cent of the scope, and the fee falls on a population overwhelmingly composed of refugees ([cards](https://nilepost.co.ug/news/365341/nira-starts-issuing-ids-to-foreign-nationals), [registration](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/news/nira-kicks-off-first-registration-of-foreigne-NV_234932_062026), [births](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/news/nira-intensifies-birth-registration-drive-lau-NV_234727_062026)).<br><br>Nothing is published about a waiver, an instalment route, or what happens to a resident foreigner who cannot pay yet needs a subscriber card or a bank account.<br><br>That combination — a fee at that level, on that population, with no waiver published — is the sharpest exclusion finding in this unit.</details> | Advanced |
| Zambia | A [sample based registration system was launched to capture deaths occurring outside health facilities](https://www.zambiamonitor.com/zambia-launches-sample-based-registration-system-to-capture-silent-deaths-outside-health-facilities/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-05 — [the health ministry launched a sample based registration system with vital statistics, extending continuous civil-registration coverage to deaths outside health facilities](https://www.zambiamonitor.com/zambia-launches-sample-based-registration-system-to-capture-silent-deaths-outside-health-facilities/) — what the reporting describes as the silent deaths the register was missing.<br><br>Deaths outside facilities are the harder half of civil registration everywhere, and a sample-based system is an acknowledgement that universal capture is not close. No coverage estimate, sample size or first result is published, and the base holds no statement of the population register's own completeness to read it against.</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | The [population register](https://apai-crvs.uneca.org/sites/default/files/resourceuploads/day-2-interoperability-zimbabwe.pdf) is being modernised and now takes automated validation from civil registration, with the voters' roll moving under the same authority. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register of residents sits under the National Registration Act as consolidated to 31 December 2016 ([Act](https://zimlii.org/akn/zw/act/1976/36)), and its modernisation has been under way since about 2023 with no completion date and no coverage figure held.<br><br>The registrar-general's ICT deputy set out automated validation and synchronisation with the national population register as part of the civil registration and health integration under way, with a three-province pilot due in the first half of 2027 ([presentation](https://apai-crvs.uneca.org/sites/default/files/resourceuploads/day-2-interoperability-zimbabwe.pdf)).<br><br>From 18 August 2026 voter registration and the voters' roll are being transferred nationwide to the registrar-general, folding electoral identity into the same institution that holds the population register ([handover](https://www.zimbabwesituation.com/news/rg-zec-begin-nationwide-handover-of-voter-registration-mandate/)).<br><br>No registered-population count, update cycle or accuracy measure is published.</details> | Advanced |

## Civil register

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | The [register's digitalisation was presented to the regional directors' meeting](https://apai-crvs.uneca.org/sites/default/files/resourceuploads/day-2-decentralisation-algeria.pdf) in July 2026, and an [appointment platform was reported](https://www.aps.dz/fr/algerie/actualite-nationale/mic326u8-lancement-lundi-de-la-plateforme-de-prise-de-rendez-vous-pour-les-usagers-du-service-de-l-etat-civil). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register was [created by article 25 bis of the 2014 law amending the 1970 ordinance](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/JO-FRANCAIS/2014/F2014049.pdf).<br><br>2025-11-23 - [an appointment platform for civil-status services was reported](https://www.aps.dz/fr/algerie/actualite-nationale/mic326u8-lancement-lundi-de-la-plateforme-de-prise-de-rendez-vous-pour-les-usagers-du-service-de-l-etat-civil).<br><br>2026-07 - [the register's digitalisation was presented at the annual regional meeting of civil-registry directors general](https://apai-crvs.uneca.org/sites/default/files/resourceuploads/day-2-decentralisation-algeria.pdf), alongside the mandatory acceptance of electronic civil-status documents this ledger holds separately. No coverage rate, document volume or completeness figure accompanies any of it.</details> | Advanced |
| Angola | Birth registration [reached 63.0% overall but only 39.2% among children aged nought to four](https://c2a.portais.gov.ao/uploads/Relatorio_Sintese_CENSO_2024_INE_Nov_2025_c5fba11d70.pdf), with [registration posts rehabilitated in two provinces](https://angop.ao/noticias/sociedade/njila-reabilita-postos-de-registos-de-nascimento-no-bie-e-huambo/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-11-20 — census definitive results [put birth registration at 63.0% overall and 39.2% among children aged nought to four, from 53.5% and 24.8% at the 2014 census](https://c2a.portais.gov.ao/uploads/Relatorio_Sintese_CENSO_2024_INE_Nov_2025_c5fba11d70.pdf). Both rose, and the gap between them is the finding: the pipeline that feeds the identity card is under-registering the newest cohort fastest.<br><br>2026-04-06 — under a US$250m local-governance project, [eleven birth-registration posts were rehabilitated in one province with five operating since August 2025, thirteen more were nearing completion in another, and mass registration campaigns reached several thousand people](https://angop.ao/noticias/sociedade/njila-reabilita-postos-de-registos-de-nascimento-no-bie-e-huambo/), on [a project running across eight provinces and 53 municipalities](https://angop.ao/noticias/sociedade/njila-reabilita-postos-de-registos-de-nascimento-no-bie-e-huambo/). Only two provinces have published delivery counts.<br><br>Nothing on the ledger states a national registration target, a backlog figure or a death-registration rate.<br><br>The education ministry made school enrolment independent of documentation: circular 05/2026 of 19 August 2026 orders every school teaching primary and first-cycle secondary education to enrol children holding no birth certificate and no identity card, and to compile by the end of October a survey of undocumented pupils for the justice ministry's registry directorate, whose services are then to travel to the schools and register them ([circular](https://novojornal.co.ao/sociedade/detalhe/med-obriga-escolas-a-aceitar-matricula-de-criancas-que-ainda-nao-possuem-registo-de-nascimento-e-bi-74156.html)).</details> | Advanced, though the youngest cohort is registering worst |
| Benin | A single-window pilot at ten maternities [lifted birth registration to 76.56% against about 22% nationally](https://www.lanation.bj/actualites/aristide-adjinacou-directeur-general-de-lanip-nous-voulons-faire-de-chaque-maternite-une-veritable-porte-dentree-vers-lidentite-legale), and generalisation [slipped three to four months](https://fraternite.bj/guichet-unique-de-declaration-des-naissances-au-benin-un-bond-spectaculaire-du-taux-denregistrement-en-six-mois/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>Behind near-universal biometric coverage sits a weak civil-registration leg: [about 2.5m births were declared between 2021 and 2025 against under 1m actually registered, because parents had to make a second trip](https://www.lanation.bj/actualites/aristide-adjinacou-directeur-general-de-lanip-nous-voulons-faire-de-chaque-maternite-une-veritable-porte-dentree-vers-lidentite-legale).<br><br>2026-07-16 — [a single-window pilot at ten maternities lifted registration to 76.56%, against about 22% nationally in 2025](https://www.lanation.bj/actualites/aristide-adjinacou-directeur-general-de-lanip-nous-voulons-faire-de-chaque-maternite-une-veritable-porte-dentree-vers-lidentite-legale). 2026-02-26 — [the six-month review recorded generalisation slipping three to four months into a consolidation phase](https://fraternite.bj/guichet-unique-de-declaration-des-naissances-au-benin-un-bond-spectaculaire-du-taux-denregistrement-en-six-mois/).<br><br>This is the sharpest finding in the country's identity estate. [A register authenticating about 200,000 times a day](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202602/benin-reports-75-of-identification-services-accessed-through-digital-platforms) is fed by a civil registration process capturing roughly a fifth of births nationally, and the fix works where it has been tried and has not been extended.</details> | Advanced, in the pilot rather than in generalisation |
| Botswana | Civil registration runs on a [partially automated system across 35 district offices and 17 on-site points](https://statsbots.org.bw/sites/default/files/publications/Vital%20Statistics%20Report%202024.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>1969 — [the Births and Deaths Registration Act is the founding statute](https://botswanalaws.com/consolidated-statutes/principle-legislation/births-and-deaths-registration).<br><br>2026-08-01 — [the registration system is described as partially automated and decentralised, introduced in 2003 and operated through 35 district registration offices and 17 on-site registration points at major health facilities](https://statsbots.org.bw/sites/default/files/publications/Vital%20Statistics%20Report%202024.pdf), with the marriage register still manual.<br><br>2026-02-24 — [online registration of births, deaths and marriages was reported complete, awaiting the government platform’s launch](https://www.botswanaspeaks.gov.bw/media/COMMITTEE%20OF%20SUPPPLY%20SPEECHES/MLHA%20-%20Committee%20of%20Supply%20Speech%20FINAL%20%20-%2024-02-2026.pdf).<br><br>No registration-completeness figure is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Burkina Faso | Thirty communes [hold fully digitised registers and 121,000 acts were issued to displaced people in 2025 against a 65,000 target](https://lefaso.net/spip.php?article144154), with [60.65% of births declared within the legal deadline](https://www.amade.org/en/missions/protection/civil-status-for-all/civil-registration-for-children-in-burkina-faso-oe-phase-ii.html). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-02-02 — [thirty communes hold fully digitised registers, and 121,000 civil-status acts were issued to internally displaced people in 2025 against a 65,000 target](https://lefaso.net/spip.php?article144154), against [3,391,907 acts digitised across 54 communes and 145,655 birth certificates issued in 2024](https://www.sidwaya.info/strategie-nationale-de-letat-civil-un-taux-dexecution-physique-de-6411-en-2024/). The two ends count different things, so no rate of progress can be derived from them.<br><br>The coverage figure is the one that bites: [60.65% of births were registered within the two-month legal deadline in 2024](https://www.amade.org/en/missions/protection/civil-status-for-all/civil-registration-for-children-in-burkina-faso-oe-phase-ii.html), leaving nearly four in ten births undeclared in time. The figure is the modernisation directorate's own, carried in a partner programme document.<br><br>Delivering acts to displaced people at nearly twice the target, in a country where two fifths of births miss the deadline, is the shape of a system doing well at recovery and badly at capture.</details> | Advanced |
| Burundi | Biometric enrolment [is operating in 13 pilot communes and decentralised to zone level](https://abpinfo.bi/2026/08/13/le-burundi-veut-construire-un-etat-plus-proche-des-citoyens-plus-efficace-et-inclusif/), with [84% of under-fives registered but 66% holding a certificate](https://www.wearetech.africa/fr/fils/actualites/tech/burundi-l-etat-civil-se-numerise-pour-renforcer-l-identite-des-citoyens). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08-13 — [biometric enrolment for the digital civil registry is operating in 13 pilot communes and has been decentralised to zone level](https://abpinfo.bi/2026/08/13/le-burundi-veut-construire-un-etat-plus-proche-des-citoyens-plus-efficace-et-inclusif/), against a starting position in which [the platform had been presented, every citizen was to re-register afresh and a family-code amendment was required](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/burundi-launches-digital-civil-registry-and-biometric-id-initiative).<br><br>2026-08-11 — coverage was stated on Africa Civil Registration Day: [84% of under-fives registered at birth and 66% holding a birth certificate](https://www.wearetech.africa/fr/fils/actualites/tech/burundi-l-etat-civil-se-numerise-pour-renforcer-l-identite-des-citoyens), on a survey whose date is not given. The eighteen-point gap between registration and certificate is the finding, and it is the gap a digital registry is supposed to close.<br><br>[The ambitions and constraints of the programme were set out in May](https://burundi-eco.com/la-carte-didentite-biometrique-en-marche-entre-ambitions-et-defis/). No national rollout timetable beyond the pilot communes is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Cameroon | Tenders to digitalise civil registration [in Bamenda](https://cameroun.minajobs.net/emplois-stage-recrutement/43874/invitation-to-bid-supply-of-equipment-for-the-digitalisation-of-civil-status-registration-at-03-pilot-center-at-pnud-programme-des-nations-unies-pour-le-developpement) and [the East region](https://www.undp.org/cameroon/stories/dans-lest-du-cameroun-la-digitalisation-de-letat-civil-transforme-progressivement-lacces-lidentite-legale-bertoua-cameroun-aout-2026) were issued in August 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[UNDP issued a tender in August 2026 to digitalise civil registration through the SIGEC system in the East region at Bertoua](https://www.undp.org/cameroon/stories/dans-lest-du-cameroun-la-digitalisation-de-letat-civil-transforme-progressivement-lacces-lidentite-legale-bertoua-cameroun-aout-2026), and [a companion tender covers Bamenda](https://cameroun.minajobs.net/emplois-stage-recrutement/43874/invitation-to-bid-supply-of-equipment-for-the-digitalisation-of-civil-status-registration-at-03-pilot-center-at-pnud-programme-des-nations-unies-pour-le-developpement). [Integration of civil registration with identity systems was argued for in May 2026](https://mail.cameroon-tribune.cm/article.html/76792/en.html/we-need-integrate-our-civil-registration-id-systems).<br><br>[The statistics institute has published civil-status statistics for 2018 to 2022](https://ins-cameroun.cm/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/Rapport-sur-les-statistiques-de-letat-civil-au-Cameroun_version-finale-13-03-2024-1.pdf). No national registration rate after 2022 and no coverage figure for the digitalisation is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Central African Republic | A civil-status modernisation programme [targets 400,000 birth certificates by 2028 and put its 2025 activities at 90% complete](https://oubanguimedias.com/2026/02/23/centrafrique-le-comite-technique-dappui-et-de-suivi-du-projet-dappui-a-la-modernisation-de-letat-civil-valide-les-activites-du-projet-modero/), beside [a 3,000-certificate regional project](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202508/la-francophonie-supports-birth-registration-efforts-across-central-africa-madagascar). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-02-20 — [a steering committee validated the implementing partner's 2025 activities on an externally funded civil-status modernisation programme run with the territorial administration ministry, extending from a capital-and-corridor pilot to new communes, with a target of 400,000 birth certificates by 2028 and 2025 activities put at 90% complete](https://oubanguimedias.com/2026/02/23/centrafrique-le-comite-technique-dappui-et-de-suivi-du-projet-dappui-a-la-modernisation-de-letat-civil-valide-les-activites-du-projet-modero/). It is a single source with no financing figure, contract or end date stated.<br><br>2025-08-12 — beside it, [a regional birth-registration project targets 3,000 certificates in the country](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202508/la-francophonie-supports-birth-registration-efforts-across-central-africa-madagascar), launched eleven days into the window with no progress, delivery or completion record since. It is an order of magnitude smaller than the modernisation programme.<br><br>Civil registration is the input to the identity card and to the digital identity system announced for 2027, and both programmes carrying it are externally run.</details> | Advanced |
| Chad | [Digital civil registration](https://lendjampost.com/tchad-letat-civil-numerique-un-pas-decisif-dans-lidentification-des-citoyens/) was launched with message transfer from registry offices, hospitals, camps and mobile units to the identity agency for validation. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The architecture is reported as message transfer from registry offices, hospitals, camps and mobile units to the identity agency for validation ([registration](https://lendjampost.com/tchad-letat-civil-numerique-un-pas-decisif-dans-lidentification-des-citoyens/), [interoperability](https://tribuneechos.com/tchad-lanats-et-loif-sunissent-pour-la-transformation-numerique-de-letat-civil/)).<br><br>A civil-registration application is dated to 2021 by an international official, at year precision from a single sentence, and no source connects it to the programme launched in September 2025 ([application](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/08/08/sarh-un-atelier-pour-renforcer-la-collecte-et-la-transmission-des-donnees-detat-civil/)).<br><br>Birth registration within the legal deadline is reported from ministry data for 2024, and three accounts round the undeclared total to 2.948m, 2.95m and 2.958m — three figures for one number ([deadline](https://www.alwihdainfo.com/tchad-une-campagne-pour-lidentite-des-enfants-afin-de-renforcer-lenregistrement-a-letat-civil/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Comoros | The [civil-registry modernisation programme is running on two islands with a residual financing need of EUR 9,009,900](http://comoresdroit.centerblog.net/3012-la-modernisation-de-l-etat-civil-un-chantier-strategique). <details><summary>Full record</summary>A strategic plan was adopted in 2024 with the preliminary study not launched. The [study was validated and the programme is running on the main island since 2 October 2025 and the third island since 10 October, with a residual financing need of EUR 9,009,900 put to an international resource-mobilisation mission and a partners' conference on 14 August 2026](http://comoresdroit.centerblog.net/3012-la-modernisation-de-l-etat-civil-un-chantier-strategique), the [study launch reported from the third island](https://www.lagazettedescomores.com/soci%C3%A9t%C3%A9/vers-un-%C3%A9tat-civil-num%C3%A9rique-moh%C3%A9li-ouvre-la-voie-%C3%A0-la-modernisation-nationale-.html). The financing need is a consultant's and no share of it is committed on the record.<br><br>The [agency provided for by the law of 27 July 2023 is in island consultation on a draft implementing decree and is not recorded as created](https://lagazettedescomores.com/soci%C3%A9t%C3%A9/vers-un-d%C3%A9cret-d%E2%80%99application-pour-l%E2%80%99agence-nationale-de-l%E2%80%99%C3%A9tat-civil-.html). The directorate that holds the population register and criminal record was [appropriated KMF 4,000,000 for 2026, wholly goods and services](https://finances.gouv.km/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Decret-N26-003PR-du-16-jan-2026.pdf).</details> | Advanced |
| Congo | An [integrated civil-status system's three modules were validated and presented to the Senate](https://brazzavilleinsider.com/congo-bets-on-digital-records-to-curb-civil-fraud/) as the fix for document fraud; deployment is unfunded. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system was [named as the civil-status reform vehicle at a July 2025 workshop](https://www.finances.gouv.cg/fr/articles/le-congo-franchit-une-%C3%A9tape-d%C3%A9cisive-dans-la-digitalisation-de-l%C3%A9tat-civil) with no build state published, and [validation closed with recommendations in February 2026](http://www.finances.gouv.cg/fr/articles/cloture-travaux-SIFEC_200226).<br><br>2026-08-07 - [three modules - birth, marriage, death - plus exceptional-procedure paths were validated and the system named before the Senate as the remedy for document fraud, with deployment still unfunded](https://brazzavilleinsider.com/congo-bets-on-digital-records-to-curb-civil-fraud/). It is built in-house by the state information-systems agency.<br><br>The interior minister told the Senate that ministry and municipal staff are among those [selling forged civil-status documents](https://datsoue.news/2026/08/08/congo-etat-civil-jean-olessongo-ondaye-annonce-la-traque-des-reseaux-de-falsification/), and no funding decision for deployment is on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The [network reaches 699 centres and more than four million digitised records](https://www.aip.ci/cote-divoire-aip-digitalisation-de-letat-civil-les-sous-prefets-du-goh-mobilises-pour-accompagner-la-reforme-de-loneci/), while [on-time registration sits at 55.4% and falling](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2635039/economie/annuaire-statistique-detat-civil-de-lannee-2024-le-taux-denregistrement-des-naissances-dans-les-delais-en-nette-regression). <details><summary>Full record</summary>Software and hardware are now [installed in every municipal and sub-prefecture civil-status centre for digital recording of births, marriages and deaths, financed by the European Union, with the national system covering 699 centres and more than four million records digitised](https://www.aip.ci/cote-divoire-aip-digitalisation-de-letat-civil-les-sous-prefets-du-goh-mobilises-pour-accompagner-la-reforme-de-loneci/).<br><br>The outcome measure went the other way. The [2024 yearbook, presented on 30 June 2025, puts on-time birth registration at 55.4% for 2024 against 56.6% in 2023 and 66.8% in 2021, and is still the latest published](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2635039/economie/annuaire-statistique-detat-civil-de-lannee-2024-le-taux-denregistrement-des-naissances-dans-les-delais-en-nette-regression). The [director general separately put the birth-declaration rate below 60%](https://www.linfodrome.com/societe/122249-etat-civil-identification-l-oneci-renforce-les-capacites-des-speakers-en-langues-locales-pour-un-relais-efficace).
