No 2025 yearbook is held, so the falling trend has not been updated in over a year.<br><br>The electronic basis is a 2019 decree: [civil-status acts may be drawn up by computerised as well as manual means, automated processing by registration centres requires prior authorisation from the data-protection authority, and hosting may be delegated to a private body only where it is established and hosting on Ivorian territory](https://www.oneci.ci/uploads/files/articles/1638800505-decret-n2019-805-du-02-octobre-2019.pdf).<br><br>2025-09-01 - the largest commune brought [a second interactive kiosk into service issuing birth, marriage and death extracts from its own records, taking stated capacity above three thousand extracts a day](https://www.koaci.com/article/2025/09/02/cote-divoire/societe/cote-divoire-yopougon-mise-en-service-de-la-deuxieme-borne-digitale-interactive-detablissement-de-documents-administratifs-3000-extraits-pourront-etre-etablis-par-jour_189848.html); the throughput figure is the municipality's own claim.</details> | Mixed, the digital civil-status network reached 699 centres while the on-time birth registration rate stands at 55.4% and has not been republished |
| Djibouti | [Civil-registry archive digitisation is running alongside enrolment](https://www.lanation.dj/notre-vision-est-de-faire-de-la-direction-generale-de-la-population-et-de-la-famille-une-administration-entierement-modernisee-interconnectee-et-proche-des-citoyens-abdi-abdillahi-obsie/), with staffing and archive space named as the constraints. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The programme was not on record at the window's opening. It is [under way alongside biometric enrolment, with staffing and archive space named as the constraints and no completion date published](https://www.lanation.dj/notre-vision-est-de-faire-de-la-direction-generale-de-la-population-et-de-la-famille-une-administration-entierement-modernisee-interconnectee-et-proche-des-citoyens-abdi-abdillahi-obsie/).<br><br>No record count, coverage figure or registration rate is held at any date. The directorate that runs it is the same one preparing real-time identity verification for banks and social protection, so a register whose digitisation is constrained by staffing and shelf space is the intended source for those checks.</details> | Advanced |
| Egypt | Registration completeness is put at [96.6% for live births and 95.3% for deaths](https://unstats.un.org/unsd/demographic-social/products/dyb/documents/metadata/2024/Egypt-2024-VT-Metadata.pdf) on the country's own submission. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statutory basis is [the 1994 civil status law, whose article 1 places implementation with the civil status organisation at the interior ministry and empowers the minister to establish civil-status information systems](https://mksegypt.org/ar/laws/23813).<br><br>An [electronic birth and death registration system was recorded as under way from 2017](http://www.au.int/sites/default/files/newsevents/workingdocuments/37474-wd-crvs-v-the_electronic_birth_and_death_registration_system_in_egypt.pdf), and the country's own submission puts [estimated completeness at 96.6% for live births and 95.3% for deaths](https://unstats.un.org/unsd/demographic-social/products/dyb/documents/metadata/2024/Egypt-2024-VT-Metadata.pdf).<br><br>2026-07-17 - the country's [own presentation to the annual registrar generals' meeting contrasts the position in 2017 with 2026](https://apai-crvs.uneca.org/sites/default/files/resourceuploads/day-2-egypt_crvs_transformation_journey.pdf).</details> | Advanced |
| Equatorial Guinea | The [civil registry remains paper-based](https://realequatorialguinea.com/destacado/politica/guinea-ecuatorial-acogera-la-reunion-africana-sobre-registro-civil-y-estadisticas-vitales/), with a 2018 digital pilot and fraudulent birth certificates identified as a control problem. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The country hosted the continental civil-registration meeting in June 2025 and no digitisation milestone or registration coverage figure has followed ([meeting](https://realequatorialguinea.com/destacado/politica/guinea-ecuatorial-acogera-la-reunion-africana-sobre-registro-civil-y-estadisticas-vitales/)).<br><br>The government has stated it wants a rigorous control mechanism for issuing birth certificates, which is the same document the identity document depends on ([control mechanism](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/el_gobierno_quiere_implementar_un_mecanismo_riguroso_de_control_para_la_adquisicion_del_certificado_literal)).</details> | No change |
| Eswatini | A [proposed DNA requirement for birth certificates](https://times.co.sz/38899/news/dna-plan-could-swallow-e126m-of-home-affairs-budget/) is costed at about E126m a year, roughly 46 per cent of the home affairs budget. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The costing is reported against the ministry's budget ([proposal](https://times.co.sz/38899/news/dna-plan-could-swallow-e126m-of-home-affairs-budget/)).<br><br>It is a proposal only. Requiring genetic testing to register a birth would be the most invasive civil-registration rule in this frame, and the account that reaches the base is about its cost rather than its lawful basis, consent model or effect on registration rates.<br><br>The improvement work has a structure behind it: a national steering committee and technical working group were established under a continental mentorship programme whose second phase launched in February 2022 ([programme](https://ecastats.uneca.org/asn/en-us/Sections/News-and-events/NE-Eswatini-became-the-2nd-country-to-officially-launch-phase-two)), with a university partner supporting the government since 2015 on foreign health-programme funding ([support](https://icap.columbia.edu/news-events/icap-supports-eswatini-to-strengthen-countrys-civil-registration-process-birth-deaths-and-marriage-records/)). [Over 10,000 of the births occurring in 2021 were registered late](https://icap.columbia.edu/news-events/icap-supports-eswatini-to-strengthen-countrys-civil-registration-process-birth-deaths-and-marriage-records/).</details> | Advanced |
| Ethiopia | A home-grown national civil registration architecture was set out with a phased rollout plan, and the register was extended to refugees ([presentation](https://apai-crvs.uneca.org/sites/default/files/resourceuploads/day-2-digitization-ethiopia.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>Ethiopia's national electronic civil registration and vital statistics architecture — home-grown, decentralised and multi-tenant — was presented in July 2026 with its phased pilot-to-national rollout plan ([presentation](https://apai-crvs.uneca.org/sites/default/files/resourceuploads/day-2-digitization-ethiopia.pdf)).<br><br>The refugee service and the Immigration and Citizenship Service ran a joint training of trainers, financed by UNICEF, implementing the updated civil registration system for refugees under the amended proclamation ([training](https://rrs.et/rrs-in-collaboration-with-the-immigration-and-citizenship-service-ics-is-conducting-a-training-of-trainers-tot-on-civil-and-family-registration-system-for-refugee-for-staff-draw/)).<br><br>The city-level pilot established connectivity from the central level down to woreda level, with family and vital-event registration running digitally ([statement](https://www.gcs.gov.et/en/2025/12/22/digital-ethiopia-2025-has-brought-about-a-significant-change-in-improving-access-to-civil-registration-and-citizenship-services/)).<br><br>The base holds architecture and plans, and no registration coverage rate for births or deaths.</details> | Advanced |
| Gabon | The [biometric civil registry](https://www.securetechalliance.org/gemalto-selected-to-build-secure-and-reliable-biometric-national-registry-in-gabon/) has operated since 2012 and remains the seed base for the register that would replace it. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It was built under a supplier contract of September 2012 ([the registry](https://www.securetechalliance.org/gemalto-selected-to-build-secure-and-reliable-biometric-national-registry-in-gabon/)) and is named as an existing system in the 2025 stocktake of legal identity ([stocktake](https://www.gabonreview.com/identification-legale-et-digitale-le-gabon-enclenche-la-mise-en-oeuvre-de-trois-projets-strategiques/)).<br><br>No registration count, coverage figure, birth-registration rate or interoperability arrangement is held for it, so what is recorded is that it operates and nothing about how much of the population it holds.</details> | No change |
| Gambia | The [electronic civil registration system](https://www.worldbank.org/en/results/2024/03/28/afw-transformative-journey-of-mass-birth-registration-and-health-insurance-in-the-gambia) holds about 1.17m people with a unique identification number, and its integration with the identity card has produced no milestone. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A mass birth-registration and health-insurance campaign reached about 1.17m people, and the system is described as the interoperability backbone ([campaign](https://www.worldbank.org/en/results/2024/03/28/afw-transformative-journey-of-mass-birth-registration-and-health-insurance-in-the-gambia)).<br><br>A June 2025 commitment to integrate it with identity cards and the passport has produced no published integration milestone in the fourteen months since ([commitment](https://thepoint.gm/africa/gambia/headlines/govt-to-integrate-id-cards-with-electronic-civil-registration)).<br><br>That matters more now than it did: an identity system went live in July 2026 and the register that would feed it is on the same unchanged footing it held two years ago.</details> | No change |
| Ghana | The births and deaths registry [received 181,000 security papers](https://www.graphic.com.gh/news/general-news/ghana-news-births-and-deaths-registry-receives-security-papers-begins-clearing-backlog.html) in July 2025 against 881,000 copies needed to clear its backlog. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Printing is decentralised across 470 offices in 261 districts with electronically signed certificates ([registry](https://www.graphic.com.gh/news/general-news/ghana-news-births-and-deaths-registry-receives-security-papers-begins-clearing-backlog.html)).<br><br>Nothing dated has followed, so whether the backlog cleared is not established, and the base holds no registration completeness rate, no digital birth record count and no link between the registry and the identity card.</details> | No change |
| Guinea | An [administrative census delivered secured civil-registry inputs](https://mediaguinee.com/2026/03/conakry-le-pn-ravec-dote-les-13-communes-dun-important-lot-dintrants-detat-civil-dont-les-certificats-de-non-remariage/) to the population register, which is now the single official source for the electoral roll. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Enumeration ran from 15 April 2025 and its outputs feed the population register and the census ([census](https://mediaguinee.com/2026/03/conakry-le-pn-ravec-dote-les-13-communes-dun-important-lot-dintrants-detat-civil-dont-les-certificats-de-non-remariage/)).<br><br>A convention between the elections directorate and the civil-registry office institutionalised data sharing from the register to the roll, making it the roll's single official source ([convention](https://mediaguinee.com/2026/01/conakry-la-dge-et-loneci-scellent-une-alliance-pour-un-fichier-electoral-fiable/)).<br><br>No registration completeness figure, record count or update rule for the register itself is published.</details> | Regressed |
| Guinea-Bissau | Birth registration [stands at about 46% of under-fives](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202506/guinea-bissau-deploys-efforts-to-digitize-birth-registration-national-id-systems), with [SIREC digitising births, deaths and marriages](https://ang.gw/justica-servicos-de-identificacao-civil-registos-e-notariado-serao-digitalizados-e-informatizados/) and no fresher figure since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Ministry of Justice's civil registration office [describes SIREC digitising births, deaths and marriages with UNDP-trained officers, roughly three months into a Bissau-first rollout, against a target of at least 90% registration, with registration of under-fives at about 46% from an undated 23.4% baseline](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202506/guinea-bissau-deploys-efforts-to-digitize-birth-registration-national-id-systems). A parallel account [covers the digitisation of civil identification, registry and notary services](https://ang.gw/justica-servicos-de-identificacao-civil-registos-e-notariado-serao-digitalizados-e-informatizados/).<br><br>Behind them, a UNDP project co-financed by Switzerland [built a digitisation centre and software to recover the paper civil-registry books](https://www.undp.org/guinea-bissau/projects/building-national-identification-system-supporting-ministry-justice-scanning-and-digitizing-civil-registry-books-guinea-bissau), held at year precision.<br><br>All of it predates the twelve-month window. No dated confirmation of regional extension, no fresher registration rate and no death or marriage registration figure appears in it.</details> | No change |
| Kenya | Birth certificates became [downloadable and printable online](https://www.kenyans.co.ke/news/124508-kenyans-download-and-print-birth-certificates-online) after application, where collection had required a visit. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The change was announced by the Interior Cabinet Secretary in June 2026 ([announcement](https://www.kenyans.co.ke/news/124508-kenyans-download-and-print-birth-certificates-online)).<br><br>It completes online delivery for one document at the point where the statute now requires a civil-registration office in each of 290 sub-counties, so the register is being made both more local and less visited at once.</details> | Advanced |
| Lesotho | [Digital birth registration](https://www.eeas.europa.eu/delegations/lesotho/ntlafatsa-bana-launch-digital-birth-registration) has been in operation since April 2025 under a programme run with the European Union and UNICEF, with no later enrolment figure. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The programme launched on 2 April 2025 with the European Union and UNICEF ([launch](https://www.eeas.europa.eu/delegations/lesotho/ntlafatsa-bana-launch-digital-birth-registration)).<br><br>No registration count, coverage share or link to the national identification system has been published since, so the base records that the registration is digital and not how much of the population it reaches.</details> | No change |
| Liberia | A [Birth and Death Registration Policy 2026-2031](https://www.liberianobserver.com/news/gov-t-sets-ambitious-health-reform-agenda/article_8c047b96-fcd8-4636-a8aa-ffbb9bf7223a.html) was launched in August 2026, superseding the 2017 policy. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [2017 policy](http://moh.gov.lr/wp-content/uploads/Birth-and-Death-Registration-Policy_Dec_18_2017.pdf) placed birth and death registration under the health ministry's Bureau of Vital Statistics and Principal Registrar and stated plainly that the system had never been fully developed.<br><br>Its successor was [launched at the national health review conference of 4-6 August 2026](https://www.liberianobserver.com/news/gov-t-sets-ambitious-health-reform-agenda/article_8c047b96-fcd8-4636-a8aa-ffbb9bf7223a.html), with no policy text, target or budget line reported. The ministry's own [statement of priorities for 2026-2036](https://moh.gov.lr/news/2026/liberia-showcases-a-decade-of-progress-in-civil-registration-and-vital-statistics-at-africas-registrar-generals-meeting/) names expanding digital registration, improving cause-of-death reporting and interoperability with the identity registry — objectives, not achieved conditions.</details> | Advanced |
| Libya | [Project Intilaqa](https://libyaherald.com/2024/09/pm-aldabaiba-follows-up-on-intilaqa-project-for-matching-civil-registry-electronic-data-with-paper-records) matched the civil registry's electronic and paper records across 257 offices to 97 per cent, and no later figure has been published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The project matched electronic data to paper records in 257 offices and printed 1.8m family records, reaching 97 per cent completion as at September 2024 ([matching](https://libyaherald.com/2024/09/pm-aldabaiba-follows-up-on-intilaqa-project-for-matching-civil-registry-electronic-data-with-paper-records)). It had been outstanding since the mid-1970s.<br><br>Nothing has been published in the two years since on the remainder, on error rates found, or on how the matched register is now maintained.</details> | No change |
| Madagascar | About [ten million civil records](https://www.intelligentcio.com/africa/2026/05/08/ai-powered-digitisation-project-positions-madagascar-as-a-model-for-african-digital-identity-modernisation/) were digitised across 1,695 communes, under an [EUR 8.5m registry and database contract](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202505/madagascar-selects-in-groupe-for-e8-5m-digital-id-contract) awarded in May 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The digitisation covered about ten million records across 1,695 communes using optical character recognition and machine indexing, carried out with mobile satellite and solar units ([digitisation](https://www.intelligentcio.com/africa/2026/05/08/ai-powered-digitisation-project-positions-madagascar-as-a-model-for-african-digital-identity-modernisation/)).<br><br>A civil registry and database management contract of EUR 8.5m was awarded in May 2025, with no delivery milestone published since ([contract](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202505/madagascar-selects-in-groupe-for-e8-5m-digital-id-contract)). No completeness rate, error rate or count of records still on paper is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Malawi | A [national birth registration campaign](https://malawi24.com/2026/01/16/k17-8bn-push-to-register-4-6-million-children/) runs seven phases across 25 districts targeting about 4.6m unregistered children, using primary schools as registration centres. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The campaign's first phase was witnessed at Dedza; the bureau had registered about 3.8m children since 2015 as at January 2026 ([campaign](https://malawi24.com/2026/01/16/k17-8bn-push-to-register-4-6-million-children/), [partnership](https://www.eeas.europa.eu/delegations/malawi/10-years-fruitful-partnership-government-malawi-civil-registration_en)).<br><br>MWK 17.8bn is required in total, MWK 5.4bn was allocated for phases one and two, and MWK 12.4bn is outstanding. No mid-campaign registration total is on record.</details> | Advanced |
| Mali | [Birth registration stands at 89.5 per cent](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202506/chad-mali-senegal-pursue-birth-registration-reforms-to-close-gaps) across 747 functional centres while [death registration is about 20 per cent](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202506/chad-mali-senegal-pursue-birth-registration-reforms-to-close-gaps), and the [village register](https://gouvernement.ml/communique-du-conseil-des-ministres-cm-n2026-29-sgg/) remains hand-kept. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Birth registration was recorded at 89.5 per cent across 747 functional primary centres in mid-2025, with no later figure published ([births](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202506/chad-mali-senegal-pursue-birth-registration-reforms-to-close-gaps)).<br><br>Death registration stood at about 20 per cent on the same account ([deaths](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202506/chad-mali-senegal-pursue-birth-registration-reforms-to-close-gaps)). A civil register that captures nine births in ten and one death in five cannot support the population arithmetic that other systems would draw on.<br><br>The village census ledger from which family record books are issued was formalised in a draft ordinance adopted in July 2026, replacing a 2006 statute, and remains a hand-kept ledger ([ordinance](https://gouvernement.ml/communique-du-conseil-des-ministres-cm-n2026-29-sgg/)).</details> | Mixed, birth registration near ninety per cent against death registration near twenty, on a village ledger still kept by hand |
| Mauritania | The [national register holds more than 5.1 million people](https://www.essadek.info/%d8%a3%d9%83%d8%ab%d8%b1-%d9%85%d9%86-5-1-%d9%85%d9%84%d8%a7%d9%8a%d9%8a%d9%86-%d9%85%d8%b3%d8%ac%d9%84-%d8%a8%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%b3%d8%ac%d9%84-%d8%a7%d9%84%d9%88%d8%b7%d9%86%d9%8a/) and enrolment is described as a permanent public service; the [civil status code was amended in 2025](https://natlex.ilo.org/dyn/natlex2/natlex2/files/download/117696/MRT-117696.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The interior minister gave the figure to parliament on 18 June 2026 and separately described enrolment as having become a permanent public service guaranteeing every citizen's right to be registered ([figure](https://www.essadek.info/%d8%a3%d9%83%d8%ab%d8%b1-%d9%85%d9%86-5-1-%d9%85%d9%84%d8%a7%d9%8a%d9%8a%d9%86-%d9%85%d8%b3%d8%ac%d9%84-%d8%a8%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%b3%d8%ac%d9%84-%d8%a7%d9%84%d9%88%d8%b7%d9%86%d9%8a/), [enrolment](https://kassataya.com/ministre-de-linterieur-lenrolement-de-la-population-est-devenu-un-service-public-permanent-qui-garantit-le-droit-de-tous-les-citoyens-a-lenregistrement/)).<br><br>The system for running the register and secure documents was renewed in August 2021 across 153 reception centres ([system](https://www.ami.mr/archives/119459)), and the ministry's own half-year report for 2026 records the biometric interconnection of the register with civil status ([interconnection](https://dgat.interieur.gov.mr/node/397)).<br><br>No denominator, coverage rate or age breakdown accompanies the 5.1 million, and no independent audit of the register is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritius | The [civil registration base](https://csd.govmu.org/DocumentsList/FINALISED%20ANNUAL%20REPORT%20CSD.pdf) is recorded as complete and underpinning the identity system, on a performance report for 2023-2024. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The civil status division's performance report for 2023-2024 is the most recent account held ([report](https://csd.govmu.org/DocumentsList/FINALISED%20ANNUAL%20REPORT%20CSD.pdf), [presentation](https://www.id4africa.com/2019_event/presentations/InF5/3-William-Ayelou-Mauritius.pdf)).<br><br>No later performance report is held, and no registration rate, backlog or error measure has been published since. A complete base is the assumption on which the identity wallet and the social-security digitisation both rest.</details> | No change |
| Morocco | The digital register created by [law 36.21](https://www.collectivites-territoriales.gov.ma/ar/fy-alwajhtakhbar/ahm-mstjdat-qanwn-alhalt-almdnyt-rqm-3621-alsadr-bshan-ttbyqh-alzhyr-alshryf-rqm) is operational; [ten governors were given three weeks](https://fr.hespress.com/447050-interieur-les-walis-sommes-daccelerer-la-numerisation-de-letat-civil.html) to digitise paper stock. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [law](https://www.collectivites-territoriales.gov.ma/ar/fy-alwajhtakhbar/ahm-mstjdat-qanwn-alhalt-almdnyt-rqm-3621-alsadr-bshan-ttbyqh-alzhyr-alshryf-rqm) creates the national digital civil-status register and the birth-linked identifier, and the [implementing decree of July 2023](https://www.collectivites-territoriales.gov.ma/fr/actualites-juridiques/publication-au-bulletin-officiel-dun-decret-relatif-letat-civil) put the platform into operation.<br><br>Catching up on paper is the live task: [ten regional governors were given three weeks in October 2025](https://fr.hespress.com/447050-interieur-les-walis-sommes-daccelerer-la-numerisation-de-letat-civil.html), and a [further bill adopted unanimously in December 2025 lets the civil-status commission sit without its president to clear the name-change backlog](https://lematin.ma/nation/le-projet-de-loi-sur-letat-civil-adopte-a-lunanimite-par-les-conseillers/318360). The municipal programme records the [registry system live in 59 of 97 municipalities](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099030926112020747/pdf/P168147-76ebb47f-2ef4-42ba-8d67-88b617eb1f27.pdf).</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | The [electronic civil registration system](https://www.crvslaws.org/mozambique/summary) established by the revised Civil Registration Code runs behind a [free birth registration campaign](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-government-launchs-free-birth-registration/) targeting about seven million people to the end of 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The national civil registration system is administered by the justice ministry through the national directorate of registries and notary services, established by the Civil Registration Code as revised in 2018 and integrated with the national identity and health systems; the Code mandates registration of all vital events, compulsory free birth registration within 120 days, and electronic transmission of data to the statistics institute ([code](https://www.crvslaws.org/mozambique/summary), [portal](https://civil.registos.gov.mz/crvs/)).<br><br>The campaign launched in March 2026 and runs to 31 December, targeting about seven million people at 25,000 registrations a day through up to 500 mobile brigades, with costs met by a donor-funded governance project ([campaign](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-government-launchs-free-birth-registration/)); no mid-campaign total is on record ([campaign](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-government-launchs-free-birth-registration/), [launch](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-chapo-calls-on-population-to-join-free-civil-registration-campaign/)).<br><br>A tender for civil registration and population register services closed in February 2026, requiring bidders to show a US$7m or two US$4m track record and a US$90,000 bid security ([tender](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202601/mozambique-seeks-expert-services-to-support-mass-birth-id-registration)); no award, contractor or contract value is established ([tender](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202601/mozambique-seeks-expert-services-to-support-mass-birth-id-registration)).<br><br>Two earlier vital-statistics programmes closed fully disbursed at the end of 2023, at CAD 19.5m and CAD 6.5m ([first phase](https://d-portal.iatistandard.org/ctrack.html#view=act&aid=CA-3-D002529001), [second phase](https://d-portal.iatistandard.org/ctrack.html#view=act&aid=CA-3-P001300002)).</details> | Advanced |
| Namibia | The civil registration department had [N$178,122,000 for 2025/26](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202505/namibia-to-step-up-civil-registration-efforts-with-9m-budget) and no 2026/27 allocation is on file. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A separate N$70m was allocated to the digital identity within a N$7.4bn ministry budget ([allocation](https://idtechwire.com/namibia-prepares-to-roll-out-national-digital-id-system)).<br><br>No 2026/27 civil-registration allocation is on file ([budget](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202505/namibia-to-step-up-civil-registration-efforts-with-9m-budget)), which matters in the year the electronic card is to be issued and the school-based registration drive is running.</details> | No change, no 2026/27 allocation published against the previous year's N$178.1m |
| Niger | A [National Civil Register](https://www.lesahel.org/wuri-niger-restructure-une-opportunite-pour-une-identification-universelle-de-la-population-au-service-de-linclusion-et-du-un-puissant-facteur-de-developpement-inclusif-et-une-dimension/) was instituted in April 2026, [birth declaration reached 79 per cent](https://www.lesahel.org/dosso-lancement-de-la-tournee-nationale-de-sensibilisation-sur-letat-civil/) on one series, and [68 per cent of communes](https://anp.ne/tahoua-lancement-des-activites-commemoratives-de-la-journee-africaine-de-letat-civil-et-des-statistiques-de-letat/) are computerised. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The civil-status and identification reforms were taken on a national awareness tour, whose fourth stage opened in Agadez to mobilise local actors for inclusive governance ([workshop](https://anp.ne/ouverture-a-agadez-dun-atelier-de-sensibilisation-sur-les-reformes-en-matiere-de-letat-civil-et-didentification-des-personnes-au-niger/)).<br><br>The register was instituted by the April 2026 ordonnance and is designed to interoperate with the population and nationality registers ([register](https://www.lesahel.org/wuri-niger-restructure-une-opportunite-pour-une-identification-universelle-de-la-population-au-service-de-linclusion-et-du-un-puissant-facteur-de-developpement-inclusif-et-une-dimension/)).<br><br>Birth declaration is given at 79 per cent against 53.48 per cent in 2022 on one series, while the interior ministry separately gave 80 per cent in 2026 against 60 in 2023 and an agency put one region alone at a rise from 69 to 75 per cent ([rate](https://www.lesahel.org/dosso-lancement-de-la-tournee-nationale-de-sensibilisation-sur-letat-civil/)); three differently based series are on record and none is reconciled to another ([rate](https://www.lesahel.org/dosso-lancement-de-la-tournee-nationale-de-sensibilisation-sur-letat-civil/), [commemoration](https://anp.ne/tahoua-lancement-des-activites-commemoratives-de-la-journee-africaine-de-letat-civil-et-des-statistiques-de-letat/), [tour](https://anp.ne/lancement-a-tahoua-dune-tournee-nationale-de-vulgarisation-des-textes-juridiques-et-des-operations-denregistrement-intermediaire-au-registre-national-de-la-population/)).<br><br>An agency puts 68 per cent of communes nationwide computerised on its own account of the support it has given, and no ministry count of computerised communes is held ([communes](https://anp.ne/tahoua-lancement-des-activites-commemoratives-de-la-journee-africaine-de-letat-civil-et-des-statistiques-de-letat/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Nigeria | [Paper registration was discontinued](https://www.nigeriacommunicationsweek.com.ng/npc-opens-nationwide-digital-birth-death-registration-platform/) from July 2026, but none of 371 targeted health facilities is live, [120,469 certificates were issued against 443,162 registrations](https://www.vanguardngr.com/2026/08/3-5m-missing-births-how-hospitals-can-close-nigerias-registration-gap/), and the platform showed charges for documents stated to be free. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Registration went nationwide on a single platform through 4,011 centres toward a target of about 8,000, with newborn registration requiring at least one parent to hold a valid identity number and the register run as a public-private partnership ([platform](https://www.nigeriacommunicationsweek.com.ng/npc-opens-nationwide-digital-birth-death-registration-platform/), [state](https://fmino.gov.ng/akwa-ibom-to-implement-digital-civil-registration-system/)).<br><br>It has stalled at the health-facility layer: none of 371 targeted facilities is live despite completed training, zero birth notifications were recorded in the reporting year, 120,469 certificates were issued against 443,162 registrations, and an estimated 3.5 million children across seven southern states hold no certificate ([gap](https://www.vanguardngr.com/2026/08/3-5m-missing-births-how-hospitals-can-close-nigerias-registration-gap/)).<br><br>The platform displayed charges of US$30 to US$80 for documents the commission says are free, and carried one man's record with a wife and a son unknown to him until the commission corrected it free of charge the day after publication ([corrections](https://fij.ng/article/after-fijs-story-npc-removes-wrong-wife-strange-son-listed-as-lagos-mans-family/), [records](https://fij.ng/article/lagos-man-visited-birth-registration-website-he-finds-strangers-listed-as-son-wife/)).</details> | Mixed, paper registration discontinued nationwide while the health-facility layer recorded zero birth notifications |
| Rwanda | A [fully digital civil registration chain](https://www.ktpress.rw/2026/04/rwanda-is-counting-everything-and-changing-in-the-process/) runs from hospitals to village administration, and the [system is documented as a foundation model](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202608/birth-registration-emerges-as-foundation-for-digital-public-infrastructure) in published case studies. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Birth-registration completeness is reported in the 2025 vital statistics report, on a chain that runs digitally from hospitals through to village administration ([completeness](https://www.ktpress.rw/2026/04/rwanda-is-counting-everything-and-changing-in-the-process/)).<br><br>The identity agency issued standard operating procedures in December 2023 for a national centralised and integrated system, making health facilities responsible for registering the births and deaths occurring in them; that is the earliest position the base holds for the system ([procedures](https://www.nida.gov.rw/index.php?eID=dumpFile&f=92643&t=f&token=7ea9d6c4602ce4d2ae43fc6b669787b89edad1c1)).<br><br>The system is documented as a digital public infrastructure foundation model in published case studies; the account and its figures are those of the advocacy organisation that has worked on the system since 2016, and no government statement of the same figures is held ([case studies](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202608/birth-registration-emerges-as-foundation-for-digital-public-infrastructure)).<br><br>That qualification matters at exactly the point the system is being held up as a model for others, and the immunisation registry recorded separately is integrated with it.</details> | Advanced |
| Sao Tome and Principe | The life-events system has run nationally since 2018, and birth registration is now [entered electronically at every maternity unit](https://rstp.st/2026/03/10/direcao-geral-dos-registos-e-notariados-com-mais-de-3-mil-registos-de-nascimento-por-ano/), eliminating paper at the point of birth. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The directorate's service page sets out the registrable acts - birth registration including free registration under one year, acknowledgement of paternity, marriage and transcription, name changes, nationality and death entries - with fees, deadlines and the offices at which each is requested ([services](https://dgrn.gov.st/reg_civil_cid.php)).<br><br>The country diagnostic records the system as implemented in 2018 enabling digital registration of life events across the country, with a centralised database holding birth data from 1920 to 2010 ([diagnostic](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099032824212568030/txt/P171592-4790f2f6-54a3-46db-9a2f-d7c1e3005beb.txt)).<br><br>The chief conservator reports registration entered directly into the database from computers at every maternity, with 3,077 births and 1,063 deaths registered in 2025 ([registry](https://rstp.st/2026/03/10/direcao-geral-dos-registos-e-notariados-com-mais-de-3-mil-registos-de-nascimento-por-ano/)). Death registration is a quarter of birth registration and nothing held explains the gap.</details> | Advanced |
| Senegal | [Civil-status software reached 437 of 629 centres](https://fr.linkedin.com/posts/anec-senegal_%C3%A9tat-davancement-au-18-juillet-2026-lanec-activity-7485345706107625472-_yhI), [15.3m records are loaded live](https://africabusinessinsight.com/senegal-digitizes-more-than-15-million/), and the [archive totals](https://aps.sn/le-programme-nekkal-a-permis-dimportantes-avancees-dans-la-modernisation-de-letat-civil-selon-un-responsable/) are given as two unreconciled figures. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The deployment figure was given at African Civil Registration Day in August 2026 against a denominator of 629 centres; the agency's director-general frames civil registration as an instrument of sovereignty, and the same agency says its 2026 budget has not been notified ([deployment](https://aps.sn/la-modernisation-de-letat-civil-une-veritable-politique-publique-et-strategique-selon-le-directeur-general-de-lanec/), [progress](https://fr.linkedin.com/posts/anec-senegal_%C3%A9tat-davancement-au-18-juillet-2026-lanec-activity-7485345706107625472-_yhI), [presentation](https://www.child-identity.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/PRESENTATIONETATSENEGAL-Aliou-Sall.pdf)).<br><br>The live database holds 15.3m records loaded, a subset of archive stock rather than a replacement figure; English coverage calls them birth records where the agency's own term covers all events ([database](https://africabusinessinsight.com/senegal-digitizes-more-than-15-million/)).<br><br>The June 2026 closing report gives 10m digitised and 20m indexed as two separate totals, unreconciled with the August 2025 reading ([archive](https://aps.sn/le-programme-nekkal-a-permis-dimportantes-avancees-dans-la-modernisation-de-letat-civil-selon-un-responsable/), [agency](https://www.child-identity.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/PRESENTATIONETATSENEGAL-Aliou-Sall.pdf)). Remediation ran at one region in August 2026 on the agency's own pilot data, with no national completion date stated ([remediation](https://directactu.net/2026/08/16/par-matar-ndiaye-dg-de-lanec-avancement-de-la-remediation-nationale-sur-la-digitalisation-de-letat-civil-une-progression-territoriale-structuree-du-lgec-et-de-sama-etat-civil/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Seychelles | [Civil-status certificates are issued online](https://www.ics.gov.sc/about/media-centre/publications/launch-of-new-digital-solutions-for-public-services) with two-working-day delivery, saved to the identity platform's document wallet. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Birth, marriage and death certificates are available online with delivery in two working days, saved into the identity platform's document wallet ([certificates](https://www.ics.gov.sc/about/media-centre/publications/launch-of-new-digital-solutions-for-public-services)).<br><br>That is a working registry-to-credential link — the certificate lands in the same wallet as the identity — and it is one of very few in this frame where the two systems are joined at the point of issue.<br><br>No transaction volume, registration completeness or uptake figure is published.</details> | No change |
| Sierra Leone | Supplier engagement opened for an [integrated digital mortality surveillance, registration and certification system](https://sierraloaded.sl/health/npha-advances-digital-mortality-system/), and no cost, supplier, timetable or coverage target is stated. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Engagement opened in August 2026 ([system](https://sierraloaded.sl/health/npha-advances-digital-mortality-system/)).<br><br>The coverage figure attached to it is the agency's own. Death registration at that level means the civil register captures a small fraction of deaths, which is what the system is meant to change and what nothing yet measures.</details> | Advanced |
| Somalia | A [unified digital civil registration system](https://www.opencrvs.org/about-us/country-stories/somalia) was documented in January 2026, following a nineteen-district pilot. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The unified system was documented on an open-source civil registration platform in January 2026 ([system](https://www.opencrvs.org/about-us/country-stories/somalia)), after a nineteen-district pilot in 2025 ([pilot](https://www.ecofinagency.com/news-digital/1108-48024-somalia-pilots-19-district-digital-civil-registry-lays-groundwork-for-nationwide-legal-identity)) and an overhaul begun at the start of that year ([overhaul](https://www.hiiraan.com/news4/2025/Jan/199818/somalia_launches_ambitious_civil_registration_overhaul.aspx)).<br><br>No registration volume, district coverage or link to the identity register is published, so whether a birth registered here produces an identity credential is unestablished.</details> | Advanced |
| South Africa | [360,000 identity documents sat uncollected](https://www.sanews.gov.za/south-africa/home-affairs-extends-operational-hours-support-final-voter-registration) at the end of July 2026, on the department's own statement; the base holds no prior figure to compare. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-31 — the department opened every office nationwide for the final voter registration weekend, [stating that 360,000 identity documents sat uncollected](https://www.sanews.gov.za/south-africa/home-affairs-extends-operational-hours-support-final-voter-registration). A valid document is a precondition of registering to vote, so the count is a measure of exclusion and not of stock control.<br><br>It is a first count. No prior figure, no breakdown by office or province and no statement of how long the documents have been waiting is published, so the direction cannot be stated and the level can. Separately, a [court found statutory exclusions from birth registration unconstitutional](https://www.timeslive.co.za/news/south-africa/2026-07-29-court-rules-birth-registration-exclusions-unconstitutional/) within the window, which goes to the same register from the entry end rather than the collection end.</details> | No change, a first count with no earlier figure behind it |
| South Sudan | A [centralised civil registration system](https://www.eyeradio.org/civil-registry-to-introduce-digital-system-for-birth-death-and-marriage-registration/) was announced with health facilities notifying births, one issuing authority and compulsory death registration, against [birth registration coverage](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/feature/2026/07/22/in-the-worlds-youngest-nation-how-do-you-prove-you-exist) that has not moved. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The announcement came in July 2026 ([announcement](https://www.eyeradio.org/civil-registry-to-introduce-digital-system-for-birth-death-and-marriage-registration/), [statelessness](https://statelessnessalliance.org/online-marketplace/strengthening-civil-registration-and-ending-statelessness-in-south-sudan)).<br><br>Birth registration coverage has not moved on the figures held, which come from an identity diagnostic and a statelessness study rather than from a national series ([coverage](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/feature/2026/07/22/in-the-worlds-youngest-nation-how-do-you-prove-you-exist), [study](https://statelessnessalliance.org/online-marketplace/strengthening-civil-registration-and-ending-statelessness-in-south-sudan)).<br><br>Making death registration compulsory and centralising issuance are both enforcement designs, and neither the register nor the statute behind it exists yet.</details> | Advanced |
| Sudan | Issuance [resumed in two eastern states](https://www.sudanakhbar.com/1754489) over the window, while [Darfur's offices remain shut](https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cr7kldz4djno) and children go unregistered. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Port Sudan resumed national identity card issuance on 27 December 2025 after more than two years, with a digital identity project launched alongside it ([resumption](https://www.alnilin.com/13423167.htm)).<br><br>Kassala followed on 15 February 2026, having restored vital records and fingerprint data, with extended hours and dedicated counters planned ([resumption](https://www.sudanakhbar.com/1754489)).<br><br>In Gezira the state governor launched service in Umm al-Qura locality and the registry director pledged six locality centres for rural residents ([launch](https://alnawrs.com/209412)).<br><br>A lawyer reports Darfur's offices shut, leaving thousands of children without birth certificates or a national number ([account](https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cr7kldz4djno)).</details> | Mixed, resuming in the east and closed in Darfur |
| Tanzania | [Under-five birth registration](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202608/tanzania-pushes-crvs-id-integration-as-birth-registration-becomes-a-dpi-layer) is quoted at 65 per cent and 68 per cent a week apart by the same minister, which is the clearest demonstration that the figure is quoted rather than published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The base holds several differently-based estimates and does not reconcile them to one series; the two newest are a week apart from the same minister ([coverage](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202608/tanzania-pushes-crvs-id-integration-as-birth-registration-becomes-a-dpi-layer), [partner](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202606/unicef-seeks-partner-to-expand-birth-registration-legal-identity-in-tanzania), [framework](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202601/tanzania-working-on-legislative-framework-for-infant-id-registration), [foundation](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202608/birth-registration-emerges-as-foundation-for-digital-public-infrastructure)).<br><br>An upgraded platform reports issuing birth certificates within 48 hours, and the government has stated an intention to integrate the identity and registration agencies ([certificates](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202601/tanzania-working-on-legislative-framework-for-infant-id-registration)).<br><br>A national partner was sought for a birth registration programme running August 2026 to August 2027, starting inside the next reporting month ([programme](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202606/unicef-seeks-partner-to-expand-birth-registration-legal-identity-in-tanzania)).</details> | Mixed, birth registration rising on estimates the base cannot reconcile to one series |
| Togo | A [second birth-registration catch-up campaign](https://atop.tg/identite-juridique-la-deuxieme-campagne-nationale-de-rattrapage-des-enregistrements-de-naissances-lancee/) was launched in July 2026, and an [online civil-status certificate service](https://www.republiquetogolaise.com/tic/1612-11418-digitalisation-les-demandes-d-actes-d-etat-civil-desormais-possibles-en-ligne) is still a pilot in five communes. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The report of the second campaign gives two incompatible reach figures — 6,461 on the ministry's account against nearly 7,400 on the supporting organisation's — unreconciled ([campaign](https://atop.tg/identite-juridique-la-deuxieme-campagne-nationale-de-rattrapage-des-enregistrements-de-naissances-lancee/)).<br><br>The online certificate service launched in five pilot communes in December 2025 and remains a pilot on the record held, with no dated extension and no completion date for national rollout ([service](https://www.republiquetogolaise.com/tic/1612-11418-digitalisation-les-demandes-d-actes-d-etat-civil-desormais-possibles-en-ligne), [platform](https://www.togofirst.com/en/public-services/1712-17829-togo-expands-public-service-digitisation-with-online-civil-registry-platform)).<br><br>The 2009 civil registration law under which both operate is under review, with proposals due before the end of 2026.</details> | Advanced |
| Tunisia | A [digital birth declaration platform](https://www.alchourouk.com/article/%D9%88%D8%B2%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D8%AD%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%B9-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%AA%D9%86%D9%81%D9%8A%D8%B0-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%AC%D8%B1%D8%A8%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84%D9%89-%D9%84%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B8%D9%88%D9%85%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%B5%D8%B1%D9%8A%D8%AD-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85%D9%8A-%D8%A8%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%88%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%AA) is running at one hospital, with 202 births registered. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The interior ministry launched remote issuance of birth certificates through the national services platform ([launch](https://www.lapresse.tn/2026/08/31/extrait-de-naissance-voici-comment-lobtenir-desormais-a-distance/)), with a remote translation service alongside it ([report](https://www.radionationale.tn/article/6a957fdc07af2054405fddb7/%D9%88%D8%B2%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%AE%D9%84%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%A5%D8%B7%D9%84%D8%A7%D9%82-%D8%AE%D8%AF%D9%85%D8%A9-%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%AC%D8%AF%D9%8A%D8%AF%D8%A9-%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%B1%D8%AC%D9%85%D8%A9-%D9%85%D8%B6%D9%85%D9%88%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%88%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%AF%D8%A9-%D8%B9%D9%86-%D8%A8%D9%8F%D8%B9%D8%AF)).<br><br>It is the first national platform of its kind and is running at a single hospital ([platform](https://www.alchourouk.com/article/%D9%88%D8%B2%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D8%AD%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%B9-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%AA%D9%86%D9%81%D9%8A%D8%B0-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%AC%D8%B1%D8%A8%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84%D9%89-%D9%84%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B8%D9%88%D9%85%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%B5%D8%B1%D9%8A%D8%AD-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85%D9%8A-%D8%A8%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%88%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%AA), [pilot](https://www.assabahnews.tn/ar/%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%AE%D8%A8%D8%A7%D8%B1/%D9%88%D8%B7%D9%86%D9%8A%D8%A9/151109)).<br><br>Two hundred and two births is a pilot volume, and no rollout plan, second site or completion date is published. The country hosted the Arab conference of civil-status directors in the same month.</details> | Advanced |
| Uganda | A [birth registration drive](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/news/nira-intensifies-birth-registration-drive-lau-NV_234727_062026) announced community drives and a fee schedule, and no birth-registration coverage figure has been published since 2016. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The drive and fee schedule were announced in June 2026 ([drive](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/news/nira-intensifies-birth-registration-drive-lau-NV_234727_062026), [notification](https://crvssystems.ca/sites/default/files/assets/images/CRVS_Webinar_Uganda_e_WEB.pdf)).<br><br>Ten years without a published coverage figure means the drive cannot be measured against anything.<br><br>Marriage registration transferred from the registration bureau to the identity authority in April 2025, three months before the window opened ([marriage](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/family/ursb-hands-over-marriage-registration-role-to-NV_209508_032026)).</details> | Advanced |
| Zambia | The [birth registration rate](https://znbc.co.zm/?p=10211) is an external agency figure cited by the Registrar General, so a 50 per cent target for end-2026 has no measured mid-point on file. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The rate is cited rather than measured domestically ([rate](https://znbc.co.zm/?p=10211), [target](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202506/zambia-says-using-multiple-strategies-to-meet-50-percent-birth-registration-goal-by-2026)).<br><br>Civil registration runs on a three-pillar strategy stated at a continental civil registration day ([registration](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202508/zambia-sees-civil-registration-as-bedrock-of-inclusive-digital-transformation)), and a modernisation programme was launched on the same three pillars ([programme](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202508/zambia-sees-civil-registration-as-bedrock-of-inclusive-digital-transformation)).<br><br>A target with no measured mid-point, four months from its date, is the position.</details> | No change |
| Zimbabwe | The civil register published a [2023-2024 vital statistics report](https://www.zimstat.co.zw/wp-content/uploads/demography/vital/2023_2024_Vital_Statistics_Report.pdf), and computerisation now covers all ten provinces with ten rural sub-offices being equipped. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statistics agency and the civil registry department published a 79-page vital statistics report for 2023-2024, reporting registered births, deaths, marriages and other vital events by province, age and sex ([report](https://www.zimstat.co.zw/wp-content/uploads/demography/vital/2023_2024_Vital_Statistics_Report.pdf)).<br><br>All ten provinces and 63 districts are computerised, reported to a meeting of African registrars general in July 2026 ([registrars](https://www.zbcnews.co.zw/african-registrars-look-to-replicate-zimbabwes-e-passport-success/)).<br><br>Ten decentralised sub-offices across eight districts are being computerised, digitalised and solar-powered under a China-funded, UNICEF-managed project, extending the register below district level for the first time on this base ([project](https://humanitarianpost.co.zw/unicef-boosts-zims-civil-registry-efficiency-in-eight-districts/)).<br><br>The statute behind all of it is unchanged since 2020 ([Act](https://zimlii.org/akn/zw/act/1986/11/eng%402020-03-06)). No completeness or timeliness rate for death registration is held.</details> | Advanced |

## Address register

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | The national address register is [reaching one province at a time](https://www.icilinfo.dz/mostaganem-lancement-des-ateliers-de-generalisation-du-referentiel-national-dadressage-en-faveur-de-dairas-et-communes/), on a [2022 procedures guide](https://dtpsetif.bevrd.dz/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/Guide_Procdures_dadressage_NA_00-08_2022.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The standard is a [national addressing procedures guide of June 2022 setting a data model and procedural standard](https://dtpsetif.bevrd.dz/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/Guide_Procdures_dadressage_NA_00-08_2022.pdf) - not just a registry fact but a national data model.<br><br>2026-05-09 - [generalisation was launched in one southern province](https://lavoiedalgerie.dz/timimoun-lancement-de-la-generalisation-du-referentiel-national-dadressage/2026/09/00/).<br><br>2026-06-26 - [a two-day workshop series for district officials ran in a northern province, convened by the national addressing commission](https://www.icilinfo.dz/mostaganem-lancement-des-ateliers-de-generalisation-du-referentiel-national-dadressage-en-faveur-de-dairas-et-communes/).<br><br>Two provinces in two months is the observed pace, and no coverage figure, address count or completion date is published against a country of 58.</details> | Advanced |
| Benin | A [harmonised national addressing strategy is in preparation, consulted on 21 May 2026](https://fr.linkedin.com/posts/ministere-cadre-vie-transports-ddurable_adressage-des-voies-et-des-portes-au-b%C3%A9nin-activity-7465035010115293184-I-KB). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The environment and transport ministry convened the sector's principal actors on 21 May 2026 for a strategic consultation on street and door addressing, under the implementation of a national strategy](https://fr.linkedin.com/posts/ministere-cadre-vie-transports-ddurable_adressage-des-voies-et-des-portes-au-b%C3%A9nin-activity-7465035010115293184-I-KB), [the Council of Ministers having taken up addressing in April 2026](https://sgg.gouv.bj/cm/2026-04-22/).<br><br>No adopted strategy, addressed-area coverage figure or address count is held, and nothing connects the work to the population register.</details> | Advanced |
| Botswana | [No national addressing system exists](https://www.ajol.info/index.php/sajg/article/view/272275); the addressing component of the land project was never implemented nationally. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2024-06-20 — [a case study on rural addressing records that no national addressing system is in place and that the addressing component of the land administration project was never implemented nationally](https://www.ajol.info/index.php/sajg/article/view/272275).<br><br>2026-03-04 — [a four-year modernisation of the land information and geospatial systems was funded in the lands budget](https://dailynews.gov.bw/news-detail/90382), the nearest thing to address infrastructure now moving.<br><br>The absence was searched on 31 August 2026 and nothing settling it was found.</details> | No change |
| Burkina Faso | No national digital address register exists; [a municipal programme had numbered streets and doors in three of the capital's twelve arrondissements by April 2025](https://burkina24.com/2025/04/02/ouagadougou-modernise-son-adressage-pour-faciliter-la-localisation/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[There is no national digital address register, and an externally financed municipal programme had numbered streets and doors in three of the capital's twelve arrondissements by April 2025](https://burkina24.com/2025/04/02/ouagadougou-modernise-son-adressage-pour-faciliter-la-localisation/). Nothing has moved since, and a national addressing instrument, or a register covering more than one commune, would settle it.<br><br>The absence is load-bearing. The land and cadastre platform, the social register and the enrolment committees reported elsewhere all locate people and property, and none of them can reference a national address. The statistics institute's locality information system, reported under administrative statistics, is the nearest substitute and is still in validation.</details> | No change |
| Burundi | An urban property-owner census [began building a geo-referenced register with a unique identifier per parcel](https://abpinfo.bi/2026/07/16/lancement-officiel-du-recensement-des-proprietaires-des-proprietes-ou-parcelles-dans-les-centres-urbains-du-burundi/), over [postal address formats](https://www.upu.int/UPU/media/upu/PostalEntitiesFiles/addressingUnit/bdiEn.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[An international addressing country file gives the national postal address formats](https://www.upu.int/UPU/media/upu/PostalEntitiesFiles/addressingUnit/bdiEn.pdf) — the whole of what the base held on addressing.<br><br>2026-07-14 — [an urban property-owner census was launched to build a digitised, geo-referenced land register with a unique identifier per parcel, interoperable with other national registers and feeding a web mapping service and future digital land titles](https://abpinfo.bi/2026/07/16/lancement-officiel-du-recensement-des-proprietaires-des-proprietes-ou-parcelles-dans-les-centres-urbains-du-burundi/), first phase covering three communes of the capital and three towns, at about 3 billion BIF, with enumeration in August and September 2026.<br><br>It is an address register arriving as a property register, which is a different object: it will number parcels and their owners rather than places, and no plan to extend it beyond the six urban areas is stated.</details> | Advanced |
| Cameroon | A [bank partnership made a certified geolocated postal address a condition of account opening](https://launchbaseafrica.com/2025/06/19/from-15k-to-700k-cameroons-findme-rides-new-bank-deal-to-scale-digital-addressing/), reaching more than 700,000 clients. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[About 15,000 addresses had been generated since a 2024 postal-operator agreement underpinning the national postal location plan](https://launchbaseafrica.com/2025/06/19/from-15k-to-700k-cameroons-findme-rides-new-bank-deal-to-scale-digital-addressing/).<br><br>2025-06-19 - a [bank partnership required every new customer to supply a geolocated postal address certified through the startup's postal-operator tie-up, reaching more than 700,000 clients of that bank, at FCFA 2,000 a certification](https://launchbaseafrica.com/2025/06/19/from-15k-to-700k-cameroons-findme-rides-new-bank-deal-to-scale-digital-addressing/).<br><br>It is a privately run register entering the identity-verification stack without a legislative act. No address count since, no coverage figure, no data-controller arrangement and no obligation on the operator to serve anyone who will not pay the fee are on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Central African Republic | The [addressing convention is post-office-box based, with no street numbering, postcode or national physical address register](https://www.upu.int/UPU/media/upu/PostalEntitiesFiles/addressingUnit/cafEn.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The international postal reference for the country [records the operative addressing convention as post-office-box based, with no street numbering, no postcode and no national physical address register](https://www.upu.int/UPU/media/upu/PostalEntitiesFiles/addressingUnit/cafEn.pdf).<br><br>That is a dated, cited statement of absence rather than a gap in the record, and nothing since 2022 changes it. No addressing project, digital address system or geocoding initiative appears anywhere on this ledger - which bears on everything that would need to reach a household: registration, transfers and service delivery alike.</details> | No change, a first stated position with no earlier account behind it |
| Chad | There is no national address register; [N'Djamena's addressing, officialised in 2016](https://www.revueakofena.com/n020_vol-1_05/), is still incomplete. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The only documented Chadian street-addressing programme ran in the capital from 2013 to 2016 at 700,000 euros co-financed by the European Union, the municipal association and the city, defining addressing as city mapping, street codification, enumeration surveys and a simple information system feeding the local tax base ([programme](https://www.aimf.asso.fr/actions/ndjamena-mobilisation-des-recettes-et-modernisation-de-la-gestion-financiere/)).<br><br>A 2026 study finds numbering begun in 1990 across the first five arrondissements and officialised in 2016, with the administrative system still incomplete, untouched in the old quarters and in some newly planned ones, so official and popular addressing coexist ([study](https://www.revueakofena.com/n020_vol-1_05/)).<br><br>A National Addressing System instrument exists in no form the base holds, and the 2026 finding rests on a survey of sixty respondents.</details> | No change |
| Comoros | An [addressing and directory process was launched in December 2022 for completion by end-2023](https://www.al-fajrquotidien.com/amenagement-du-territoire-et-urbanisme-un-processus-dadressage-et-annuaire-officiellement-lance/); no completion is on record. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The programme was [launched to assign a street address and a directory entry to every household and business across the three islands, with a cadastral and urban-planning payoff, and a stated completion target of end-2023](https://www.al-fajrquotidien.com/amenagement-du-territoire-et-urbanisme-un-processus-dadressage-et-annuaire-officiellement-lance/).<br><br>That target passed. No second record of the programme exists in the base, no coverage figure has been published, and no authority is named as holding the resulting register.<br><br>What did move is adjacent and separate: the [cartographic unit's fourteen-theme national geospatial database was validated at a restitution workshop in January 2026](https://fr.linkedin.com/posts/nair-aboubacar-2265a092_atelier-de-restitution-sur-la-cartographie-activity-7423252816028184576-w35t). A themed map layer is not a street-address register, and nothing on file connects the two.</details> | Stalled, the programme's own completion target passed three years ago with nothing recorded since |
| Congo | The addressing project [has sat at the regulator for two years untransferred to the postal operator](https://www.adiac-congo.com/content/numeriquegouvernance-frederic-malik-nguema-nze-fait-de-larpce-son-fer-de-lance-170823), with no deployment on file. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A [postal codification decree was adopted in council of ministers in July 2025, presented as bringing the country to international postal standards](https://sgg.cg/ccm/compte-rendu-cmd-2025-07-23.pdf).<br><br>2026-07-14 - the minister recorded that [the regulator has run the addressing project for two years and the ministry has not taken it up for deployment at the postal operator, so the project is still held at the regulator with no deployment, code range or address database on file](https://www.adiac-congo.com/content/numeriquegouvernance-frederic-malik-nguema-nze-fait-de-larpce-son-fer-de-lance-170823). His own framing is that without the handover the work done has not had the expected impact.</details> | Stalled |
| Cote d'Ivoire | A [digital post box became compulsory from 1 March 2026 for all individuals and businesses with postal addresses](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202601/cote-divoire-digitizes-postal-services-with-mobile-app-launch), tied to the identification number. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-01-20 - the [digital post box was made obligatory from 1 March 2026 for all individuals and businesses with postal addresses and tied to the identification number, at annual fees from FCFA 2,000 for an individual to 50,000 for a large business](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202601/cote-divoire-digitizes-postal-services-with-mobile-app-launch).<br><br>No count of boxes assigned is published and no account of enforcement after the March deadline is on file. A compulsory paid credential with no published take-up, no exemption regime and no stated legal instrument behind the obligation is the shape of the evidence here; it is also the nearest thing the country has to an address register.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | A [national locality directory with resident counts was published in November 2025](https://firebasestorage.googleapis.com/v0/b/instad-dj.appspot.com/o/RGPH%2F0%2FTome%2020_Localit%C3%A9s%20et%20Populations%20r%C3%A9sidentes%20de%20Djibouti_18112025.pdf?alt=media&token=4d122ebf-70f1-4de4-8d13-0b20748795f5); no civic address register exists. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-11-18 - the statistics institute [published a directory of localities and resident populations from the third census, covering every region, commune, district, quarter, village and hamlet](https://firebasestorage.googleapis.com/v0/b/instad-dj.appspot.com/o/RGPH%2F0%2FTome%2020_Localit%C3%A9s%20et%20Populations%20r%C3%A9sidentes%20de%20Djibouti_18112025.pdf?alt=media&token=4d122ebf-70f1-4de4-8d13-0b20748795f5) - a georeferenced locality reference rather than a street-address register.<br><br>2026-01 - a [territorial portal was launched combining the census demographic data with national boundary layers](https://www.geohorn.org/portail_djibouti/).<br><br>The addressing service a resident can actually use is commercial and postal: a [three-word geocoding grid offered by the post office](https://www.laposte.dj/). No addressing law, no naming authority and no register of addresses appears on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Egypt | A [unified real estate identifier database was established by law](https://projects.sharkia.gov.eg/legislations/file/183) on a unified base map, with [a first platform phase live](https://www.dailynewsegypt.com/2025/12/18/property-egypt-platform-launched-to-drive-foreign-currency-inflows/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-06-17 - a law [establishes a unified non-repeating national identifier database for all real estate - land, buildings and installations - built on a unified base map](https://projects.sharkia.gov.eg/legislations/file/183). It is the closest thing to a national address register the base holds.<br><br>2025-09-06 - the statistics agency [chaired a meeting on the law, reviewed the explanatory memorandum and the draft executive regulations it had itself prepared, and formed a subcommittee](https://gate.ahram.org.eg/News/5280444.aspx).<br><br>2025-12-18 - the platform's first phase carries [more than 3,000 units in one new city, all issued unique real estate identification numbers](https://www.dailynewsegypt.com/2025/12/18/property-egypt-platform-launched-to-drive-foreign-currency-inflows/).</details> | Advanced |
| Equatorial Guinea | No street coding exists: the responsible minister [told parliament in March 2026](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/comparecencia_del_ministro_delegado_del_interior_y_administracion_local) that its absence undermines the cadastre. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The postal union's [filed address format for the country carries no postcode field in any delivery mode](https://www.upu.int/UPU/media/upu/PostalEntitiesFiles/addressingUnit/gnqEn.pdf), which is the formal position.<br><br>The minister responsible put the practical one on the record: [colonial-era plans and the absence of street coding undermine both the cadastre and the address system](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/comparecencia_del_ministro_delegado_del_interior_y_administracion_local). No addressing programme has been announced against that statement.</details> | No change |
| Eritrea | Eritrea does not appear on the postal union's list of countries operating a postcode system ([reference](https://www.upu.int/UPU/media/upu/documents/PostCode/General-Addressing-Issues.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Universal Postal Union's addressing reference, dated August 2026, does not list Eritrea among the countries that require or use a postal-code system, establishing that no national postcode system exists ([reference](https://www.upu.int/UPU/media/upu/documents/PostCode/General-Addressing-Issues.pdf)).<br><br>The document does not itself state whether any other national addressing scheme operates, and the base holds nothing else on the question — no street-naming programme, no digital addressing initiative and no geocoding of the population registers.</details> | No change |
| Eswatini | [National addressing standards](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/eswatini-standards-authority_improvingtheworldwelivein-eswasa-nationaladdressing-activity-7484866707195322368-8UQ2) launched in July 2026 with two municipal pilots; no address register exists yet. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Eswatini National Addressing Standards, the ISO 19160 parts 1 and 2 domesticated as SZNS, launched on 20 July 2026 by the standards authority with the communications regulator and the ICT and housing ministries ([launch](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/eswatini-standards-authority_improvingtheworldwelivein-eswasa-nationaladdressing-activity-7484866707195322368-8UQ2)).<br><br>Mbabane is leading implementation of a national street addressing and house numbering programme, framed as a smart-city initiative to make deliveries, emergency response and navigation work ([Mbabane](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/municipal-council-of-mbabane_mbabanemycity-mbabanesmartcity-activity-7485998259241594880-9ytq)), and Ezulwini is among the pilot municipalities ([Ezulwini](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/ezulwini-municipality-discover-happiness-0812b9250_smartezulwini-nationaladdressingstandards-activity-7484859620780466176-AWHI)).<br><br>What exists is a standard and two pilots. No address register, no count of addressed properties and no national coverage figure is held, and the standard is six weeks old at the close of this window.</details> | Advanced |
| Ethiopia | Nationwide expansion of the digital address system to all urban centres was named a priority ([report](https://ena.et/web/eng/w/eng_8039305)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Space Science and Geospatial Institute stated that Digital Ethiopia 2030 makes nationwide expansion of the national digital address system to all urban centres a named priority ([report](https://ena.et/web/eng/w/eng_8039305)).<br><br>The institute's mandate to build and administer geospatial data including a digital addressing system rests on the 2022 regulation establishing it ([regulation](https://justice.gov.et/am/?jet_download=3c0aa75f4052b577ccd15833bf732a736fa7dcaf)).<br><br>The base holds a priority and a mandate, and no count of addresses assigned or urban centres covered.</details> | Advanced |
| Gambia | A [national digital addressing steering committee](https://techafricanews.com/2025/10/13/the-gambia-takes-major-step-toward-a-fully-digital-nation-with-launch-of-ndasc/) was launched in October 2025, on a policy dating from 2023. <details><summary>Full record</summary>No national coverage figure or rollout milestone has been published since ([committee](https://techafricanews.com/2025/10/13/the-gambia-takes-major-step-toward-a-fully-digital-nation-with-launch-of-ndasc/)).<br><br>The only measured addressing anywhere in the base is municipal, which makes a national committee launched two years after its own policy the weaker half of the record.</details> | Advanced |
| Ghana | The [national digital address system is live](https://www.ghanapostgps.com/) and its application added [ride and delivery ordering keyed to an address](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/ghanapostgps/id1296627307) in October 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [system is live and publicly reachable through its own application](https://www.ghanapostgps.com/); the page carries no internal publication date, so the date is proxied to capture.<br><br>2025-10-15 - the application [added ride and delivery ordering keyed to a digital address](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/ghanapostgps/id1296627307) - the first third-party service on this ledger built on the address as an identifier rather than on the map.<br><br>No address count, coverage figure, update cadence or public-sector use of the register appears anywhere on the base, so what is established is that the register exists and is being built on, not how complete or how used it is.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea-Bissau | No addressing system, postcode scheme or street register appears in the base for Guinea-Bissau. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Nothing in the base records a Guinean address register, a national addressing programme or a postcode scheme.<br><br>The geographic reference work that is happening is agricultural and meteorological: [eleven hydrometeorological stations sited across three southern regions](https://meteoguinebissau.gw/documents/185/Relat%C3%B3rio_da_miss%C3%A3o_do_terreno_do_PAR_nas_Regi%C3%B5es_do_Sul__de_17_a_23_de_setemb_Ndb88BK.pdf) and satellite growing-season monitoring produced by international agencies.</details> | No change |
| Kenya | A [national addressing system](https://www.ca.go.ke/index.php/national-addressing-system-kenya) has been under development by the communications regulator since 2023, with nothing on it since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The framework sits under the regulator's numbering and addressing mandate, made under the Kenya Information and Communications (Numbering) Regulations 2010, and provides for the naming and numbering of streets and the numbering of buildings and land parcels so that a parcel or dwelling can be identified and located on the ground ([system](https://www.ca.go.ke/index.php/national-addressing-system-kenya)).<br><br>The regulator's project page is the whole of the held record. It carries no coverage, timetable or completion figure, and nothing after May 2023 is held, so whether any street or parcel has been numbered under it is unestablished.</details> | No change |
| Lesotho | [No state address register exists](https://www.lena.gov.ls/mountain-areas-are-project-based-areas-mahooana/); a [private nationwide platform launched in May 2026](https://www.gedditapp.com/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2011-02-24 — [the land regulations carry the allocation and identification registers](https://lesotholii.org/akn/ls/act/ln/2011/21/eng@2011-02-24), the closest operative instrument to an address register.<br><br>2026-05-08 — [officials proposed a national spatial development infrastructure policy, stating that no spatial-data policy currently exists](https://www.lena.gov.ls/mountain-areas-are-project-based-areas-mahooana/).<br><br>2026-05-22 — [a private nationwide digital-address platform launched](https://www.gedditapp.com/), filling the gap the officials had described a fortnight earlier.<br><br>The addressing layer a state usually owns is in private hands here, and the base holds no arrangement governing that.</details> | Mixed, a private platform where the state has none |
| Liberia | A [national digital postal address rollout](https://elbcnews.com/2026/07/23/commissioner-honnah-reaffirms-liberia-telecom-authoritys-commitment-to-expanding-digital-access/) is stated as begun across all fifteen counties, with no completion date or addressing standard published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statement was made at a technology summit in July 2026 ([rollout](https://elbcnews.com/2026/07/23/commissioner-honnah-reaffirms-liberia-telecom-authoritys-commitment-to-expanding-digital-access/)).<br><br>Digital addressing is one of the eight priority areas of the national digital strategy, and this is the only delivery statement against any of them.</details> | Advanced |
| Libya | [National postal addressing](https://libyaobserver.ly/inbrief/libya-launches-postal-addressing-project-tripoli-district) is in phase one covering 42,000 buildings in one Tripoli district, with no completion figure published since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Phase one covers 42,000 buildings in Hay Al-Andalus, Tripoli, and was under way at the start of 2025 ([phase one](https://libyaobserver.ly/inbrief/libya-launches-postal-addressing-project-tripoli-district)).<br><br>No later count, no extension to other districts and no link to any other register has been published, so the address layer that other systems would draw on does not yet exist beyond one district.</details> | No change |
| Madagascar | [Spatial data infrastructures are not yet implemented](https://iho.int/uploads/user/Inter-Regional%20Coordination/RHC/SAIHC/SAIHC19/SAIHC19-7.12_2023_EN_Madagascar_report.pdf) on the national geographic institute's own account, and no state address register exists. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The institute's own status report states under its ninth item that spatial data infrastructures are not yet implemented, the most recent dated authoritative statement located on the country's addressing and geospatial register infrastructure ([report](https://iho.int/uploads/user/Inter-Regional%20Coordination/RHC/SAIHC/SAIHC19/SAIHC19-7.12_2023_EN_Madagascar_report.pdf)).<br><br>The only in-window addressing development is a community-built mobile layer whose publisher states it is not a government service and that its codes have no legal value ([layer](https://github.com/Xpirix/adiresy-mobile)).<br><br>No addressing standard, no state register and no mandate for one is held. The gap is a dated, searched absence rather than an unexamined one.</details> | No change |
| Malawi | The [National Addressing System Project](https://macra.mw/national-addressing-project/) has had nothing published about it since February 2024, and the absence of any update is itself the finding. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The regulator-coordinated programme would give every property a physical address, spanning a national postcode system, addressing standards, an address database, physical infrastructure and geographic information systems ([project](https://macra.mw/national-addressing-project/)).<br><br>No completion date and no delivery figure were given then, and nothing dated after February 2024 is held, so the position is carried forward rather than re-established.</details> | Stalled |
| Mauritania | [Terms of reference were issued in March 2025](https://mtnima.gov.mr/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/TDR-adressage.pdf) for a geolocated addressing system to be implemented through the postal company, and nothing has followed on the record. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The terms of reference were issued under the regional digital integration programme, presenting the system as favouring the development of commerce and electronic commerce in particular ([terms](https://mtnima.gov.mr/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/TDR-adressage.pdf)).<br><br>Eighteen months on, no award, supplier, coverage, address standard or delivery date is on the record, so what exists is a specification and not a register.</details> | No change |
