| Mauritius | No national address register exists; the [national data strategy](https://dataprotection.govmu.org/Documents/NDS_final.pdf) identifies addressing as a data gap. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The strategy of January 2026 is the instrument that names the gap ([strategy](https://dataprotection.govmu.org/Documents/NDS_final.pdf)).<br><br>The closest work on file is the verifiable-credential wallet's identity attributes, market-sounded in September 2025 ([request](https://mitci.govmu.org/mitci/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/Press-Communique-RFI-from-Potential-suppliers-for-the-Development-of-a-SuperApp-based-on-Verifiable-Digital-Credentials-in-a-Secure-ID-Wallet.pdf)).<br><br>The island's postal addressing is not organised as a state register, and no addressing standard, register or mandate for one is held. The absence is dated and searched rather than unexamined.</details> | No change, a searched absence the data strategy itself names |
| Morocco | From [9 December 2025 the identity card address is the sole legal domicile](https://www.lebrief.ma/ladresse-de-la-cin-devient-le-seul-domicile-legal-a-compter-du-9-decembre-2025-100129381/) for judicial notices. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Addressing is a communal competence under a [2017 decree and organic law 113-14](https://collectivites-territoriales.gov.ma/ar/la-commune), which sets the system but not its legal weight.<br><br>That changed: [from 9 December 2025 the address on the national identity card became the sole legal domicile for judicial notices, ending the proxy arrangement that preceded it](https://www.lebrief.ma/ladresse-de-la-cin-devient-le-seul-domicile-legal-a-compter-du-9-decembre-2025-100129381/). An address on a credential now carries legal consequence, which is a stronger position than a register alone.</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | A [national postal address code](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/new-postal-address-code-across-mozambique-budgeted-at-us33-million/) was piloted at one district in 2020 against a five-year, US$33 million rollout, and nothing on it since is held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The regulator inaugurated a pilot covering 1,500 homes and other premises and more than 1,200 streets at Manhica in July 2020, with Chimoio in preparation and Matola, Beira, Quelimane, Nampula and Nacala to follow over five years ([pilot](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/new-postal-address-code-across-mozambique-budgeted-at-us33-million/)).<br><br>The regulator's feasibility study puts the cost of implementing the code at at least US$33 million, from state funds and cooperation partners ([study](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/new-postal-address-code-across-mozambique-budgeted-at-us33-million/)).<br><br>The base holds nothing on the code after July 2020, so the five-year rollout would have run its course unobserved.</details> | No change |
| Namibia | No address register exists. The [postal code system](https://www.nampost.com.na/postal/postal-codes) covers sorting and delivery only, and a national addressing system is named as a memorandum objective. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The postal operator regrouped its codes from postal administrative regions into political regions, changing the numbering for some towns, and states that this phase covers postal sorting and delivery only and not administrative or geographic addressing, with a physical-address phase still to come ([codes](https://www.nampost.com.na/postal/postal-codes)).<br><br>The communications regulator's chief executive named development of a national addressing system as an objective of an April 2026 memorandum with the statistics agency on data sharing and spatial information management ([memorandum](https://economist.com.na/106279/general-news/communications-regulator-and-nsa-sign-landmark-agreement-to-bolster-national-data-integrity/)); no specification, budget or date follows it.<br><br>The National Digital Strategy launched in August 2025 is the enabling framework ([strategy](https://www.namibian.com.na/govt-launches-new-digital-emergency-telecommunications-plans/)).<br><br>An addressing system is named in a memorandum and absent from every instrument that could fund or mandate it.</details> | No change, a postal code system that says it is not an address register |
| Niger | The only addressing system on file is the [Niamey geoportal](https://modernisation-villes.ne/fr/presentation-de-l-agence) run by the city modernisation agency; there is no national register. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The city modernisation agency was [created by decree in February 2021](https://modernisation-villes.ne/fr/presentation-de-l-agence) and is responsible for Niamey's addressing geoportal, the only addressing system located in the country.<br><br>Nothing in the window moves it, and the base holds no national street or building address register, no coverage figure for a national scheme, and no instrument providing for one.</details> | No change |
| Nigeria | A [national alphanumeric postcode](https://www.vanguardngr.com/2026/08/fg-to-rollout-90000km-fibre-optic-network-tijani/) has a launch date of 1 October 2026, and the [addressing council ratified a framework](https://www.thisdaylive.com/2026/08/25/nac-okays-framework-to-build-nigerias-digitised-modern-addressing-system/) replacing the 2017 policy. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The postcode is explicitly property-level rather than cluster-level, with integration to existing government identity databases to follow; no lawful basis, custodian or data-protection arrangement for that integration is stated ([postcode](https://www.vanguardngr.com/2026/08/fg-to-rollout-90000km-fibre-optic-network-tijani/)).<br><br>The council, chaired by the Vice-President, ratified a preliminary framework naming the science ministry as secretariat and assigning individual verification to the identity commission, business address verification to the corporate registry, postcode administration to the postal service and implementation to states; it would replace a 2017 standard described as obsolete, focused on mail delivery and silent on business address verification ([framework](https://www.thisdaylive.com/2026/08/25/nac-okays-framework-to-build-nigerias-digitised-modern-addressing-system/), [standard](https://nipost.gov.ng/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/NIGERIAN-NATIONAL-ADDRESSING-STANDARD-AND-GUIDELINES.pdf)).<br><br>A state is separately piloting digital house-numbering ([pilot](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/nigeria-lagos-unveils-digital-house-numbering-system-to-power-smart-city-vision)).</details> | Advanced |
| Sao Tome and Principe | There is no enacted national address register; the [postal base bill](https://www2.camara.leg.br/saotomeeprincipe/diarios-da-an/xi-legislatura-22-11-2018/ii-serie-a/8.a-sessao/DAN19-IIS.pdf/at_download/file) referring to a postcode system and an address database was submitted for debate and is not shown in force. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The parliamentary gazette carries the government's postal base bill, which refers to a postal code system and an address database among the postal service obligations ([bill](https://www2.camara.leg.br/saotomeeprincipe/diarios-da-an/xi-legislatura-22-11-2018/ii-serie-a/8.a-sessao/DAN19-IIS.pdf/at_download/file)).<br><br>It is a bill submitted for debate, not an enacted law, and the record consulted does not show it in force ([bill](https://www2.camara.leg.br/saotomeeprincipe/diarios-da-an/xi-legislatura-22-11-2018/ii-serie-a/8.a-sessao/DAN19-IIS.pdf/at_download/file)). Four years on, nothing records its passage.<br><br>The absence bears on more than post: the property registry holds an estimated 162,000 property records ([registry](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099112024053534841/pdf/P16212912eb16e03d183301d6b1e658ec19.pdf)) with no national addressing scheme behind them.</details> | No change |
| Senegal | A [national digital addressing project was relaunched](https://techreviewafrica.com/news/6646/senegal-relaunches-national-digital-addressing-project) at a workshop in August 2026, with no budget, lead implementing agency or pilot geography stated. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The relaunch workshop opened on 5 August 2026 ([relaunch](https://techreviewafrica.com/news/6646/senegal-relaunches-national-digital-addressing-project)).<br><br>The government states the project made conceptual and technical progress in earlier years, and the base holds no position for it before the relaunch — so what is being relaunched cannot be measured against what existed.</details> | Advanced |
| Seychelles | Cabinet approved implementation of a [national addressing and postcode system](https://seychellespostalregulator.com/pages/national-addressing-system) on an international address standard, with the legal framework being drafted. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The standard was adopted and a technical committee is drafting the legal framework with the attorney-general's office ([system](https://seychellespostalregulator.com/pages/national-addressing-system)).<br><br>The source is undated and proxy-dated to 2025, so the status may have moved with nothing later on record. Adopting the international standard before drafting the law is the sequence here, and it is the right one for an addressing system whose value depends on conforming to something.</details> | Advanced |
| Sierra Leone | A [national base map](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/the-directorate-of-science-technology-and-innovation_sierra-leone-advances-development-of-a-national-activity-7490000031215824896-fudt) is in development, phase one covering Western Area Urban; the only addressing scheme in service is [Freetown's use of Plus Codes](https://fcc.gov.sl/property-reform-faq/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>Phase one of the base map covers Western Area Urban District, built on OpenStreetMap with Japanese technical direction, with no cost, timetable, hosting arrangement or data licence stated ([map](https://owlpress-sl.com/dsti-leads-digital-mapping-system-sierra-leone-moves-develop-national-base-map/)); the innovation directorate announced the work in August 2026 ([announcement](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/the-directorate-of-science-technology-and-innovation_sierra-leone-advances-development-of-a-national-activity-7490000031215824896-fudt)).<br><br>Freetown City Council's property reform uses Plus Codes for addressing ([reform](https://fcc.gov.sl/property-reform-faq/)).<br><br>There is no national address register. A base map is the layer under one, not the register itself, and the only operating scheme covers one city.</details> | Advanced |
| South Sudan | ***Not held***, searched 2026-09-06 - the [statutory duty to establish addresses and postal codes](https://mojca.gov.ss/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/National-Communication-Act-24-of-2012.pdf) has stood unused since 2012. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The National Communication Act 2012 requires the Authority to establish a national postal system for addresses and postal codes ([Act](https://mojca.gov.ss/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/National-Communication-Act-24-of-2012.pdf)).<br><br>No address register, postcode scheme or addressing project is recorded under it. Fourteen years of an unexercised statutory duty is the finding, and it bears on every service that would need to reach a household.</details> | No change |
| Sudan | An address register exists only as a [statutory mandate on the regulator](https://tpra.gov.sd/en/regulatory/postal/); no deployed national register was located. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The regulator's own statement of its duties under the Telecommunications and Post (Organization) Act 2018 includes establishing a postal addressing system and managing and regulating it ([mandate](https://tpra.gov.sd/en/regulatory/postal/)).<br><br>That is the most authoritative reachable statement, and it establishes an obligation rather than a system. The Act's own text would not fetch, so the institutional page stands in for it.<br><br>No addressing standard, coverage figure, or deployment record was found for any part of the country.</details> | No change |
| Tanzania | [Twelve million housing addresses](https://ippmedia.com/the-guardian/news/local-news/read/tanzania-registers-12-million-housing-addresses-to-boost-digital-governance-2026-02-09-105732) were registered alongside a continental postal certification, and a [Zanzibar address programme](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202508/tanzania-digital-id-address-system-facilitates-access-to-student-loans-other-services) has assigned more than 450,000 digital addresses. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The base holds that the population rate and the address count are not derivable from one another and must not be charted together ([addresses](https://ippmedia.com/the-guardian/news/local-news/read/tanzania-registers-12-million-housing-addresses-to-boost-digital-governance-2026-02-09-105732), [registration](https://www.thecitizen.co.tz/tanzania/news/national/tanzania-ramps-up-national-physical-address-registration-4685750)).<br><br>The Zanzibar programme has been operational since June 2025 with more than 450,000 digital addresses assigned ([zanzibar](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202508/tanzania-digital-id-address-system-facilitates-access-to-student-loans-other-services)); twelve months with no update is itself the finding, and the first payoff was student finance ([zanzibar](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202508/tanzania-digital-id-address-system-facilitates-access-to-student-loans-other-services)).<br><br>An addressing system whose first evidenced use is disbursing student loans is a useful thing to be able to say about what an address register is for.</details> | Advanced |
| Togo | A [single window for infrastructure mapping](https://www.togofirst.com/fr/gouvernance-economique/0304-18635-togo-le-guichet-unique-de-cartographie-des-infrastructures-officiellement-lance) and a [national satellite-positioning correction network](https://www.togofirst.com/fr/gouvernance-economique/0304-18635-togo-le-guichet-unique-de-cartographie-des-infrastructures-officiellement-lance) were launched together, and [urban addressing in the capital](https://www.republicoftogo.com/toutes-les-rubriques/societe/adressage-urbain-effort-de-modernisation) began modernisation. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The single window and the correction network were both launched at the capital in April 2026. No legal obligation to declare works, no user count and no data-governance or access terms are on file for the window ([window](https://www.togofirst.com/fr/gouvernance-economique/0304-18635-togo-le-guichet-unique-de-cartographie-des-infrastructures-officiellement-lance)), and no cost, operator, subscription model or availability figure for the correction network ([network](https://www.togofirst.com/fr/gouvernance-economique/0304-18635-togo-le-guichet-unique-de-cartographie-des-infrastructures-officiellement-lance)).<br><br>Urban addressing modernisation in the capital began after updating the geographic address database of thirteen communes, with no completion date, address count, budget or operator, from a single source with no update since ([addressing](https://www.republicoftogo.com/toutes-les-rubriques/societe/adressage-urbain-effort-de-modernisation)).<br><br>A positioning correction network is the piece of national infrastructure that makes precise addressing and cadastral work possible, and it is recorded with no operator named.</details> | Advanced |
| Zambia | A [national spatial data infrastructure policy and geoportal launched](https://www.lusakatimes.com/2026/06/16/znsdi-policy-and-zambia-portal-launched/), over a [2017 regulation assigning the postcode and address database](https://www.zambialaws.com/Zambia2017Pdfsi/SI%204%20of%202017.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[A 2017 regulation under the postal services statute assigns the communications authority responsibility to regulate, allocate and manage postcodes and to maintain a national address database, and sets the technical postcode model](https://www.zambialaws.com/Zambia2017Pdfsi/SI%204%20of%202017.pdf). The legal duty has existed for nine years.<br><br>2026-06-16 — [a national spatial data infrastructure policy and a national geoportal were launched, which government describes as ending fragmentation of address and geospatial data across agencies](https://www.lusakatimes.com/2026/06/16/znsdi-policy-and-zambia-portal-launched/).<br><br>A geoportal is not an address register, and the record does not say whether the postcode database the 2017 regulation requires was ever built — which is the question the launch implicitly answers by describing fragmentation.</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | No address register is held. A [national addressing system](http://www.mdpcz.co.zw/external_policies/1682978512National%20ICT%20Policy.pdf) was named in the 2016 ICT policy as a matter for a postal sector policy, and named again in 2026 as a topic for expansion. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The 2016 national ICT policy names a national address system among the matters a postal sector policy would need to cover, alongside market structure, post office infrastructure and universal service ([policy](http://www.mdpcz.co.zw/external_policies/1682978512National%20ICT%20Policy.pdf)).<br><br>Ten years later the regulator's call for chapters for a book on the postal sector lists expanding the national addressing system as a topic, framed around last-mile accessibility and rural inclusion ([call](https://technomag.co.zw/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Call-for-Book-Chapters-2026-copy-1.pdf)).<br><br>Between the two the base holds nothing: no address register, no coverage figure, no implementing instrument, no operator and no postal sector policy. What both ends of the decade record is the same intention, restated.</details> | No change, named in policy for a decade and never described |

## Business register

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | The electronic commercial register recorded [173,651 new registrations against 125,195 struck off in 2025](https://www.echoroukonline.com/%D8%A8%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B1%D9%82%D8%A7%D9%85-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B3%D8%AC%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%AC%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D9%81%D8%AA%D9%88%D8%AD%D8%A9-%D8%AA), and trade platforms went live. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register recorded [173,651 new registrations against 125,195 struck-off registrations in 2025](https://www.echoroukonline.com/%D8%A8%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B1%D9%82%D8%A7%D9%85-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B3%D8%AC%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%AC%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D9%81%D8%AA%D9%88%D8%AD%D8%A9-%D8%AA), with [business registration and stamp duties moved online in December 2025](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/finance/algeria-moves-business-registration-stamp-duties-online).<br><br>2026-03-18 - a [national export-data platform went live](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/algiers-targets-export-growth-with-national-data-platform), [reported in French the same day](https://www.wearetech.africa/fr/fils/actualites/tech/algerie-un-fichier-numerique-national-pour-recenser-les-biens-et-services-destines-a-l-exportation), followed on 24 March by a [national import-tracking system](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/algiers-tightens-trade-controls-with-new-import-tracking-system).<br><br>2026-08-01 - a [services-import platform entered operation as the exclusive route for services-import applications, covering software and licences, cloud services, maintenance and engineering, consultancy and training](https://www.echoroukonline.com/وزارة-التجارة-الخارجية-تطلق-منصة-رقمي), with [no fallback or assisted channel stated](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1_UovYiTOhikpGJ7QiOUNQJsAoYu6xqw_/view).</details> | Advanced |
| Benin | Business registration [runs online as the formalisation route for the informal sector](https://www.ilo.org/media/379846/download), with no registration total published; [a regional credit bureau reports union-wide figures only](https://www.lanation.bj/economie/1er-cafe-des-donnees-du-bic-uemoa-a-cotonou-la-qualite-des-informations-au-coeur-des-travaux). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The business registration platform registers economic units online from desktop and mobile, as the formalisation route for the informal sector](https://www.ilo.org/media/379846/download). The account held is a 2021 case study and carries no registration total; nothing later is on file, so the position is unchanged and unmeasured.<br><br>2026-08-13 — beside it, [the regional credit information bureau operates across all eight union states with 321 data providers, about 48 million contracts and more than 20 million active borrowers, and at its first Cotonou data forum the finance ministry framed data quality as the route to cutting credit-approval turnaround to 48 hours](https://www.lanation.bj/economie/1er-cafe-des-donnees-du-bic-uemoa-a-cotonou-la-qualite-des-informations-au-coeur-des-travaux). Those figures are the operator's own and union-wide: no Benin coverage, dispute or data-protection statement is held.</details> | No change |
| Botswana | The enterprise agency [ran nationwide consultations to March-May 2026 and is lobbying for mandatory small-business registration](https://www.mmegi.bw/business/lea-embarks-on-nationwide-smme-registration/news); no database is in build. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-04-29 — [nationwide consultations ran from March to May 2026 with the enterprise agency lobbying for mandatory registration of small and medium enterprises into a national database](https://www.mmegi.bw/business/lea-embarks-on-nationwide-smme-registration/news). It is proposed only: no instrument, no penalties intended and no database in build.<br><br>Nothing else on this ledger touches business registration, so the country's record here is a consultation and an intention. The comparison worth making is with the taxi financing product reported under payment uptake, where formalisation is being achieved by a bank offering credit against a transaction history rather than by a register compelling registration.</details> | Advanced, a proposal with no instrument behind it |
| Burkina Faso | A compulsory estate-agency register [launched in July 2026, applied for and tracked wholly online with a queryable public register](https://digitalmagazine.bf/2026/07/23/burkina-faso-lancement-officiel-de-la-plateforme-de-delivrance-de-la-carte-nationale-de-demarcheur-pour-le-bail-dhabitation-prive/) and commissions capped. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-21 — [a compulsory national card for property brokers launched under an April 2026 interministerial order, applied for and tracked wholly online, with a legally recognised virtual card issued on validation, a register the public can query, and commissions capped](https://digitalmagazine.bf/2026/07/23/burkina-faso-lancement-officiel-de-la-plateforme-de-delivrance-de-la-carte-nationale-de-demarcheur-pour-le-bail-dhabitation-prive/). Before it, brokering private residential leases required no licence and no register.<br><br>No count of cards issued or applications received is published, so the register's coverage of the trade is unknown a month after launch.<br><br>It is the clearest example on this ledger of digitalisation used to formalise an informal trade, and it sits against [a business census finding that 96.5% of the country's economic units operate informally](https://www.actualite.bf/recensement-general-des-entreprises-au-burkina-faso-389-172-unites-economiques-dominees-par-des-nationaux-selon-une-etude-de-linsd/).</details> | Advanced |
| Burundi | The commercial register [is established in the 2015 code](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/bur223755.pdf), and the historic manual stock [is being converted onto a digital platform](https://investburundi.bi/avis-de-prolongation-des-delais-de-reenregistrement-des-personnes-physiques-et-morales-enregistrees-avant-la-mise-en-place-de-la-plateforme-digitale-dimmatriculation/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Title V of the commercial code establishes the registre de commerce et des societes and the tax identification register and names the holding agency](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/bur223755.pdf) — the statutory basis, in force since 2015.<br><br>2026-06 — [the development agency extended the window for persons and companies recorded under the old manual register to re-register free of charge on the digital platform](https://investburundi.bi/avis-de-prolongation-des-delais-de-reenregistrement-des-personnes-physiques-et-morales-enregistrees-avant-la-mise-en-place-de-la-plateforme-digitale-dimmatriculation/). That operation is what converts a historic paper stock into a digital register, and an extension is what happens when it is going slowly; no count of firms re-registered, no total on the old register and no closing date for the manual one is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Cape Verde | A [company-lifecycle platform entered beta with a pilot user group in March 2026](https://expressodasilhas.cv/pais/2026/03/20/plataforma-empresa-online-reforca-digitalizacao-dos-servicos-publicos-em-cabo-verde/101997). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-03-20 - a [beta platform for online company-lifecycle procedures was presented at a socialisation ceremony in Mindelo, developed by a private supplier with the registry and notary directorate and resting on interoperability between public systems](https://expressodasilhas.cv/pais/2026/03/20/plataforma-empresa-online-reforca-digitalizacao-dos-servicos-publicos-em-cabo-verde/101997).<br><br>No user count, cost or full-launch date is published, and the developer rather than the state describes the interoperability design - which matters because the standards instrument that would govern it was announced for 2024 and never adopted.</details> | Advanced |
| Central African Republic | The single-window agency states [complete digitalisation of registration procedures](https://gufe-rca.org/a-propos/notre-histoire/) and [opened a third territorial branch](https://gufe-rca.org/antenne-gufe-berberati-operationnelle/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The agency's own history states a 2025 milestone of [complete digitalisation of business-registration procedures, including online pre-registration and electronic handling](https://gufe-rca.org/a-propos/notre-histoire/).<br><br>2026-02-02 - it [opened a third territorial branch, after the capital and one other town, extending on-the-spot company registration, modification and dissolution beyond the capital](https://gufe-rca.org/antenne-gufe-berberati-operationnelle/).<br><br>Both accounts are the agency's own. No registration volume, processing time, register size or online-versus-counter split is published, so what is established is that the procedure is digital and the counters are spreading, not what either delivers.</details> | Advanced |
| Chad | The business register was [computerised in February 2020](https://www.ohada.org/deploiement-du-rccm-informatise-au-tchad/); the one-stop shop's digitalisation was [still in technical review](https://lendjampost.com/creation-dentreprise-au-tchad-lanie-mise-sur-le-numerique-pour-simplifier-les-demarches/) in 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The regional business-law organisation handed Chad the integrated local-registers and national-file software and equipment for four local sites and the national file on 28 February 2020, World Bank-financed ([handover](https://www.ohada.org/deploiement-du-rccm-informatise-au-tchad/)).<br><br>A technical workshop on 18 February 2026 validated the digitalisation of the one-stop shop for company creation, explicitly a review rather than a deployment ([workshop](https://lendjampost.com/creation-dentreprise-au-tchad-lanie-mise-sur-le-numerique-pour-simplifier-les-demarches/)).<br><br>In August 2026 the commerce ministry restated what the register does not confer: registration alone never authorises a regulated activity, and naming an activity in the statutes or the extract does not replace a licence ([communique](https://www.alwihdainfo.com/tchad-le-gouvernement-rappelle-linterdiction-des-activites-regaliennes-pour-le-secteur-prive/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Comoros | The [investment agency is deploying the operational phase of a platform to move business creation online](https://fr.linkedin.com/posts/anpi-comores_anpicomores-transformationnum%C3%A9rique-cnuced-activity-7476580734892367872-44ph), targeting full operation by end-2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The standing register is a [public online business directory, filterable by island, sector, year, registration number and city](https://www.investcomoros.net/annuaire-des-entreprises/); its results render in the browser, so no total record count or last-update date is visible on it.<br><br>2026-06 - the agency, [with a United Nations trade experts' mission and the economy minister, discussed deploying the operational phase of a European Union-financed platform to move business-creation and investment procedures online, adding artificial-intelligence features to simplify the user path; the minister stated an objective of a fully operational system by the end of 2026](https://fr.linkedin.com/posts/anpi-comores_anpicomores-transformationnum%C3%A9rique-cnuced-activity-7476580734892367872-44ph).<br><br>The agency's own post is the only account of the platform in the base, and no procurement, supplier or budget is named.</details> | Advanced |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The [register runs on a three-tier file structure with electronic filing carrying the force of paper](https://tribunalcommerceabidjan.ci/fonctionnement_rccm) and [recorded 26,948 creations in 2025](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2640103/economie/investissements-en-cote-divoire-26-948-entreprises-creees-en-2025-soit-une-hausse-de-6-). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register runs on the regional uniform act's local, national and regional file structure. A [2021 decree designated the commercial court as the national file, obliging every jurisdiction to transmit its files in paper and digital form and the national file to transmit onward to the regional file; electronic filing and transmission carry the same legal force as paper, applications run through two online portals, and issued acts carry a QR code for verification](https://tribunalcommerceabidjan.ci/fonctionnement_rccm).<br><br>2026-02-19 - the investment promotion centre published the year's throughput: [26,948 enterprises created in 2025, up 6% on 2024, alongside 154 approved investment projects and FCFA 5,108bn of stated investment intentions](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2640103/economie/investissements-en-cote-divoire-26-948-entreprises-creees-en-2025-soit-une-hausse-de-6-).<br><br>2026-04-17 - the trade ministry [validated the business-process designs for a single window intended to centralise internal-trade and industry services and take administrative acts off paper](https://www.commerce.gouv.ci/index.php/actualite/236); no launch date, service list or cost is published for it.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | An [online company creation platform was launched by the Prime Minister on 26 January 2026](https://www.lanation.dj/djibouti-lance-sa-plateforme-de-creation-en-ligne-des-entreprises/), completing formation without attendance. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The platform did not exist at the window's opening. 2026-01-26 - it was [launched by the Prime Minister and is in service, with company formation completed without attendance](https://www.lanation.dj/djibouti-lance-sa-plateforme-de-creation-en-ligne-des-entreprises/).<br><br>No registration count through the platform is published. The [company-creation and survival figures that accompany the announcement are the investment agency's own study and cover the one-stop counter rather than the platform](https://www.lanation.dj/djibouti-lance-sa-plateforme-de-creation-en-ligne-des-entreprises/), so nothing on file measures what the online route has actually done.</details> | Advanced |
| DR Congo | A [single-window portal and application now issue sole-trader attestations in under 48 hours](https://actualite.cd/2026/07/28/formalisation-des-entreprises-en-rdc-daniel-mukoko-samba-mise-sur-le-numerique-pour), but full online company creation is not possible. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-28 - a ministerial inspection presented a [portal that proposes a legal form and tracks a file in real time and an application that issues sole-trader attestations in under 48 hours, while full online company creation remains impossible, the chain still spanning the revenue directorate, a collecting bank, the notariat and verification](https://actualite.cd/2026/07/28/formalisation-des-entreprises-en-rdc-daniel-mukoko-samba-mise-sur-le-numerique-pour). The 48-hour figure describes the attestation segment, not the registration process.<br><br>2026-08-07 - a financial-sector package [announced a modernised credit-risk registry and an electronic movable-collateral registry to be built with the central bank](https://acp.cd/business/rdc-la-banque-mondiale-annonce-des-reformes-pour-faciliter-le-financement-des-pme/), with no instrument, custodian, timetable or access rule stated, and no data-protection provision reported for the credit file.</details> | Advanced |
| Egypt | A [single investment window offers 460 licences from 41 issuing bodies](https://gate.ahram.org.eg/News/5482679.aspx), and a [state-companies platform is being built](https://gate.ahram.org.eg/News/5849412.aspx). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The base carries no position on either at the window's opening. The investment platform is [a single electronic window offering 460 licences, approvals and permits from 41 government issuing bodies, with electronic fee payment, real-time tracking and licences issued within 20 working days where documents are complete](https://gate.ahram.org.eg/News/5482679.aspx); no count of licences actually issued through it, and no measurement of the 20-day undertaking, is held.<br><br>A [platform to enumerate and classify every company the state owns or holds a stake in is under protocol for hosting, secure storage and compute for its artificial-intelligence applications, which analyse and monitor the financial and operating performance of the state's corporate portfolio](https://gate.ahram.org.eg/News/5849412.aspx); no value, term, data-governance instrument, company count or launch date is disclosed, and the host is a listed company in which the state is the dominant shareholder.<br><br>An [economic entities platform is announced as a single gateway for establishing companies and obtaining approvals electronically, with no build stage, launch date, budget or governing instrument stated](https://www.dailynewsegypt.com/2026/08/03/government-develops-digital-integration-to-accelerate-companies-capital-increases-investment-minister/).</details> | Advanced |
| Equatorial Guinea | The single window [reached two provincial capitals during 2025](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/presentacion_de_varios_proyectos_de_leyes_y_balance_de_la_gestion_del_gobierno), and chamber registration is now a condition of state payment. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [founding decree](https://xn--embajadadeguineaecuatorialenespaa-yfd.com/decreto-de-creacion-de-la-ventanilla-unica-empresarial/) folds the notary step, the tax identification number and the treasury into one digitised deed, which remains the country's clearest working data exchange.<br><br>It is spreading and hardening: [extended to the provincial capitals of two mainland provinces during 2025](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/presentacion_de_varios_proyectos_de_leyes_y_balance_de_la_gestion_del_gobierno), and from [1 January 2026 registration with the chambers of commerce is required before any state contract or payment](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/orden_para_impulsar_la_inscripcion_de_los_operadores_economicos_en_las_camaras_oficiales_de_comercio). A [single annual trade licence system opened training in February with a 40 per cent tax cut pitched at informal traders](https://minhacienda-gob.com/acto-de-apertura-oficial-del-seminario-de-formacion-sobre-el-patente-tributario-unico-anual/).</details> | Advanced |
| Eritrea | The statutory business register is still operated manually, with no registration website and clearance through six offices ([assessment](https://www.eritrea-focus.org/2024-investment-climate-statements-eritrea/)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>An investment climate assessment records that Eritrea has no business-registration website and requires manual registration and clearance through six government offices ([assessment](https://www.eritrea-focus.org/2024-investment-climate-statements-eritrea/)).<br><br>The register itself is statutory: a 2002 proclamation requires the trade ministry to establish and maintain a business information register covering trade, manufacturing, services, fisheries and commercial farming, specifying the fields collected, annual reporting obligations, penalties and judicial review ([proclamation](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/eri200613.pdf)).<br><br>A register that exists in law and on paper is a different thing from a digital one, and nothing on the base records the register being computerised or published.</details> | No change |
| Eswatini | Company registration runs online with [incorporation in a day](https://eswatinitradeportal.com/index.php?id=23&r=searchProcedure%2Fview1), and a Bill to replace the 2009 Act and digitise registration is between the two Houses. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Company name search and reservation run online with incorporation in a day ([procedure](https://eswatinitradeportal.com/index.php?id=23&r=searchProcedure%2Fview1)), and a one-stop shop for investors opened in July 2025 offering a trading licence and a taxpayer number in one place.<br><br>The Companies Bill of 2024, which would replace the Companies Act of 2009 and digitise company registration, passed the House of Assembly on 18 September 2025 ([passage](https://www.eswatiniobserver.com/mps-pass-companies-bill-to-ensure-accountability-and-prevent-scandals/)) and the Senate moved on 5 March 2026 to take it at the stage it had reached ([order paper](https://parliament.gov.sz/senate/order_paper/docs/Order%202.2026%20-%2005%20MARCH%202026.pdf)).<br><br>The Bill is not assented or commenced, so the register still runs under a statute of 2009 ([Act](https://eswatinilii.org/akn/sz/act/2009/8/eng@1998-12-01)).<br><br>No count of registered companies, or of registrations completed online, is published at any date.</details> | Advanced |
| Ethiopia | First-time central bank account numbers are now issued to new traders through the customs single window, removing a separate registration step ([service](https://nbe.gov.et/nbe_news/national-bank-of-ethiopia-streamlines-nbe-account-number-issuance-for-new-importers-and-exporters-through-the-customs-electronic-single-window/)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The central bank and the customs commission introduced a digital service issuing first-time account numbers to newly licensed importers and exporters directly through the customs electronic single window, removing a separate registration step and tightening interoperability of trade registration data ([service](https://nbe.gov.et/nbe_news/national-bank-of-ethiopia-streamlines-nbe-account-number-issuance-for-new-importers-and-exporters-through-the-customs-electronic-single-window/)).<br><br>The founding instrument is the 2016 Commercial Registration and Business Licensing Proclamation, which establishes the nationwide central commercial register and trade name register and is carried as in force in the justice ministry's law catalogue ([proclamation](https://justice.gov.et/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/አዋጅ-ቁጥር-980-2008-1.pdf)).<br><br>The base holds no count of registered businesses and no measure of how much of the register is digital.</details> | Advanced |
| Gabon | A [single file of state participations](https://techafricanews.com/2026/01/14/gabon-accelerates-digital-transformation-with-new-public-finance-and-e-government-initiatives) was reported established in January 2026 to centralise data on the state's shareholdings. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It was stated established at the ministerial session of 13 January 2026 ([ministerial session](https://techafricanews.com/2026/01/14/gabon-accelerates-digital-transformation-with-new-public-finance-and-e-government-initiatives)).<br><br>The account is a single secondary one, and no register, scope, publication or operator is held, so nothing establishes what it contains or who keeps it. The base holds no commercial business register at all.</details> | Advanced |
| Gambia | A [digital single-window business registration system](https://thepoint.gm/africa/gambia/headlines/trans-gambia-economic-zone-set-to-create-3400-jobs-as-investor-interest-grows) is being finalised, with more than 350 agricultural enterprises digitally mapped. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The trade minister stated on 4 August 2026 that the system is being finalised, inside a programme reaching more than 4,050 firms ([registration system](https://thepoint.gm/africa/gambia/headlines/trans-gambia-economic-zone-set-to-create-3400-jobs-as-investor-interest-grows)); no launch date is published ([registration system](https://thepoint.gm/africa/gambia/headlines/trans-gambia-economic-zone-set-to-create-3400-jobs-as-investor-interest-grows)).<br><br>The mapped enterprises are a sector programme's output rather than the register's, so what exists is a mapping exercise and a system said to be near completion.</details> | Advanced |
| Ghana | A [business registration platform](https://amchamghana.org/2026/07/23/amcham-ghana-engages-registrar-general-on-digital-transformation-and-business-registration-reforms/) is in pilot for end-to-end filing with no physical submission, integrating with national identification. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It covers name search to certification, and a companion beneficial-ownership verification service pilots with banks on a subscription basis ([platform](https://amchamghana.org/2026/07/23/amcham-ghana-engages-registrar-general-on-digital-transformation-and-business-registration-reforms/)).<br><br>Launch is given only as the coming months, and no registration volume, processing time or fee is published, so the pilot's scale is unmeasured.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea | The [movable-collateral electronic registry](https://letravail224.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/AMI-IT-WARDIP-3_Copier.pdf) has software built and none of the institutional, legal or regulatory provisions it needs. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A rescue consultancy was tendered under the donor programme in July 2026, opening on the 27th and closing on 17 August ([rescue tender](https://letravail224.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/AMI-IT-WARDIP-3_Copier.pdf)).<br><br>Its security posture and user base are also not in place, so a system that exists as code has no legal effect, which is the clearest instance in this ledger of a build running ahead of its statute.</details> | No change |
| Guinea-Bissau | [743 new companies were registered in 2025](https://ang.gw/diretor-geral-do-cfe-diz-que-a-instituicao-registou-743-novas-empresas-em-2025-contra-598-de-2024/), up 24 per cent, with formation taking about five hours. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry's own director general gives the figures: [743 new companies in 2025 against 598 in 2024, with company formation now taking about five hours](https://ang.gw/diretor-geral-do-cfe-diz-que-a-instituicao-registou-743-novas-empresas-em-2025-contra-598-de-2024/).<br><br>It is the one Guinean register in the base publishing a throughput series and a service-time claim. The tax side hardened alongside it, with the [tax identification number issued solely through the payment platform from December 2025](https://kontaktu.mef.gw/instructions).</details> | Advanced |
| Kenya | A [one-stop registration service](https://brs.go.ke) carries the companies, collateral and hire-purchase registries with name reservation and registration merged into one online step. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It was signed in August 2026 between the chamber of commerce and the council of governors, with a national digitisation strategy called for to track growth, output and employment ([memorandum](https://www.kenyachamber.or.ke/2026/08/13/kncci-and-cog-sign-landmark-strategic-partnership-to-drive-msme-growth-data-digitization-and-county-led-economic-development/)).<br><br>No budget, milestone or data-governance arrangement for the pooled enterprise data is stated, which matters because the pooling would cross all 47 county jurisdictions.<br><br>The service carries the companies registry, the official receiver in insolvency, the movable property security rights collateral registry and the hire purchase registry, with [name reservation and business registration merged into one online application](https://brs.go.ke) taking three preferred names. No registration volume, processing time or register size is published on it.</details> | Advanced |
| Lesotho | A [single window is in development at the one-stop business facilitation centre](https://lestimes.com/?p=89016), and registration fees were waived for small firms. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2011-09-02 — [the Companies Act establishes the Registrar of Companies and the incorporation regime](https://businessregistrations.gov.ls/law/companies-act-2011.html).<br><br>2025-11-20 — [a competitiveness project confirms a business facilitation single window to simplify registration and licensing](https://lestimes.com/?p=89016).<br><br>2026-05-07 — [the Senate backed a registration and licensing fee waiver for micro, small and medium enterprises, already yielding new registrations](https://www.lena.gov.ls/senate-backs-fee-waiver-to-boost-youth-owned-businesses/).<br><br>No count of registered companies, and no processing time, is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Liberia | An enhanced collateral registry launched, extended to immovable property ([notice](https://www.cbl.org.lr/sites/default/files/documents/Public%20Service%20Announcement_Enhanced%20Collateral%20Registry%20for%20Movable%20and%20Immovable%20Property.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The central bank launched an enhanced collateral registry on 27 August 2026 under an amended secured transactions regulation, extending it to immovable property ([notice](https://www.cbl.org.lr/sites/default/files/documents/Public%20Service%20Announcement_Enhanced%20Collateral%20Registry%20for%20Movable%20and%20Immovable%20Property.pdf)) and presenting it as a way to curb duplicate claims and expand business credit ([report](https://liberianinvestigator.com/liberia-digital-collateral-registry-business-credit/)). The bank's own account adds the delivery figures for the credit reference system modernisation behind it ([release](https://www.cbl.org.lr/media/press-releases/cbl-launches-enhanced-collateral-registry-unlock-finance-and-spur-inclusive)).<br><br>It forms part of a wider financial-sector reform ([registry](https://elbcnews.com/2026/08/14/central-bank-of-liberia-prepares-to-launch-collateral-registry/)).<br><br>No launch date, registration volume, fee schedule or relationship to the land registry is stated, which matters because extending a movable-assets registry to immovable property crosses into the land registry's own subject.</details> | Advanced |
| Libya | A [Unified Economic Number](https://libyaherald.com/2026/07/libyan-government-issues-decision-on-implementation-of-unified-economic-number-project) was authorised in July 2026, the [chambers of commerce system](https://libyaherald.com/2026/03/unified-chambers-of-commerce-digital-system-launched-to-develop-electronic-services-within-libyas-chambers-of-commerce) went live in March, and the [one-stop shop](https://libyaherald.com/2026/02/one-stop-shop-system-for-company-registration-to-be-activated-again) is again promised. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Decision No. 348 of 2026 authorised implementation of a Unified Economic Number with a national council constituted for it, and no timetable, budget or legal basis for the data linkage is stated ([decision](https://libyaherald.com/2026/07/libyan-government-issues-decision-on-implementation-of-unified-economic-number-project)).<br><br>The Unified Digital System for Chambers of Commerce went live on 1 March 2026 with digital certificates of origin, chamber registration and a verification channel for customs ([system](https://libyaherald.com/2026/03/unified-chambers-of-commerce-digital-system-launched-to-develop-electronic-services-within-libyas-chambers-of-commerce)).<br><br>Reactivation of the One-Stop Shop for company registration under Law No. 23 of 2010 was agreed in February 2026, against a record of repeated past failures to reform registration and nothing yet delivered ([reactivation](https://libyaherald.com/2026/02/one-stop-shop-system-for-company-registration-to-be-activated-again)).<br><br>The health ministry's registration portal has registered 131 local and 53 foreign pharmaceutical companies and is being extended to the products themselves, confining attendance at the ministry to the final stage and licence collection ([portal](https://libyaherald.com/2026/08/ministry-of-health-developing-its-pharmagate-system-to-regulate-register-pharmaceutical-companies)) - a sectoral register rather than the general one.</details> | Advanced |
| Madagascar | The [online business registration platform](https://cnlegis.gov.mg/uploads/D2024-1911-VF.pdf) reached two more regions in the window as the investment board decentralised. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A decree of November 2024 established the platform, administered exclusively by the investment board with interoperability across the courts, tax and statistical registers, and preserved the paper procedure in regions where it is not deployed ([decree](https://cnlegis.gov.mg/uploads/D2024-1911-VF.pdf)).<br><br>A regional antenna opened at Manakara in May 2026, the platform having been live there since July 2025, coordinating registration between the tribunal, the statistics office, the tax administration and the board ([antenna](https://newsmada.com/2026/05/26/investissement-prive-ledbm-sinstalle-a-manakara/)).<br><br>A further antenna opened in the Sava region in July 2026, allowing local operators to register a company without travelling to the capital ([antenna](https://newsmada.com/2026/07/02/promotion-des-investissements-ledbm-simplante-dans-la-sava/)). No registration volume through the platform is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Malawi | A [new registration centre launched in June 2026](https://justice.gov.mw/index.php/node/554), replacing the Registrar General's department and extending online processing. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statutory basis is the [Business Registration Act, which establishes the business registration database and the office of the Registrar of Businesses](https://malawilii.org/akn/mw/act/1922/7/eng@2014-12-31).<br><br>2026-01 - the pre-transition reform report [puts 2025 revenue at 10 billion kwacha, largely from business registrations, with archiving and digitalisation under way](https://www.justice.gov.mw/node/509).<br><br>2026-06 - the [Companies, Registrations and Intellectual Property Centre was formally launched, replacing the department and extending online processing on the registration system to stamp and estate duty](https://justice.gov.mw/index.php/node/554).<br><br>Revenue is the only number on the record. No count of registered businesses, no processing time and no online share is published at either end of the window, so what the reorganisation changed for a person registering a company is not knowable from the base.</details> | Advanced |
| Mali | A [single window for business creation](https://sgg-mali.ml/JO/2008/mali-jo-2008-26.pdf) has existed by decree since 2008; the investment agency's [quarterly newsletter](https://apimali.gov.ml/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/News-Letter-Trimestres-1-2026-API-Mali-1.pdf) is the newest account of its work. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Decree n. 08-276/P-RM of 13 May 2008 fixes the administrative formalities for creating a business through a single window ([decree](https://sgg-mali.ml/JO/2008/mali-jo-2008-26.pdf)).<br><br>No registration volume, processing time or online channel is published, so the single window exists in law and its performance is unmeasured ([newsletter](https://apimali.gov.ml/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/News-Letter-Trimestres-1-2026-API-Mali-1.pdf)).</details> | No change |
| Mauritania | [Law 2021-005](https://natlex.ilo.org/dyn/natlex2/natlex2/files/download/113004/MRT-113004.pdf) amends the Commercial Code and carries the trade register and the movable securities regime; nothing states whether either runs electronically. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The law of 10 February 2021 amends, completes and repeals provisions of the Commercial Code of 2000 ([law](https://natlex.ilo.org/dyn/natlex2/natlex2/files/download/113004/MRT-113004.pdf)).<br><br>An investment code of February 2025 and its application decree of August 2025 carry a single-window arrangement for investors ([code](https://apim.gov.mr/wp-content/uploads/2025/10/Code-des-Investissements-2025-FR.pdf), [decree](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/mau240422.pdf)), and no operating single window, service list, processing time or investor count is on the record.<br><br>Nothing says how many entries either register holds.</details> | No change |
| Mauritius | The register runs under the [2006 Act](https://lawsofmauritius.govmu.org/portal/viewlegislationdocument/web/?docnumber=&doctitle=QnVzaW5lc3MgUmVnaXN0cmF0aW9uIEFjdA%3D%3D&doctype=act) and feeds [quarterly enterprise statistics](https://statsmauritius.govmu.org/Documents/Statistics/By_Subject/Businesses/BusinessStatistics_2Qtr26_290726.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Business Registration Act has been in force since October 2006 ([Act](https://lawsofmauritius.govmu.org/portal/viewlegislationdocument/web/?docnumber=&doctitle=QnVzaW5lc3MgUmVnaXN0cmF0aW9uIEFjdA%3D%3D&doctype=act)).<br><br>The register feeds quarterly enterprise-dynamics statistics, published for the second quarter of 2026 in July ([statistics](https://statsmauritius.govmu.org/Documents/Statistics/By_Subject/Businesses/BusinessStatistics_2Qtr26_290726.pdf)).<br><br>The economic and financial measures bill debated in July 2026 touches its statutory base ([debate](https://mauritiusassembly.govmu.org/mauritiusassembly/index.php/download/482/2026/26641/debate-no-26-of-2026-unrevised-friday-31-july-2026-2.pdf)). No registration volume, processing time or online-share figure for the register itself is held.</details> | No change |
| Morocco | [109,644 enterprises on the register in 2025, up 14.6 per cent](http://www.ompic.ma/fr/actualites/lompic-affiche-un-bilan-2025-remarquable-confirmant-la-dynamique-entrepreneuriale-et), with 42,905 new registrations to May 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register was founded by the [1996 commercial code and given electronic filing by law 88-17](https://rnesm.justice.gov.ma/Documentation/MA/3_TradeRecord_fr-FR.pdf).<br><br>Its throughput is published and rising: [109,644 enterprises in 2025, up 14.6 per cent, with the country climbing to 57th in the global innovation index](http://www.ompic.ma/fr/actualites/lompic-affiche-un-bilan-2025-remarquable-confirmant-la-dynamique-entrepreneuriale-et), and [42,905 new registrations in the first five months of 2026 with legal persons rising to 75 per cent of the total](http://www.ompic.ma/fr/actualites/creation-dentreprises-au-maroc-plus-de-42-900-nouvelles-immatriculations-fin-mai-2026).</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | [74 per cent of legal-entity registrations](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-74-of-legal-entity-registration-applications-now-submitted-electronically-minister-says/) were submitted electronically by June 2026, on the justice minister's figure. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The share was stated by the minister at an international legal forum, with no published series behind it ([share](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-74-of-legal-entity-registration-applications-now-submitted-electronically-minister-says/)).<br><br>No absolute registration count, processing time or rejection rate is held. A three-quarters electronic share with no denominator is the shape of most of this unit's administrative evidence.</details> | Advanced |
| Namibia | A [Corporate Laws Bill](https://www.bipa.na/download/law-reform/) at third draft would require every company to keep a beneficial-owner register in Namibia, above [existing online registration services](https://www.bipa.na/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The draft consolidates and re-enacts the Companies Act with the business and intellectual property authority as registrar, and its clause 114 would require every company, and every external company establishing a place of business in the country, to keep a register of beneficial owners recording each owner's name, date of birth, identity-document number and residential, business, email and postal addresses; company process is routed through the electronic transactions statute ([bill](https://www.bipa.na/download/law-reform/)).<br><br>Online name search, name reservation and application-status verification are already offered, with no transaction or uptake figure published ([services](https://www.bipa.na/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Niger | Business formalities are [moved online for completion through a single window](https://anp.ne/commerce-abdoulaye-seydou-met-en-avant-les-reformes-engagees-pour-renforcer-lattractivite-des-investissements-au-niger/), on a register held at the commercial court. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The commerce minister [stated on 30 June 2026](https://anp.ne/commerce-abdoulaye-seydou-met-en-avant-les-reformes-engagees-pour-renforcer-lattractivite-des-investissements-au-niger/) that business-formality procedures are being moved online so investors can complete them through a single window, alongside investment-code and small-business charter measures; the statement names no technical milestone.<br><br>The register's legal basis is the [2019 commercial courts law](http://www.tribunalcommerceniamey.ne/uploads/documents/2019%20josp%20n%C2%B0%204%20-%20ProCiv-TribCom-TransElect.pmd%20%281%29.pdf), whose article 15 establishes the commercial and movable credit register at the commercial court, makes the Niamey register serve as the national file pending offices elsewhere, and provides for electronic documents and electronic dissemination of registry information.<br><br>No count of registered businesses, and no public search interface, is on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Nigeria | A [beneficial ownership register](https://www.opengovpartnership.org/documents/nigeria-results-report-2023-2025/) launched in 2023 is in use by enforcement agencies, alongside a [national electronic assets register](https://www.thisdaylive.com/2025/03/07/uzoka-anite-national-electronic-assets-register-will-boost-nigerias-rating-with-creditors-unlock-investments-others) live since March 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [electronic assets register went live in March 2025](https://www.thisdaylive.com/2025/03/07/uzoka-anite-national-electronic-assets-register-will-boost-nigerias-rating-with-creditors-unlock-investments-others); no asset count, agency coverage or update cycle is published.<br><br>The [beneficial ownership register, launched in 2023, is open to government institutions, civil society, journalists and citizens, and regulatory and enforcement agencies are using it for transparency, enforcement and asset recovery](https://www.opengovpartnership.org/documents/nigeria-results-report-2023-2025/); it is named as contributing to Nigeria's exit from the Financial Action Task Force grey list.<br><br>[Compliance by legacy companies and digitisation of the corporate registry's records remain outstanding](https://www.opengovpartnership.org/documents/nigeria-results-report-2023-2025/). Business registration itself reaches the base only through the centralised service gateway that offers it as one of several services.</details> | Advanced |
| Rwanda | The registrar's own page states that [registration is wholly online and free of charge](https://org.rdb.rw/business-registration/), with incorporation completed within six working hours. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Registration moved fully online in January 2026 on a market-expansion report's account, which puts incorporation at 24 to 72 hours at a cost among the continent's lowest ([platform](https://techcabal.com/2026/07/22/norebase-report-nigeria-rwanda-lead-africas-fastest-incorporation-markets/)).<br><br>The Office of the Registrar General's own page states the process is wholly online, free of charge, and completed within six working hours on delivery of a certificate of incorporation ([registrar](https://org.rdb.rw/business-registration/)). The page carries no date of its own, so which of the two processing times is current cannot be settled from what the base holds.<br><br>No registration volume is published, so what the move changed for the stock of registered businesses is not measurable from the base.</details> | Advanced |
| Sao Tome and Principe | The [company one-stop window](https://dgrn.gov.st/reg_gue.php) performs formation, alteration, dissolution and branch filings and requires the taxpayer identifier throughout, over a [modernisation diagnostic](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099102025185177863/pdf/P177158-d0ebd79d-6646-4e92-b9ae-1183b632c15a.pdf) completed in 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The directorate's page sets out the acts performed - company formation, alteration of the articles, dissolution, branch registration and sole-trader registration - and shows the taxpayer identifier in operational use: the taxpayer card is required of the applicant, the identifier of shareholders, foreign non-resident partners and companies, and a negative certificate of tax debt is required to dissolve ([window](https://dgrn.gov.st/reg_gue.php)).<br><br>A legal and technical diagnostic toward a modern, data-oriented registry was presented by a lender mission in September 2025, with the directorate and the identity institute flagging the need for offline access given electricity and internet limits ([diagnostic](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099102025185177863/pdf/P177158-d0ebd79d-6646-4e92-b9ae-1183b632c15a.pdf)).<br><br>The window is operating and the modernisation is a diagnostic. No registration count, turnaround figure or online filing route is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Senegal | A [business registration platform](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/public-management/senegal-launches-new-platform-to-streamline-business-registration) launched in February 2025 for the enterprise and association identification number, and eighteen months have passed with no update. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The platform launched in February 2025 ([platform](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/public-management/senegal-launches-new-platform-to-streamline-business-registration)).<br><br>Eighteen months with no update in the base means the object may have moved without the base holding it, which is a limit on this reading rather than a finding about the platform.</details> | No change |
| Seychelles | The President [toured the registrar general office](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/public-management/seychelles-pledges-to-modernize-public-services-through-digital-transformation), praised its digitalisation and announced unspecified legislative reforms. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The tour was in February 2025 and the reforms were not specified ([tour](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/public-management/seychelles-pledges-to-modernize-public-services-through-digital-transformation)).<br><br>Nothing has entered the base since. A business one-stop portal was separately launched in pilot with a full rollout targeted for June 2026, and no source connects it to the registry.</details> | No change |
| Sierra Leone | A [Companies and Business Registration Bill 2026](https://nib.gov.sl/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/COMPANIES-002.pdf) is drafted, following a budget proposal to [launch 24-hour registration](https://nib.gov.sl/nib-proposes-le-22-million-budget-to-drive-investment-and-launch-24-hour-business-registration/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The draft bill was published by the National Investment Board in March 2026 ([bill](https://nib.gov.sl/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/COMPANIES-002.pdf)).<br><br>The board proposed a Le 22 million budget in October 2025 to drive investment and launch 24-hour business registration ([budget](https://nib.gov.sl/nib-proposes-le-22-million-budget-to-drive-investment-and-launch-24-hour-business-registration/)); its own statutory basis is the National Investment Board Act 2022 ([Act](https://www.parliament.gov.sl/uploads/acts/THE%20NATIONAL%20INVESTMENT%20BOARD%20ACT%202022.pdf)).<br><br>No registration figure, no current turnaround time and no parliamentary stage for the bill is held, so the 24-hour claim has nothing to be measured against.</details> | Advanced |
| Somalia | The registration system [expanded into second-tier cities and rural areas](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/feature/2026/03/31/how-stronger-financial-and-digital-services-are-supporting-women-s-msmes-and-jobs-in-somalia), and women's share of registrations rose from 21 to 31 per cent between 2024 and 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The founding instrument is the 2021 company law regulations, which established the registrar's office, the general register, online registration, a fee schedule and an industrial classification code ([regulations](https://ebusiness.gov.so/resources/company-law-regulations-english)).<br><br>Expansion into second-tier cities and rural areas under a financial-services project raised women's share of business and identity registrations from 21 per cent in 2024 to 31 per cent in 2025 ([feature](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/feature/2026/03/31/how-stronger-financial-and-digital-services-are-supporting-women-s-msmes-and-jobs-in-somalia)).<br><br>A statistical business register drawn from the 2024 establishment census was published in January 2026, recording 174,149 establishments ([booklet](https://nbs.gov.so/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Somalia-Business-Establishment-Analysis-Bookle.pdf)).</details> | Advanced |
| South Africa | A simplified beneficial-ownership declaration [processed more than 23,000 filings in three weeks](https://www.cipc.co.za/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/BO-Optimized-system-4-September-2025_.pdf), a [partnership register was operationalised](https://www.sars.gov.za/guide-to-the-beneficial-owner-register-for-partnership-it3bo/), and [a bill would add deregistration for non-filing](https://www.parliament.gov.za/storage/app/media/Bills/2026/B15-2026_General_Laws_Anti_Money_Laundering_AB.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-08-22 — [a simplified online beneficial-ownership declaration entered service, with more than 23,000 declarations processed by the notice date three weeks later](https://www.cipc.co.za/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/BO-Optimized-system-4-September-2025_.pdf), against [the companies commission's own annual reporting](https://www.cipc.co.za/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/CIPC_2024-2025-ANNUAL-REPORT_Rev-5_vd20250804-1.pdf).<br><br>2026-05-20 — [the revenue service operationalised a structured partnership beneficial-owner register and filing workflow, generating persistent partnership and partner reference numbers for later validation and pre-population](https://www.sars.gov.za/guide-to-the-beneficial-owner-register-for-partnership-it3bo/). Two beneficial-ownership registers now run in parallel and no source states whether they reconcile.<br><br>2026-05-27 — [a bill was published requiring reporting of material beneficial-ownership discrepancies, deregistration after prolonged failure to file required registers, and tribunal review of administrative fines](https://www.parliament.gov.za/storage/app/media/Bills/2026/B15-2026_General_Laws_Anti_Money_Laundering_AB.pdf). It is the enforcement half of the two registers and it is not enacted.</details> | Advanced |
| South Sudan | The [Companies Act 2012](https://mofaic.gov.ss/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/Companies-Act-2012.pdf) creates a statutory Register of Companies and an office of Registrar; nothing else about it is published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act establishes the register and the Registrar ([Act](https://mofaic.gov.ss/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/Companies-Act-2012.pdf)).<br><br>No registration count, digital filing route, search interface or turnaround figure appears in the base. The register exists in law and is invisible in practice - which is the same shape as the address register recorded separately.</details> | No change |
| Sudan | The register runs on a [database built from 2003](https://moj.gov.sd/posts/post/64), and all administrative work in Port Sudan including trademarks was [suspended in December 2025](https://www.agip.com/News/en/news/23512). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The justice ministry's description of the commercial registrations department records its divisions, a computerised company database built from 2003, and 13 state branch offices; it still cites the 1925 Companies Act rather than the 2015 Act ([description](https://moj.gov.sd/posts/post/64)).<br><br>The registrar suspended all administrative work in Port Sudan, including the trademark office, from 14 December 2025 as part of moving institutions back to Khartoum, with no resumption date given ([suspension](https://www.agip.com/News/en/news/23512)).<br><br>In April 2026 the justice and communications ministries agreed to activate a digital-transformation partnership with the registrar present ([partnership](https://moj.gov.sd/posts/post/1137)).</details> | Regressed |
| Tanzania | The electronic government agency gave a [registered-society count](https://dailynews.co.tz/ega-drives-digital-farming/) for a cooperative management system at an agricultural exhibition. <details><summary>Full record</summary>No spend, timeline or denominator is given, so the count stands unbenchmarked ([system](https://dailynews.co.tz/ega-drives-digital-farming/)).<br><br>A registry count with no denominator cannot show coverage, and the exhibition at which it was given is the same venue at which the crop payment figures and the university prototypes were presented.</details> | Advanced |
| Togo | Registrations rose to [8,129 in the first half of 2026](https://www.togofirst.com/fr/gouvernance-economique/0707-19479-togo-plus-de-8000-entreprises-creees-en-six-mois-un-rebond-apres-deux-annees-de-recul), the first rise after two annual declines. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register itself rests on [loi n 2016-034 of 2 December 2016](http://www.jo.gouv.tg/sites/default/files/publications/JOS_02_12_16-61%C3%A8%20ANNEE%20N%C2%B037.pdf), which creates a national file at the Lome court of appeal and local files at six towns, sets the registrars' duties and provides for transmission to the regional file.<br><br>Annual throughput fell in 2025 to [14,202 on the government's figure](https://www.republiquetogolaise.tg/gestion-publique/0501-11470-14-202-entreprises-creees-au-togo-en-2025), down 4.8 per cent on 14,919 in 2024 — though the formalities centre's own bilan gives [14,172](https://cfetogo.tg/uploads/statistiques/6981ad50957a6.pdf) for the same year, and the two figures have not been reconciled publicly.<br><br>The first half of 2026 recorded [8,129 registrations](https://www.togofirst.com/fr/gouvernance-economique/0707-19479-togo-plus-de-8000-entreprises-creees-en-six-mois-un-rebond-apres-deux-annees-de-recul), up about 5 per cent on the 7,747 of the same half of 2025.</details> | Advanced |
| Tunisia | [Paper filing at the national business register was discontinued](https://en.africanmanager.com/tunisia-national-business-register-accelerates-digitalization-of-its-services/) from 1 July 2026 under a February decree, and a [register of community companies](https://www.nessma.tv/fr/economie/actu/lancement-du-registre-national-des-societes-communautaires-pour-impulser-le-developpement-local/560812) launched in July. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Paper filing ended on 1 July 2026 under the finance minister's decree of 12 February ([register](https://en.africanmanager.com/tunisia-national-business-register-accelerates-digitalization-of-its-services/)).<br><br>That is the same date the mobile identity credential became compulsory for business registration, so the paper channel closed and the digital credential became mandatory together.<br><br>A national register of community companies launched on 20 July 2026 under a 2025 decree-law ([community](https://www.nessma.tv/fr/economie/actu/lancement-du-registre-national-des-societes-communautaires-pour-impulser-le-developpement-local/560812)).</details> | Advanced |
| Uganda | A [non-individual register](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/business/centralised-digital-register-to-streamline-bu-NV_233277_052026) launched in May 2026 as the corporate counterpart to the national identity number, with no registered-entity count published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It launched in May 2026 ([register](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/business/centralised-digital-register-to-streamline-bu-NV_233277_052026)).<br><br>Giving legal persons an identifier matching the one natural persons hold is the design that lets the two be joined, and no source states whether they are.</details> | Advanced |
| Zambia | [9,163 companies were registered between January and May 2026](https://www.lusakatimes.com/2026/07/03/pacra-registers-9163-companies-between-january-and-may-2026/), and a project [began rebuilding the register in house](https://www.openownership.org/en/blog/beneficial-ownership-and-business-register-reform-in-zambia-announcing-our-new-partnership-with-pacra/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The registration agency reported 9,163 companies incorporated between January and May 2026, against 8,551 in the same months of 2025](https://www.lusakatimes.com/2026/07/03/pacra-registers-9163-companies-between-january-and-may-2026/), a rise of 7 per cent.<br><br>2025-12-30 — [an amendment Act strengthened beneficial-ownership disclosure, verification and periodic review obligations on the register of companies and the register of beneficial owners](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/sites/default/files/documents/acts/Act%20No.%2023%20of%202025%2C%20The%20Companies%20%28Amendment%29.pdf).<br><br>2026-03 — [a multi-year project was launched with the registration agency and a donor funder to build a next-generation, in-house business and beneficial ownership registration system](https://www.openownership.org/en/blog/beneficial-ownership-and-business-register-reform-in-zambia-announcing-our-new-partnership-with-pacra/). In-house is the operative word: a register rebuilt by its own agency rather than licensed from a vendor is a different long-run proposition, and no cost, timetable or go-live date is published for it.</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | The deadline for re-registering onto the [electronic registry](https://www.veritaszim.net/sites/veritas_d/files/SI%202026-076%20Companies%20and%20Other%20Business%20Entities%20%28Re-Registration%29%20%28Amendment%29%20Regulations%2C%202026%20%28No.%201%29.pdf) was moved out two years in April 2026, from 20 April 2026 to 20 April 2028. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Companies and Other Business Entities Act constitutes the registration office under a chief registrar and defines the electronic registry and electronic records the register runs on ([Act](https://zimlii.org/akn/zw/act/2019/4)).<br><br>Statutory Instrument 108 of 2025 set 20 April 2026 as the deadline for companies and private business corporations registered before the electronic registry to re-register, with automatic deregistration and removal from the register for those that did not ([regulations](https://www.veritaszim.net/sites/veritas_d/files/SI%202025-108%20Companies%20and%20Other%20Business%20Entities%20%28Re-Registration%29%20Regulations%2C%202025.pdf)).<br><br>Three days before it fell due, Statutory Instrument 76 of 2026 deleted that date and substituted 20 April 2028 ([amendment](https://www.veritaszim.net/sites/veritas_d/files/SI%202026-076%20Companies%20and%20Other%20Business%20Entities%20%28Re-Registration%29%20%28Amendment%29%20Regulations%2C%202026%20%28No.%201%29.pdf)).<br><br>No count of entities re-registered, or still to re-register, is published at either date. Separately, of 204,798 establishments surveyed, more than 76 per cent were found operating informally ([establishments](https://zimbabwenow.co.zw/articles/15650/76-of-zim-businesses-unregistered-zimstat)).</details> | Stalled |

## Social protection register

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Angola | A single social register [launched in the capital in July 2026](https://angop.ao/noticias/sociedade/lancamento-do-cadastro-social-unico-marca-a-semana-social/) as the centralised targeting database, with no governing instrument stated. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-25 — [a single national social registry launched to map vulnerable households and act as the centralised database directing social supports](https://angop.ao/noticias/sociedade/lancamento-do-cadastro-social-unico-marca-a-semana-social/), replacing a position in which social-transfer targeting rested on one programme's own registry.<br><br>No enrolment target, data-protection basis, interoperability plan or governing instrument was stated at launch. That is the whole of the record: a database of the country's poorest households, built to direct payments to them, with nothing published about who may read it, how long it holds what it holds, or which other system it connects to.<br><br>The transfer programme it now targets is reported under government-to-person payments, and reached about 6.5 million citizens.</details> | Advanced |
