| Benin | A single social register [was created in January 2026](https://lematinal.bj/registre-social-unique-au-benin-la-protection-des-plus-vulnerables-renforcee/) to decide eligibility across programmes from one secured socio-economic database. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-01-21 — [the Council of Ministers created an information system registering households and deciding eligibility across social-protection programmes, classifying them as non-poor, non-extreme poor and extreme poor and updated periodically, so that cash and in-kind transfers, health insurance, training, credit and pension schemes draw on one secured socio-economic database](https://lematinal.bj/registre-social-unique-au-benin-la-protection-des-plus-vulnerables-renforcee/). The two social-protection programmes already running are named among its users.<br><br>Nothing existed before it. No enrolment figure, no data-protection assessment, no retention rule and no appeal route against a poverty classification is published — and a classification that gates health insurance and credit is the most consequential automated decision on this ledger.</details> | Advanced |
| Botswana | The [Single Social Registry reached 98 per cent coverage of beneficiaries in the five main programmes by March 2025](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099121825122532143/txt/BOSIB-35f31917-1454-433b-a021-4d7246073f71.txt), against a 75.8 per cent target. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2022 — [a review dates the registry’s development to 2016, a pilot in four districts across 2019 and 2020, and six districts uploaded by May 2021](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099645103062256866/pdf/P1721750af4ddb0ce098d30b331412b0ab8.pdf).<br><br>2025-10-07 — [coverage of beneficiaries in the five main poverty-targeted programmes reached 98 per cent by March 2025 against a target of 75.8 per cent](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099121825122532143/txt/BOSIB-35f31917-1454-433b-a021-4d7246073f71.txt).<br><br>2025-11-11 — [the registry’s operationalisation was announced from the podium](https://dailynews.gov.bw/news-detail/89025).<br><br>No figure for people recorded, as against beneficiaries of existing programmes, is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Burkina Faso | The single social register [holds 362,698 households and 2,217,518 people across 99 communes](https://www.sidwaya.info/registre-social-unique-le-burkina-faso-renforce-la-protection-sociale/), with about fifty further communes prepared for 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-01-06 — [the register holds 362,698 households and 2,217,518 people, of whom 1,191,859 women, across 99 communes in ten regions, after extension to 24 new communes during 2025, with about fifty further communes prepared for 2026](https://www.sidwaya.info/registre-social-unique-le-burkina-faso-renforce-la-protection-sociale/). It is stated to interoperate with the unique identifier to prevent duplicates.<br><br>That is the largest population database on this ledger with published figures, and it is being built to interoperate with an identifier that has not yet been enrolled — so the de-duplication it promises is prospective.<br><br>No data-protection basis, retention rule, appeal route or eligibility formula is published for it, and the supervisor that would examine those questions is mid-merger, reported under data protection.</details> | Advanced |
| Burundi | The unified social registry holds [249,671 households against a target of 290,000](https://lerenouveau.bi/projet-merankabandi-ii-bilan-a-mis-parcours/), across all 3,044 collines and quartiers. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The unified social registry and its management system are the foundation of the national social protection system, reused by two named programmes](https://www.worldbank.org/en/results/2026/05/01/building-burundi-s-national-social-protection-systems-to-deliver-jobs-and-respond-to-crises).<br><br>2026-06-16 — [the national social protection commission put the state of the registry on its own oversight agenda at its ninth annual general assembly](https://abpinfo.bi/2026/06/16/lancement-de-la-9eme-assemblee-generale-de-la-cnps/), alongside the programme and the 2024-2033 national policy.<br><br>2026-06-30 — [the modernised system is deployed in the government data centre and 249,671 households are recorded against a final target of 290,000, with coverage extending to all 3,044 collines and quartiers](https://lerenouveau.bi/projet-merankabandi-ii-bilan-a-mis-parcours/). It is the most completely reported register on this ledger: a count, a denominator, a target and a host.</details> | Advanced |
| Cameroon | The [unified social registry stands where it was appraised in February 2022](http://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/116291646834371027/pdf/Cameroon-Adaptive-Safety-Nets-and-Economic-Inclusion-Project.pdf), with nothing on file since on what has been built. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry is [provided for under the adaptive safety nets and economic inclusion project, whose component on shock-responsive digital delivery systems - a social registry and a digital payments system - is appraised at US$5m equivalent, with safety nets estimated to reach 25% of the poor population](http://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/116291646834371027/pdf/Cameroon-Adaptive-Safety-Nets-and-Economic-Inclusion-Project.pdf), [as set out at project identification](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099150001062231657/pdf/Project0Inform0n0Project000P175363.pdf).<br><br>Nothing on file since appraisal reports what has been built. Later reporting on the registry is in the base but is not citable, so the position stated here is four years old and older than the record.</details> | No change |
| Cape Verde | The single social registry [now queries held data directly instead of taking paper declarations](https://expressodasilhas.cv/pais/2025/12/17/csu-passa-a-permitir-consulta-directa-de-dados-e-elimina-declaracoes-em-papel/100473). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The Cadastro Social Unico stopped requiring paper declarations in December 2025 and queries the data directly](https://expressodasilhas.cv/pais/2025/12/17/csu-passa-a-permitir-consulta-directa-de-dados-e-elimina-declaracoes-em-papel/100473). [Portaria Conjunta 16/2026 of 13 February 2026 extends it to the allocation of state housing](https://boe.incv.cv/Bulletins/View?id=89831), and [in June 2026 the government called households in climate-vulnerable zones to register in it](https://www.governo.cv/apelo-ao-registo-no-cadastro-social-unico-csu-para-agregados-familiares-em-zonas-climaticamente-vulneraveis/).<br><br>[It was created by Decreto Regulamentar 7/2018 and is run by the family and social inclusion ministry](https://mfids.gov.cv/v2/index.php/cadastro-social-unico).<br><br>No registered-household count and no coverage rate against the population are held.</details> | Advanced |
| Central African Republic | A [safety-nets project finances the social registry](https://ewsdata.rightsindevelopment.org/files/documents/11/WB-P179211_5aXsjNN.pdf) and a [second additional financing of US$50m was approved](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099060226181018944/pdf/BOSIB-b67bf62d-5670-4d2e-9c1c-d85c2fdb544c.pdf) in May 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A closed project [established a social registry and a fully digitised payment system as the foundations of a social-protection system, transferred to the responsible ministry](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099021625182027556/pdf/BOSIB-eb93e29a-8615-48fa-bb03-851377fddb5f.pdf).<br><br>The registry's development is [financed by a safety-nets project appraised in May 2024](https://ewsdata.rightsindevelopment.org/files/documents/11/WB-P179211_5aXsjNN.pdf), and [a second additional financing of US$50m, taking emergency support to US$150m, was approved on 28 May 2026, scaling emergency cash transfers](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099060226181018944/pdf/BOSIB-b67bf62d-5670-4d2e-9c1c-d85c2fdb544c.pdf).<br><br>No registered-household figure, coverage rate or update cycle for the registry is published at any point.</details> | Advanced |
| Chad | The unified social registry carries [684,500 households](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099735205212519675/pdf/IDU-f63a01b3-734b-45cc-bdf8-323adb04c51d.pdf), with a [pastoral component of 37,449](https://praps2tchad.org/vers-une-cartographie-exhaustive-de-la-vulnerabilite-pastorale-grace-au-registre-social-unifie/) across 13 provinces. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry is described as a centralised, dynamic, spatially aware database interoperable with other systems, carrying 684,500 households under the adaptive social protection strategy for 2025-2030 ([registry](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099735205212519675/pdf/IDU-f63a01b3-734b-45cc-bdf8-323adb04c51d.pdf)).<br><br>Its pastoral component enumerated 37,449 households across 13 provinces and more than 250 pastoral concentration zones, presented in March 2026 with an instruction to extend the survey ([mapping](https://praps2tchad.org/vers-une-cartographie-exhaustive-de-la-vulnerabilite-pastorale-grace-au-registre-social-unifie/)).<br><br>An adaptive safety-nets project financed at 120 million dollars launched at Mao on 3 April 2026, deploying across nine provinces ([project](https://manara.td/kanem-lancement-du-projet-de-filets-sociaux-adaptatifs-et-productifs-a-mao/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Comoros | The [register's technical build was near complete at December 2025](https://pfss-comores.org/ressources/download/9), with project disbursement at 82.8 per cent. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The founding instrument is now on file: [decret n 20-090/PR of 23 June 2020, fifteen articles creating the register and setting its organisation and functioning, signed by the president at Moroni](https://pfss-comores.org/ressources/download/19).<br><br>2025-12 - the supervision mission [put the register and its information system near complete and project disbursement at 82.8 per cent, up from 31 per cent in October 2023, crediting the digitisation of operations, electronic payments and a digitised monitoring system for the delivery](https://pfss-comores.org/ressources/download/9). The same mission lists interoperability with the identity, civil-registration and health systems as work still recommended.<br><br>2026-07 - the [World Bank's representative described it as the most complete socio-economic database ever built in the country](https://lagazettedescomores.com/soci%C3%A9t%C3%A9/registre-social-unique-62-000-m%C3%A9nages-d%C3%A9j%C3%A0-enregistr%C3%A9s-.html). No inter-administration sharing protocol is in force.</details> | Advanced |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The [single social register is interconnected with the identity system](https://filetsociaux-ci.org/registre-social-unique-des-menages-pauvres-et-vulnerables-rsu-signature-dune-convention-entre-le-bureau-de-coordination-du-programme-filets-sociaux-bcpfs-et-loffice-nation/), with more than 200,000 households registered against a two-million target. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register held [more than 200,000 households in the process of registration in April 2025](https://filetsociaux-ci.org/operationnalisation-du-registre-social-unique-rsu-des-menages-pauvres-et-vulnerables-plus-de-200-000-menages-en-cours-denregistrement), against a [2021-2025 target of more than 2 million poor and vulnerable households](https://solidarite.gouv.ci/actualite/actudetail/plus-de-2-millions-de-mnages-pauvres-et-vulnrables-cibls-par-le-registre-social-unique-pour-la-priode-2021-2025298). Its [own indicators were elaborated and validated in August 2025](https://filetsociaux-ci.org/elaboration-et-validation-des-indicateurs-du-registre-social-unique-des-menages-pauvres-et-vulnerables-rsu/).<br><br>2026-04 - a [convention interconnected it with the national register of natural persons to remove duplicates, curb fraud and cut inclusion and exclusion error](https://filetsociaux-ci.org/registre-social-unique-des-menages-pauvres-et-vulnerables-rsu-signature-dune-convention-entre-le-bureau-de-coordination-du-programme-filets-sociaux-bcpfs-et-loffice-nation/).<br><br>No household count has been published since April 2025, so the register is sixteen months without an update on the measure that would show whether the target is reachable.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | The registry [held 515,730 people in February 2025](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099022525185016955/pdf/BOSIB-87c444de-4797-4bf9-b654-4932a7fb0112.pdf) and the [scaling to 600,000 had not begun by January 2026](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099020526154525280/pdf/P506969-8944364d-b716-457b-8444-723719632bd9.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry sits at the social affairs ministry, [founded by decree in 2017 and amended in 2020, with provisions to include refugees](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/journal-officiel/n13-du-13-07-2020/), and [held 515,730 people as of February 2025, undergoing an audit, an operational-manual revision and an interoperability upgrade](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099022525185016955/pdf/BOSIB-87c444de-4797-4bf9-b654-4932a7fb0112.pdf).<br><br>2026-02-05 - the first supervision report since the project became effective in August 2025 records the [scaling component, targeting growth to 600,000, had not commenced activities as at 31 January 2026](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099020526154525280/pdf/P506969-8944364d-b716-457b-8444-723719632bd9.pdf).<br><br>No coverage figure later than February 2025 is published.</details> | No change, the scaling component effective in August 2025 had not begun by January 2026 |
| DR Congo | A [draft decree creating the single social register was adopted in Council on 25 July 2025](https://actualite.cd/2025/07/27/rdc-adoption-du-projet-de-decret-portant-creation-organisation-et-fonctionnement-du) and has not been promulgated. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-07-25 - the 53rd Council of Ministers [adopted a draft decree creating and organising the register](https://actualite.cd/2025/07/27/rdc-adoption-du-projet-de-decret-portant-creation-organisation-et-fonctionnement-du).<br><br>Twelve months later there is no signed decree number, signature date or publication, no operator, no household enrolment and no link to the identification office. Adoption in council is not promulgation, and twelve months in the same state is the finding. It would also create a second population register alongside the identification office's, and nothing on file states how the two would relate.</details> | No change |
| Egypt | The [unified social registry covers about 12m households and reaches 4.7m active households](https://english.ahram.org.eg/News/547276.aspx); no fresher figure is held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry [covers about 12m households, reaching 4.7m active households and about 17m individuals, roughly a fifth of families, with payments by national scheme card, national-identity-based monitoring across more than 3,000 offices and targeting by proxy means testing plus home verification](https://english.ahram.org.eg/News/547276.aspx).<br><br>No fresher registry, caseload or payment figure is held. It is [a candidate base for automatic enrolment into universal health insurance per a February 2025 policy note](https://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/099052025143012394), and nothing establishes that this has been done.</details> | No change |
| Equatorial Guinea | A 2025 census [purged 2,282 untraceable pensioners](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/el_delegado_nacional_del_inseso_comparece_ante_la_camara_de_los_diputados) from a roll of 137,445 affiliates. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The institute's roll stood at [137,445 affiliates](https://inege.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/GE-en-cifras-2025-Final.pdf) in the 2025 compendium, and a [2025 census removed 2,282 pensioners who could not be traced, with benefits moved to bank-only payment](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/el_delegado_nacional_del_inseso_comparece_ante_la_camara_de_los_diputados).<br><br>The register runs on a system its own delegate calls [obsolete, unreplicated and dependent on a single internet provider](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/comparecencia_del_delegado_nacional_del_inseso), and the [social protection law passed in March 2026 specifies no successor](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/pleno_de_aprobacion_del_proyecto_de_ley_reguladora_del_sistema_de_proteccion_social).</details> | Advanced |
| Eswatini | A [social registry is piloting in one of four regions](https://eswatinipositivenews.online/world-bank-injects-e42-5-million-into-social-registry-project-to-deliver-homes-and-jobs/), with 44,683 households enrolled and targeting run on a proxy means test. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The national dynamic social registry published the results of its Shiselweni region pilot, including disability data ([results](https://www.eswatiniobserver.com/2-304-shiselweni-residents-have-difficulty-walking/)).<br><br>[The Shiselweni pilot covered 47,766 households, registered 44,683 of them and recorded 3,088 as vacant or non-consenting](https://eswatinipositivenews.online/world-bank-injects-e42-5-million-into-social-registry-project-to-deliver-homes-and-jobs/), against a projected 47,260 households in the region.<br><br>[Targeting uses a proxy means test built on 20 variables from the household income and expenditure survey, and the programme carries US$2.5 million, about E42.5 million, of World Bank funding](https://eswatinipositivenews.online/world-bank-injects-e42-5-million-into-social-registry-project-to-deliver-homes-and-jobs/); each region is to be given three months to implement.<br><br>No national rollout date, no statement of which grant programmes will draw on the registry, and no data-protection or grievance arrangement for it, are on record.</details> | Advanced |
| Ethiopia | The national social registry moved from a concept-stage activity into funded implementation ([approval](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2026/03/03/ethiopia-a-new-phase-of-support-for-safety-nets-will-foster-resilience-and-create-pathways-to-decent-jobs)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The concept-stage project document of June 2025 named development and rollout of a national social registry as a project activity, showing it as a build rather than an established system at appraisal ([project document](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099062025094012802/pdf/P511478-6a2b36f2-7288-40c0-9da6-f053dcf4873b.pdf)).<br><br>The World Bank board approved the US$200m sixth Productive Safety Net Project in March 2026, moving that registry into funded implementation ([approval](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2026/03/03/ethiopia-a-new-phase-of-support-for-safety-nets-will-foster-resilience-and-create-pathways-to-decent-jobs)).<br><br>The programme's existing management information system is further advanced: 444 of 492 woredas use it for programme tracking and 5.1 of 7.9 million beneficiaries' data had been synchronised to the federal system, with 252 woredas on electronic payments ([restructuring paper](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099102025134040722/pdf/P172479-f2834346-c6c0-4568-a19e-46544cf006e6.pdf)).<br><br>A registry and a programme management system are different things, and the base holds no enrolment figure for the registry itself.</details> | Advanced |
| Gabon | The [unified social register](https://agpgabon.ga/gabon-protection-sociale-presentation-du-premier-livrable-du-registre-social-unifie-et-du-programme-dacces-aux-agr/) presented its first deliverable in July 2026, and a second census of the economically weak begins fieldwork in September. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Technical work was finalised at a closing workshop in August 2025 with nothing delivered ([technical close](http://gouvernement.ga/2025/08/28/registre-social-unique-gabon-modernisation-de-la-protection-sociale)); the first deliverable was presented at Libreville in July 2026 alongside a commitment to full digitalisation of non-contributory social protection under the national plan ([first deliverable](https://agpgabon.ga/gabon-protection-sociale-presentation-du-premier-livrable-du-registre-social-unifie-et-du-programme-dacces-aux-agr/)).<br><br>An interministerial committee fixed the terms of a second census of economically weak citizens in July 2026, with fieldwork from September drawing on the population census and health-insurance records, because the existing social file is not reliable enough to target protection ([census terms](https://www.gabonreview.com/gabonais-economiquement-faibles-le-gouvernement-lance-un-nouveau-recensement-en-septembre/)).<br><br>The state's own source uses three different names for the register, and no beneficiary count, coverage or platform is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Gambia | The [social registry](https://blogs.worldbank.org/en/nasikiliza/the-gambia-social-registry-a-success-story-in-systems-building) holds 203,022 households across 40 of 43 districts, about 70 per cent of the population, with no coverage update since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It was built in two years and carries the national cash transfers ([registry](https://blogs.worldbank.org/en/nasikiliza/the-gambia-social-registry-a-success-story-in-systems-building)).<br><br>Its coverage is cited internationally as a systems-building success ([global comparison](https://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/099071525165029379)).<br><br>No update has been published since 2024, so a register described as near-complete is measured at a point two years old, and its relationship to the identity system launched in 2026 is not stated anywhere.</details> | No change |
| Ghana | The [household registry publishes registered-household figures by region](https://gnhr.mogcsp.gov.gh/dissemination.php) as at May 2026 and is the system of record behind the cash-grant reassessment. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry [publishes its registered-household figures by region, stated as at May 2026](https://gnhr.mogcsp.gov.gh/dissemination.php). The region-by-region tables are served as an interactive tool and did not render as text at capture, so the national total is not held.<br><br>2026-04 - a [registry-based nationwide reassessment enrolled 400,000 newly eligible households](http://leap.mogcsp.gov.gh/background.php) into the cash-grant programme - the registry working as the targeting instrument it exists to be.<br><br>What is not published is the registry's own coverage: how many households it holds against how many there are, when each region was last enumerated, or how a household corrects its record.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea | The [unified social register](https://fdsi.gov.gn/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/Presentation-RSU_FDSI_2025.pdf) has operated since a 2019 decree without a unique identifier. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It was created by decree in October 2019 and its position is unchanged ([register](https://fdsi.gov.gn/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/Presentation-RSU_FDSI_2025.pdf)).<br><br>No beneficiary count, coverage figure, update cycle or link to the civil registry is published, and the absence of an identifier is what the number now being announced would have to fix.</details> | No change |
| Guinea-Bissau | No social registry is described; payment runs through the [wage bill and pensions platform](https://www.imf.org/en/News/Articles/2024/10/02/cf-guinea-bissau-is-using-blockchain-to-boost-fiscal-transparency) rather than a beneficiary register. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Nothing in the base names a Guinean social registry or beneficiary database.<br><br>What exists is a payment rail on the ledger — the blockchain wage bill and pensions platform — and a financial system too thin to carry transfers widely: [one payment institution and one electronic money issuer nationally](https://www.bceao.int/sites/default/files/inline-files/4%20-%20Liste%20Guin%C3%A9e-Bissau_2026.pdf), and [22 per cent of women holding an account](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099037006302214402/pdf/IDU06117454a06d9c041600bf07075952fc5ee49.pdf).</details> | No change |
| Kenya | The register [gained a statutory framework](https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/2025/12/eng@2025-08-05) in 2025, with general regulations out for comment and backed by a lending operation. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Social Protection Act commenced in August 2025, establishing the National Board for Social Protection ([act](https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/2025/12/eng@2025-08-05)).<br><br>Draft general regulations went out for public participation in February 2026 ([notice](https://www.socialprotection.go.ke/socialprotectionpublicnotice)).<br><br>A USD 750 million development policy operation approved in June 2026 names those regulations among the actions it supports ([operation](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2026/06/30/world-bank-supports-kenyas-push-for-stronger-accountability-and-social-protection)). The register itself is not described anywhere in the base: no enrolment figure, no coverage rate and no account of how households enter or leave it.</details> | Advanced |
| Lesotho | [NISSA](https://dpi.africa.com/lesothos-digital-leap/) holds data on more than 90 per cent of households and has a child-centred redesign piloted in one district. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry holds data on more than 90 per cent of households, categorised by vulnerability, and a child-centred redesign was piloted in Mafeteng District under the Universal DPI Safeguards Framework ([redesign](https://dpi.africa.com/lesothos-digital-leap/)).<br><br>The registry and its companion management information system were procured together in 2024 ([procurement](https://lesotho.un.org/en/274430-request-proposals-national-information-system-social-assistance-nissa-social-registry-and)). No update to the coverage figure and no evaluation of the redesign is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Liberia | The [household social registry](https://realise.liberiasp.gov.lr/2026/05/18/government-of-liberia-presents-crisis-response-plan-to-world-bank/) holds over 442,000 poverty-ranked households, 35 per cent of the population. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [crisis response plan of May 2026](https://realise.liberiasp.gov.lr/2026/05/18/government-of-liberia-presents-crisis-response-plan-to-world-bank/) puts the registry at over 442,000 verified and poverty-ranked households, 35 per cent of the population, across 14 of the 15 counties, with transfers delivered through mobile money. The [implementation report of July 2025](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099072825133035694/pdf/P174417-17056a8a-f1db-4adb-af5f-982e8fb95722.pdf) carried the same order of magnitude with integration to other systems ongoing.<br><br>The project that carries it is scheduled to close in late 2026 with [no successor operation named](https://realise.liberiasp.gov.lr/2026/01/27/president-boakai-hails-social-protection-interventions-through-realise-project/), which is the open question the register faces.</details> | Advanced |
| Libya | A [Unified Social Registry](https://lana.gov.ly/post.php?lang=en&id=335111) draft was approved by the prime minister in June 2025 and nothing on its build has been published since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The draft was approved on 23 June 2025 ([approval](https://lana.gov.ly/post.php?lang=en&id=335111)).<br><br>No build, budget, timetable, lead agency or data source has been published in the fourteen months since, and no count of households or beneficiaries held in any existing social-protection list is available in this base.</details> | No change |
| Madagascar | The social registry [reached 2.6 million households](https://www.lexpress.mg/2026/05/recensement-26-millions-de-menages.html) by May 2026, passing its June target early. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A decree of September 2017 created the beneficiary register for non-contributory social protection, the legal basis of the current registry ([decree](https://natlex.ilo.org/dyn/natlex2/natlex2/files/download/106894/MDG-106894.pdf)).<br><br>The registry reached 2.6 million households by May 2026, up from 990,000 in February 2023, exceeding the ministry's target of 2.5 million by June, with registration continuing in 99 districts to December 2026 ([count](https://www.lexpress.mg/2026/05/recensement-26-millions-de-menages.html)).<br><br>A proposed USD 50 million additional financing would expand it across all 23 regions and extend the project to December 2027 ([financing](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099012326100017065/txt/P179466-b0e3c23f-4024-4dae-9418-88897afe4e50.txt)). The merger with the biometric identity database remains unfinished.</details> | Advanced |
| Malawi | A [six-month pilot is testing on-demand registry updates](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/giz-malawi_socialprotection-socialregistry-digitaltransformation-activity-7475071354057256960-qS1N), moving the registry from a static list to one households can change. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The standing arrangement is the [unified beneficiary registry, named the harmonised targeting mechanism by the national social protection policy](https://www.ilo.org/sites/default/files/2025-07/NATIONAL%20SOCIAL%20PROTECTION%20POLICY--.pdf).<br><br>2026-02 - a [programme at concept stage, 119.2 million US dollars, carries a dynamic delivery systems sub-component covering the social registry, an integrated management information system and digital payments](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099021926060033985/txt/P512324-615da32f-4b7c-4a0d-8ae8-4cb1ef5bcad9.txt).<br><br>2026-06 - a [six-month pilot in Balaka and Dowa began testing on-demand updates through district offices, a mobile short code and village development committees](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/giz-malawi_socialprotection-socialregistry-digitaltransformation-activity-7475071354057256960-qS1N). A registry compiled once ages from the day it closes; this is the first attempt on file to let a household change its own entry between sweeps.</details> | Advanced |
| Mali | An [enrolment campaign for the unified social register](https://apidev.org/campagne-denrolement-au-registre-social-unifie-rsu/) was running in November 2025, against a safety-net programme [reviewed for its operation amid conflict](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099515109162536671/pdf/IDU-20b55bfe-10dc-41b4-bcef-06b27137e3ce.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>No household count, coverage figure, targeting method or grievance route is published for the register, and the campaign's own reach is not stated ([campaign](https://apidev.org/campagne-denrolement-au-registre-social-unifie-rsu/)).<br><br>The technical paper reflects on the safety-net programme's operation amid fragility, conflict and violence ([paper](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099515109162536671/pdf/IDU-20b55bfe-10dc-41b4-bcef-06b27137e3ce.pdf)).<br><br>Against it, humanitarian caseload reporting is current: the refugee agency publishes quarterly operational updates and the food programme a monthly brief ([update](https://reliefweb.int/attachments/71934176-0890-4bed-8159-9c9c145773bc/Operational%20Update%20UNHCR_%20Mali_%20Q2-%202026.pdf)).</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritania | The [social register carries more than 2 million citizens](https://taazour.mr/archives/7559), and a [separate database for disaster-affected households](https://www.informea.org/en/content/legislation/decret-ndeg2025-162-du-15-octobre-2025-portant-creation-dune-base-de-donnees) was created by decree in October 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The delegation ran a coordination round to update the register across the Nouakchott wilayas in April 2026 ([update](https://ami.mr/fr/archives/294304)), and the safety-net programme behind it was re-established by decree in May 2024 ([decree](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/mau229659.pdf)).<br><br>Decree n. 2025-162 creates a special database for poor and vulnerable households affected by disasters ([disaster database](https://www.informea.org/en/content/legislation/decret-ndeg2025-162-du-15-octobre-2025-portant-creation-dune-base-de-donnees)); no operator, household count or relationship to the social register is stated for it.</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritius | The [social register](https://pmo.govmu.org/CabinetDecision/2009/Cabinet-Decisions-taken-on-26-March09.aspx) targeted the 2026-2027 budget's measures and a free-internet decision, with its platform being replaced. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register was decided in Cabinet in March 2009 ([decision](https://pmo.govmu.org/CabinetDecision/2009/Cabinet-Decisions-taken-on-26-March09.aspx)).<br><br>Cabinet used it in February 2026 to target a free-internet measure ([Cabinet](https://pmo.govmu.org/CabinetDecision/2026/Highlights%20of%20Cabinet%20Meeting%20Friday%2006%20February%202026.pdf)), and the 2026-2027 budget's measures are targeted on it ([budget](https://mauritiusassembly.govmu.org/mauritiusassembly/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/SPEECH-2026_2027.pdf)).<br><br>The social integration division reported on its performance for 2024-2025 ([report](https://mof.govmu.org/Documents/2026/Annual%20Report%20on%20Performance%20for%20Ministries%20and%20Department/9-1%20Social%20Integration/SOCIAL%20INTEGRATION%20DIVISION%20ANNUAL%20REPORT%202024-2025.pdf)). No enrolment figure for the register is published in any source held.</details> | Advanced |
| Morocco | The population and unified social registries hold [22 million of 39 million](https://timesofindia.indiatimes.com/technology/times-techies/theyre-taking-dpi-to-the-world/articleshow/131849408.cms) on a June 2026 account, built on an open-source identity platform adopted in 2018. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Enrolment stood at about half the population when the second identity and targeting project was approved in December 2024 ([approval](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2024/12/04/world-bank-greenlights-70m-boost-for-morocco-s-innovative-social-protection-delivery-systems)).<br><br>A June 2026 account gives 22 million of 39 million registered, on a platform the country adopted first, in 2018 ([account](https://timesofindia.indiatimes.com/technology/times-techies/theyre-taking-dpi-to-the-world/articleshow/131849408.cms)). The figure reaches the base through the press rather than a programme report, and no error, exclusion or appeal measure for the registries is held.</details> | Advanced, on a press figure |
| Mozambique | A [biometric register of humanitarian-aid recipients](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202608/mozambiques-digital-humanitarian-response-system-set-for-october-launch) completed validation for national launch in October 2026, built to interoperate with the national social-support database. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Validation of the beneficiary and humanitarian assistance management system is complete, with national launch stated for October 2026 after a pilot at Beira, at a costed investment of about 20 million metical ([validation](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202608/mozambiques-digital-humanitarian-response-system-set-for-october-launch), [register](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-aid-recipient-digital-register-to-be-launched-to-prevent-duplication/)).<br><br>It is built to interoperate with the national social-support database so responders can see who has and has not received aid and duplicate or fictitious registrations are curbed. No data-protection basis, retention rule or enrolment target is published, and the country has no data-protection statute.</details> | Advanced |
| Namibia | An [integrated beneficiary registry and social assistance system](https://hppii.gov.na/activities/activity-2-consolidation-of-social-grants-and-the-food-bank/) has run since 2022, and [15,825 old-age grants were suspended in June 2026](https://www.namibian.com.na/system-says-no-to-hungry-pensioners/) over unverified names. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry and the social assistance system were built under the second national prosperity plan, [launched in March 2022 and piloted to June 2022 at a cost of N$4.63 million](https://hppii.gov.na/activities/activity-2-consolidation-of-social-grants-and-the-food-bank/), with memoranda to exchange data with the pension fund, the social security commission, the tax administration system, the financial management system and the veterans system.<br><br>[At the May 2026 payment cycle 15,825 old-age grants were suspended because the names could not be verified against the national population registry](https://www.namibian.com.na/system-says-no-to-hungry-pensioners/); pensioners were turned away at pay points and told to bring an identity document and birth certificate to a regional office for reinstatement in the next cycle, with back payment promised. The ministry states beneficiaries must verify twice a year with a month's grace, and that it is planning to link its system to the home affairs ministry's for better death reporting ([suspensions](https://www.namibian.com.na/system-says-no-to-hungry-pensioners/)).<br><br>No figure for reinstatements, nor any count of beneficiaries on the register, is published.</details> | Regressed, the register is in use and 15,825 pensioners were cut off by a verification it failed to reach them about |
| Niger | The [unified social registry passed 500,000 households](https://www.lesahel.org/formation-des-cadres-nationaux-du-systeme-de-protection-sociale-du-niger-pour-la-mise-en-place-dun-systeme-durable-coherent-et-fonde-sur-les-droits-humains/) by September 2025, the first enrolment figure the base holds. <details><summary>Full record</summary>At a [capacity-building workshop on 8 September 2025](https://www.lesahel.org/formation-des-cadres-nationaux-du-systeme-de-protection-sociale-du-niger-pour-la-mise-en-place-dun-systeme-durable-coherent-et-fonde-sur-les-droits-humains/) a partner official stated that more than 500,000 vulnerable households are registered in the unified social registry and receive adapted services, with targeting to be expanded to the worst-affected zones.<br><br>The [third phase of the Sahel adaptive social protection programme, announced on 12 May 2026](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2026/05/12/sahel-adaptive-social-protection-program-launches-next-phase-accelerate-job-creation-and-boost-resilience-in-the-sahel) and running from 2025 to 2030, names strengthening social registries, digital payment mechanisms and secure data platforms among its priorities — a financing commitment rather than a delivered change.<br><br>The registry's governance rests on [founding acts from 2018 to 2022](https://rsu.gouv.ne/bibliotheques) under the prime minister's office. No coverage rate against the eligible population is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Nigeria | The [national social register expanded to 19.7m households](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202506/nigeria-amplifies-efforts-to-id-prisoners-expand-social-register) from 13m, using artificial intelligence, satellite imagery and telecoms data to find urban-slum households. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The expansion used artificial intelligence, satellite imagery and telecoms data to reach households the register had missed, and 74 per cent of prison inmates hold an identity number ([register](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202506/nigeria-amplifies-efforts-to-id-prisoners-expand-social-register)).<br><br>No exclusion, appeal or error measure for the register is published, and finding households by satellite and call-detail inference is a targeting method the data-protection regime does not separately address.</details> | Advanced |
| Rwanda | An [appeals process for the social registry](https://www.newtimes.co.rw/article/37878/news/rwanda/360000-claimsin-new-social-registryhandled-since-2024) was reported on in August 2026, and the correction rate is the social affairs directorate's own figure. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry has an appeals process and the correction rate published for it comes from the directorate that runs the registry rather than from an independent review ([appeals](https://www.newtimes.co.rw/article/37878/news/rwanda/360000-claimsin-new-social-registryhandled-since-2024)).<br><br>A social register with a working appeals route is more than most units in this frame can show. That the only measure of how well it works is the operator's own is the qualification.</details> | Advanced |
| Sao Tome and Principe | The social protection directorate [stated a coverage figure for the single social registry](https://rstp.st/2026/08/03/direcao-da-protecao-social-esclarece-sobre-cadastro-social-unico-stp/) and clarified that it is not a poverty count. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The single social register now covers more than 12,500 households, up from 8,000, and the vulnerable families programme is to expand from 5,000 to 6,000 households, on the prime minister's account ([statement](https://rstp.st/2026/08/26/pm-defende-reforco-da-protecao-social-para-apoiar-mais-pessoas-em-situacao-de-vulnerabilidade/)).<br><br>The clarification was issued in August 2026 ([clarification](https://rstp.st/2026/08/03/direcao-da-protecao-social-esclarece-sobre-cadastro-social-unico-stp/), [mission](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099102025185177863/pdf/P177158-d0ebd79d-6646-4e92-b9ae-1183b632c15a.pdf)).<br><br>An agency publicly correcting a misreading of its own registry figure is unusual in this frame and worth recording as such: a registry count measures who is enrolled, not who is poor, and conflating the two misstates both.<br><br>No enrolment total, update cycle or link to the identity system is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Senegal | The [single national social register rose from 542,000 to one million households](https://aps.sn/protection-sociale-le-rnu-passe-de-542-000-a-un-million-de-menages-responsable) over 2024, and no later figure is held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The growth was reported in December 2025 and ingested in July 2026, so it is a 2024 event rather than movement in the window ([register](https://aps.sn/protection-sociale-le-rnu-passe-de-542-000-a-un-million-de-menages-responsable), [brief](https://ipcig.org/publication/29291)).<br><br>The unified social-protection information system now being designed would sit above it, and the workshop that opened that design names data governance as an output rather than an input.</details> | Advanced |
| Seychelles | A [statutory duty since 2011](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/sey209065.pdf) to hold a beneficiary database has a platform behind it only since October 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Section 5(e) of the Agency for Social Protection Act 2011 requires the agency to maintain a national database of every applicant for and beneficiary of social assistance ([Act](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/sey209065.pdf)).<br><br>The Electronic Social Protection System announced in December 2024 for a third-quarter 2025 go-live ([announcement](https://asp.gov.sc/launching-of-esps-sensitisation-programme/)) launched internally on 15 October 2025 through the SeyID portal ([launch](https://www.nation.sc/articles/27935/asp-launches-digital-system)); in March 2026 Cabinet extended vendor support through 2026 and directed the ICT department to lead the transition to government ownership ([Cabinet](https://statehouse.gov.sc/news/6829/cabinet-business-wednesday-4th-march-2026)).<br><br>The lender's June 2025 report had the management information system behind the social registry still pending ([status](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099062625164514304/pdf/P168993-cdd171f8-8f75-415e-9083-d46ea92aa53d.pdf)).</details> | Advanced |
| Sierra Leone | The [social registry](https://www.nacsa.gov.sl/post/nacsa-ancollaborationd-statistics-sierra-leone-sign-amended-mou-to-strengthen-data-for-pssnye-proje) is being expanded towards national coverage, on data structures set out in a 2023 technical note. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry's development began in 2014 under the Social Safety Net project, and its data structures and operationalisation are described in a World Bank technical note ([note](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099060123071017741/txt/P1749420c981460708f7206678358ba026.txt)).<br><br>The social action commission and the statistics office signed an amended memorandum on 8 April 2026 to take it to national coverage ([memorandum](https://www.nacsa.gov.sl/post/nacsa-ancollaborationd-statistics-sierra-leone-sign-amended-mou-to-strengthen-data-for-pssnye-proje)).<br><br>No household count, coverage percentage or update cycle is published, so national coverage is an intention with no distance from it stated.</details> | Advanced |
| Somalia | The [unified social registry](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2025/10/28/over-738000-somalis-to-benefit-from-world-bank-supported-initiative-for-jobs-social-protection-and-climate-resilience) supports over 500,000 households and carries a USD 112 million operation to strengthen it and survey 12 million people. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The operation approved in September 2025 and announced in October is expected to benefit over 738,000 people, strengthening the registry and running a national socio-economic survey covering 12 million ([announcement](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2025/10/28/over-738000-somalis-to-benefit-from-world-bank-supported-initiative-for-jobs-social-protection-and-climate-resilience)).<br><br>Its appraisal names the registry and the delivery system as established government-owned platforms, and makes a registry survey, quality assurance and standard operating procedures conditions of effectiveness ([appraisal](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099091625171529631/pdf/BOSIB-00f4f571-1823-453b-9c9d-8aeb7c1ee72e.pdf)).<br><br>The ministry reported both supporting over 500,000 households at its final implementation mission in November 2025 ([mission](https://molsa.gov.so/agaasimaha-guud-ee-wasaaradda-shaqada-iyo-arrimaha-bulshada-mudane-yussuf-wadani-oo-furay-shirkii-ugu-dambeeya-ee-snhcp/)).</details> | Advanced |
| South Africa | The grant payment system [was confirmed in Parliament as administering payments to more than 18 million monthly beneficiaries](https://pmg.org.za/committee-question/31126/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-07-01 — [a ministerial reply confirmed the system administering grant payments to more than 18 million monthly beneficiaries](https://pmg.org.za/committee-question/31126/). Nothing was held on it before, so what moved is the record rather than the register.<br><br>It is the largest beneficiary count of any system on this ledger by a wide margin, and the base holds no architecture, uptime, modernisation programme or replacement plan for it.<br><br>That absence is the finding. The systems this report describes as advancing — the biometric register, the exchange pilot, the verification portal — all assume a social protection register they can query, and the register itself appears in the record only as an answer to a parliamentary question.</details> | No change, the system established in the record for the first time |
| South Sudan | [87,867 households, 615,069 people](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/feature/2025/09/18/cash-transfers-are-helping-women-create-businesses-and-regain-hope-in-south-sudan), biometrically registered across 15 counties - project-scoped, not universal. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry is a government-led management information system linked to biometric beneficiary registration, described in its financing paper as project-scoped rather than a standalone universal social registry ([paper](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099082324105031932/pdf/BOSIB1e1f76b710ba19bb915ace2051e7ff.pdf)).<br><br>It had registered and paid 87,867 households across 15 counties by September 2025 ([scale](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/feature/2025/09/18/cash-transfers-are-helping-women-create-businesses-and-regain-hope-in-south-sudan)).<br><br>Fifteen counties of eighty. No plan to widen it into a national registry, and no other programme drawing on it, is recorded.</details> | Advanced |
| Sudan | Ministers [reviewed progress on a Unified Social Registry](https://www.suna.sd/posts/lkaaa-ozary-yaazz-althol-alrkmy-oaslah-alkhdm-almdny-nho-dol-mossat-hdyth), a foundational social-protection targeting database, in April 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The review took place at a ministerial meeting on digital transformation and civil-service reform ([review](https://www.suna.sd/posts/lkaaa-ozary-yaazz-althol-alrkmy-oaslah-alkhdm-almdny-nho-dol-mossat-hdyth)).<br><br>No coverage, household count, targeting rule, data source or completion date is published. In a country where humanitarian cash transfers are a principal channel of assistance, a targeting database with no published design is the instrument that most needs one.</details> | Advanced |
| Tanzania | A [Zanzibar dynamic social registry](https://povertyevidence.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/DEEP-Tanzania_Challenge-Fund_Policy-Brief_Social-registery_Zanzibar.pdf) reached a briefing on what establishment would require — research commissioned to specify a registry rather than a decision to build one. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The briefing was held in August 2026, on a blueprint prepared the previous year ([briefing](https://povertyevidence.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/DEEP-Tanzania_Challenge-Fund_Policy-Brief_Social-registery_Zanzibar.pdf), [blueprint](https://povertyevidence.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/10/A-Blueprint-for-Creating-a-Zanzibar-Dynamic-Social-Registry-ZDSR.pdf)).<br><br>No custodian, budget, data-protection arrangement or timetable is stated. Distinguishing commissioned research from a build decision is the whole of what this cell can honestly record.</details> | Advanced |
| Togo | The registry is [75 per cent built](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099103024111041029/pdf/P178835-e634417e-785f-4b55-bb2c-063c891f286b.pdf) on the last report held, from September 2024; a policy framework was adopted around it. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Created by [decree of 24 April 2023](https://jo.gouv.tg/sites/default/files/JO/JOS_24_04_2023%20-%2068%20E%20ANNEE%20N%C2%B030%20BIS.pdf), it is held electronically, carries the unique identification number and a household number, is managed by the identity agency, and obliges administrations to base aid programmes on it and keep electronic beneficiary registers. An annual audit against the targeting rules is required.<br><br>The last supervision report held, from [30 September 2024](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099103024111041029/pdf/P178835-e634417e-785f-4b55-bb2c-063c891f286b.pdf), records the software 75 per cent complete, the data-exchange platform not in place and the beneficiary operations management system not yet adopted. Nothing later restates any of it.<br><br>The Council of Ministers adopted a [national social-protection policy](https://presidenceduconseil.gouv.tg/2025/12/17/conseil-des-ministres-du-17-decembre-2025/) by decree on 17 December 2025, the frame the registry now sits inside.</details> | No change |
| Tunisia | The [social registry](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/feature/2024/11/14/tunisians-share-how-their-life-changed-through-the-amen-program) carried more than 333,000 households on cash transfers as of December 2023, and a [social housing application platform](https://www.webmanagercenter.com/2026/08/04/570984/logements-sociaux-en-tunisie-une-plateforme-numerique-pour-accelerer-les-demandes-daccession-a-la-propriete/) has beneficiary-database interconnection still under way. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry figure is from December 2023 and nothing later is held ([registry](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/feature/2024/11/14/tunisians-share-how-their-life-changed-through-the-amen-program), [nexus](https://crvssystems.ca/sites/default/files/assets/files/SocialProtection_5_Tunisia_e_WEB.pdf)).<br><br>The housing platform's completion timetable was set at a follow-up meeting in August 2026, with beneficiary-database interconnection still under way ([housing](https://www.webmanagercenter.com/2026/08/04/570984/logements-sociaux-en-tunisie-une-plateforme-numerique-pour-accelerer-les-demandes-daccession-a-la-propriete/)).<br><br>A social identifier is separately being worked on inside a social-security reform, so three social-data systems are in motion and none states how it relates to the others.</details> | No change |
| Zambia | The social cash transfer [reached 1,555,840 households in February 2026](https://www.zambiamonitor.com/social-cash-transfer-programme-reaches-1-56-million-vulnerable-households-zambian-govt-claims/), up from 1.3 million, after 305,000 were added against a plan for 250,000. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The ministry reported the nationwide scale-up complete in February 2026, taking coverage from 1.3 million to 1,555,840 households](https://www.zambiamonitor.com/social-cash-transfer-programme-reaches-1-56-million-vulnerable-households-zambian-govt-claims/).<br><br>[More than 300,000 households were added where 250,000 had been planned for 2025](https://www.zambiamonitor.com/social-cash-transfer-programme-reaches-1-56-million-vulnerable-households-zambian-govt-claims/), which the minister attributed to the reach of the sensitisation campaign and to demand.<br><br>The count is the ministry's own, given at a press briefing, with no independent verification held, and it sits against a unified national social registry approved by cabinet in April 2025 that is still in early implementation.</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | The [social registry](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099030926024025866/txt/P514401-e5a07636-f85a-4936-8383-b57dbd46cf7c.txt) completed first-phase enumeration in December 2025 and has a funded second phase aiming at a further 4 per cent of the population. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The first phase of enumeration was completed in Mangwe district in December 2025 and extends to Nkayi and Mudzi, aiming at about 2 per cent of the population, roughly 80,000 households ([project document](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099030926024025866/txt/P514401-e5a07636-f85a-4936-8383-b57dbd46cf7c.txt)).<br><br>A proposed second phase would enumerate at least a further 4 per cent, about 160,000 households, with US$1.7m for enumeration and US$300,000 for IT and capacity building ([project document](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099030926024025866/txt/P514401-e5a07636-f85a-4936-8383-b57dbd46cf7c.txt)).<br><br>Field collection began publicly in Nkayi district in March 2026, gathering demographics, education, employment, housing, land ownership, access to services and economic activity ([launch](https://www.heraldonline.co.zw/zim-rolls-out-nationwide-survey-to-strengthen-social-protection-systems/), [survey](https://zimstat.co.zw/2025-social-registry-survey-press-release/)).<br><br>No enrolled-household count, hosting arrangement or completion date is held, and the registry has not yet carried a live targeting decision.</details> | Advanced |

## Electoral register

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | The 2025 revision closed with [24,503,060 registered electors](https://www.aps.dz/fr/algerie/actualite-nationale/mkechga1-plus-de-24-5-millions-d-electeurs-inscrits), and [the system was reformed by organic law](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/jo-francais/2026/F2026024.pdf) in April 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register's legal regime is set by [the organic ordinance of 10 March 2021 on the electoral system](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/jo-francais/2021/F2021017.pdf) - a statute establishing the regime, not merely a fact about the register.<br><br>2026-01-14 - the electoral authority announced the completed 2025 periodic revision: [24,503,060 registered electors](https://www.aps.dz/fr/algerie/actualite-nationale/mkechga1-plus-de-24-5-millions-d-electeurs-inscrits).<br><br>2026-04-04 - an organic law reformed the system around it: [one third of candidate-list seats reserved for women, at least one half for candidates under 40, and state financing for independent young candidates](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/jo-francais/2026/F2026024.pdf). No biometric or deduplication measure for the register itself is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Angola | A proof-of-life operation [runs 254 of a planned 634 counters against a universe of 16,707,455 citizens](https://www.novojornal.co.ao/politica/detalhe/partidos-da-oposicao-lancam-alerta-sobre-obstaculos-burocraticos-que-ameacam-exclusao-massiva-de-cidadaos-do-processo-de-actualizacao-do-registo-eleitoral-74052.html), and four opposition parties demand an independent audit. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-06-15 — [the proof-of-life operation to update the electoral register opened](https://novojornal.co.ao/politica/detalhe/eleicoes-2027-arranca-hoje-a-prova-de-vida-para-actualizacao-do-registo-eleitoral-72520.html). By August, [254 of a planned 634 counters were live across all 21 provinces and the diaspora, with 10,747 field workers against a target universe of 16,707,455 citizens, and over 730,000 updated nationally on the opposition's count](https://www.novojornal.co.ao/politica/detalhe/partidos-da-oposicao-lancam-alerta-sobre-obstaculos-burocraticos-que-ameacam-exclusao-massiva-de-cidadaos-do-processo-de-actualizacao-do-registo-eleitoral-74052.html).<br><br>The count is the problem. The electoral administration has published no enrolment figure since the operation opened, so the only national number the base holds comes from a political declaration by four parties that are contesting the operation. They allege administrative discretion, uneven deployment of registration brigades and recurring biometric-system failures, and say the administration refuses to release statistics disaggregated by province, municipality and commune, which blocks any audit of the register's real flow; the electoral commission had earlier refused accreditation to a civic monitor. None of it has been adjudicated and no official series exists to test it against.<br><br>At provincial level, [one government reports over 40,000 residents updated and is pressing mining-sector workers to register through one-stop service desks](https://angop.ao/noticias/politica/governadora-apela-a-actualizacao-dos-dados-eleitorais/). Ahead of all of it, [a law replaces in-person registration with automatic enrolment from the civil identity database, with the voter card used for the last time in 2027](https://executivedigest.sapo.pt/servicos-de-informacoes-angolanos-acedem-a-dados-biometricos-sem-controlo-judicial-relatorio/).</details> | Advanced, contested |
| Benin | The population register [compiled and managed the roll for the April 2026 presidential poll](https://techreviewafrica.com/public/news/4880/ecowas-and-african-union-assess-benins-electoral-preparedness-ahead-of-presidential-poll), with regional observer missions satisfied on preparedness. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The register is the single source of electoral data under the 2017 law, and platforms to modernise the electoral process were presented in August 2025](https://www.gouv.bj/article/3188/modernisation-processus-electoral-anip-devoile-plateformes-numeriques), untested at a presidential poll.<br><br>2026-04-12 — [the agency compiled and managed the list for the presidential poll, and regional and continental observer missions assessed its preparedness and pronounced themselves satisfied](https://techreviewafrica.com/public/news/4880/ecowas-and-african-union-assess-benins-electoral-preparedness-ahead-of-presidential-poll). [Card fees were waived, with web, mobile, unstructured-menu and photo-update channels open to polling morning](https://fraternite.bj/scrutin-du-12-avril-lidentification-des-electeurs-facilitee-jusqua-la-derniere-heure-par-lanip/).<br><br>It is the clearest test any system on this ledger has been put to, and it passed on the observers' assessment. The one published intervention against it came from the data protection authority, which required extraneous fields stripped from the roll before publication, reported under data protection.</details> | Advanced |
| Botswana | P50,000,000 [was appropriated for electronic voter registration](https://botswanaspeaks.gov.bw/media/COMMITTEE%20OF%20SUPPPLY%20SPEECHES/IEC.pdf), with government [committing to a reform package built on one-time registration and a verified electronic roll](https://allafrica.com/stories/202608190305.html). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08-19 — [government committed in Parliament to an electoral-reform package built on one-time voter registration, the national identity card and a verified electronic voters' roll accessible to political parties](https://allafrica.com/stories/202608190305.html), [after the commission pressed for digital reform in June](https://www.mmegi.bw/news/iec-pushes-for-digital-election-reforms/news). [P50,000,000 is appropriated within the commission's development budget](https://botswanaspeaks.gov.bw/media/COMMITTEE%20OF%20SUPPPLY%20SPEECHES/IEC.pdf) — the first time the voter register carries a price in any document held. The package has no bill, timetable or procurement on record.<br><br>2026-07-06 — [an elections management system is built and under extensive testing, replacing platforms developed in the 1990s, with deployment planned for the 2029 general election](https://www.sundaystandard.info/electoral-transparency-credibility-and-the-dark-shadow-of-the-evm-in-botswana/), and no supplier, cost or acceptance date held. The commission's secretary separately states, as personal opinion, that voters do not want electronic voting machines.<br><br>The reform rests on the identity card, whose replacement service is failing, reported under digital identity.</details> | Advanced |
| Burundi | Preparations for the 2027 electoral register [are under way and contested, with opposition parties demanding suspension](https://www.burunditimes.com/burundi-opposition-parties-challenge-cenis-2027-election-preparations-president-calls-for-parental-dialogue/); no biometric card is in use. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-05-09 — [opposition parties demanded suspension of the electoral commission's preparations for the 2027 register](https://www.burunditimes.com/burundi-opposition-parties-challenge-cenis-2027-election-preparations-president-calls-for-parental-dialogue/). No baseline was held before it and the substance of the objection is not resolved on the record.<br><br>The biometric card that might have underpinned the roll is not in use: it [was confirmed as unavailable for the 2025 enrolment](https://burundi-eco.com/enrolement-des-electeurs-de-2025-la-carte-nationale-didentite-biometrique-ne-sera-pas-disponible) and [is still waiting on its governing instrument](https://www.burunditimes.com/burundi-unveils-biometric-national-id-system-awaits-law-before-full-rollout/).<br><br>So the register is being prepared on the old basis, under objection, while the credential intended to modernise it sits ready and unusable. No enrolment figure, audit or de-duplication statement is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Cameroon | A [register of about 8.2 million voters went through deduplication before the October 2025 vote](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202509/cameroon-signals-urgency-on-biometric-voter-id-collection-as-presidential-vote-nears), with hundreds of thousands of cards uncollected. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register ran on third-generation kits at the window's opening, the elections board having [acquired a thousand biometric enrolment kits](https://lejour.cm/listes-electorales-elecam-acquiert-1000-nouveaux-kits-denrolement/).<br><br>2025-09-11 - [about 8.2 million voters were under deduplication ahead of the 12 October presidential vote, with hundreds of thousands of biometric cards uncollected - more than 350,000 in the Littoral region, about 50,000 of 115,000 collected in the South, 31,861 of 66,515 distributed in the South West - some printed in 2018 or earlier, the board warning that a registered voter without a card cannot vote](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202509/cameroon-signals-urgency-on-biometric-voter-id-collection-as-presidential-vote-nears).<br><br>No post-election collection figure, deduplication result or audit is on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Cape Verde | [419,755 electors](https://boe.incv.cv/Bulletins/Download/34015) on the roll at 28 April 2026, published by constituency in the gazette. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [global electorate map](https://boe.incv.cv/Bulletins/Download/34015) puts the roll at 419,755 electors at its reference date of 28 April 2026, broken down by constituency.<br><br>It reaches the base as a republication: the original gazette text of 28 April was found inexact and the full corrected table was reissued two days later, which is the register's own correction mechanism working in public.</details> | No change |
| Central African Republic | The definitive electoral list [reached 2,398,158 registered voters, 757,075 of them registered in 2025](https://french.xinhuanet.com/20250825/216678a5ca894a1d915b2f5528ff0973/c.html), used across 3,910 voting centres. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-08-23 — [the electoral authority published a definitive list of 2,398,158 registered voters, 1,641,083 carried from the 2020 revision plus 757,075 registered in 2025](https://french.xinhuanet.com/20250825/216678a5ca894a1d915b2f5528ff0973/c.html), [used on 28 December 2025 across 3,910 voting centres](https://fr.apanews.net/news/rca-touadera-elu-au-premier-tour-avec-7615-des-voix-resultats-provisoires/).<br><br>Adding almost half again to the roll in one revision is the largest registration operation on this ledger. The defect is small and telling: [the authority's own provisional results state a turnout percentage computed on an electorate 5,212 lower than its definitive list](https://fr.apanews.net/news/rca-touadera-elu-au-premier-tour-avec-7615-des-voix-resultats-provisoires/), which no source reconciles.<br><br>The roll rests on the identity card reported above, whose issuance at one prefecture is reported as religiously targeted and informally priced.</details> | Advanced |
| Chad | [8,204,830 electors](https://www.lepaystchad.com/elections-legislatives-et-communales-au-tchad-les-chiffres-cles-du-corps-electoral/) were registered for the December 2024 polls; a [revision owed for June 2026](https://atpe.td/legislatives-et-senatoriales-partielles-lange-peut-elle-garantir-un-scrutin-apaise/) is unconfirmed. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The most recent published national figure is 8,204,830 registered electors for the coupled legislative, provincial and communal elections of 28 and 29 December 2024, 8,173,058 of them inside Chad and 31,772 abroad ([figures](https://www.lepaystchad.com/elections-legislatives-et-communales-au-tchad-les-chiffres-cles-du-corps-electoral/)).<br><br>Partial legislative and senatorial elections of 20 and 21 June 2026, convoked by decret of 22 May 2026, put the electoral agency under an explicit obligation to revise the register within the deadline; nothing held confirms the revision was carried out ([obligation](https://atpe.td/legislatives-et-senatoriales-partielles-lange-peut-elle-garantir-un-scrutin-apaise/)).<br><br>In June 2026 the permanent political dialogue framework published a roadmap whose second strategic objective runs from a reliable electoral register to equitable party financing ([roadmap](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/06/13/reforme-du-systeme-electoral-transparence-des-elections-dialogue-inclusif-le-cpdp-publie-sa-feuille-de-route/)).</details> | Stalled |
| Comoros | The file is [computerised and centralised by article 7 of the 2023 electoral code](https://justice.gouv.km/texte/code-electoral/); an [audit sits in the 2029 roadmap](https://www.rfi.fr/fr/afrique/20260418-comores-fin-du-s%C3%A9minaire-d-%C3%A9valuation-portant-sur-la-cr%C3%A9dibilit%C3%A9-des-scrutins-%C3%A9lectoraux). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [electoral code promulgated on 8 March 2023 computerises and centralises the national voter file at article 7 and admits the biometric identity card as one of two identity documents for registration at article 11](https://justice.gouv.km/texte/code-electoral/).<br><br>2026-04 - a [three-day seminar evaluating the 2023 to 2025 electoral cycles produced a roadmap to 2029 including an independent audit of the file and a single electoral code](https://www.rfi.fr/fr/afrique/20260418-comores-fin-du-s%C3%A9minaire-d-%C3%A9valuation-portant-sur-la-cr%C3%A9dibilit%C3%A9-des-scrutins-%C3%A9lectoraux).<br><br>2026-07 - a [national workshop covered the file's revision, the interior minister announcing first implementing activities from August 2026 over a twelve to eighteen month rollout](https://www.comoresinfos.net/processus-electoral-les-acteurs-appellent-a-davantage-de-transparence-et-de-concertation/).<br><br>No registration figure, audit finding or revision result is on file. The two records are both plans, ten weeks apart.</details> | No change, an audit and a revision are both scheduled and neither has begun on the record |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The [definitive 2025 electoral list was closed by the electoral commission on 2 June 2025](https://cei.ci/point-de-presse-relatif-a-larret-de-la-liste-electorale-definitive-2025/) after revision and a public audit. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-06-02 - the electoral commission's central commission [closed the definitive list, following the revision of 19 October to 17 November 2024 under decret 2024-792, deletion of the deceased and of those stripped of civil rights on lists supplied by the justice and interior ministries, and a public audit of the provisional list, the commission stating that residual imperfections are neutralised by the register's biometrics](https://cei.ci/point-de-presse-relatif-a-larret-de-la-liste-electorale-definitive-2025/).<br><br>The release states no enrolment total, so the register's size is not on file. Nothing states how the justice and interior lists are supplied, on what cycle, or whether the biometric deduplication is audited by anyone outside the commission.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | The [voter card is produced from the register of natural persons by law](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-n39-an-19-8eme-l-portant-identification-des-personnes-physiques-en-republique-de-djibouti-creation-du-numero-national-didentification-et-etablissement-dun-registre-national/); no electoral roll or its size is held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The only statement the base holds is statutory: the founding law [makes the register of natural persons the unique reference for producing every secure national document, the voter card among them, alongside the identity card, passport and driving licence](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-n39-an-19-8eme-l-portant-identification-des-personnes-physiques-en-republique-de-djibouti-creation-du-numero-national-didentification-et-etablissement-dun-registre-national/).<br><br>Nothing dated inside the period touches it.<br><br>What is absent is the register itself: no electoral roll, no registered-voter count, no revision cycle, no instrument governing its compilation, and nothing on whether the identification number has been applied to it. The [biometric identity card issued in the period](https://www.lanation.dj/carte-nationale-didentite-biometrique-dans-les-coulisses-de-la-nouvelle-identite-nationale/) carries no stated electoral use.</details> | No change |
| Egypt | [69,891,913 electors were registered at the 2025 House elections](https://gate.ahram.org.eg/News/5457664.aspx), of whom about 22 million voted. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register is governed by [the 2014 law on the exercise of political rights](https://www.elections.eg/images/pdfs/laws/PoliRights2014-45_En.pdf).<br><br>2025-10-17 - the electoral authority [launched an updated voter database through its own site and application, letting a voter query their polling committee and its location](https://www.elwatannews.com/news/details/8156294).<br><br>2026-01-10 - final statistics put [69,891,913 electors registered in the database at the 2025 House elections, with about 22 million votes cast](https://gate.ahram.org.eg/News/5457664.aspx). No deduplication, biometric or roll-maintenance measure for the register is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Equatorial Guinea | The [2022 decree](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/decreto_por_el_que_se_dispone_el_levantamiento_del_censo_electoral) orders a periodic electoral census with no biometrics and no digital register. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [decree](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/decreto_por_el_que_se_dispone_el_levantamiento_del_censo_electoral) is the operative instrument and is explicit in what it does not provide: no biometric capture, no digital register, a periodic census taken as an exercise rather than a maintained roll.<br><br>Nothing since records a change of method, and no register size is published.</details> | No change |
| Eritrea | The electoral law provides for a voter register, and nothing records one ever having been compiled ([electoral law](https://constitutionnet.org/sites/default/files/Eritrea%20Election%20Laws.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Eritrean electoral law establishes an independent electoral commission responsible for preparing and administering the voter register, issuing voting cards, and closing and publicly displaying the register for inspection ([electoral law](https://constitutionnet.org/sites/default/files/Eritrea%20Election%20Laws.pdf)).<br><br>The base holds no record of a national election held under it, no commission constituted and no register compiled. The hosted text of the law is itself undated.<br><br>This is a register that exists as a statutory design and not as a system.</details> | No change |
| Eswatini | The commission runs [continuous voter registration](https://eswatinipositivenews.online/by-elections-registrations-begin-tomorrow/), and a proposed compulsory chief code and residency rule are under review. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Continuous voter registration was operationalised from 19 to 28 September 2025 for two by-election divisions, open to newly eligible voters and to prospective candidates ([registration](https://eswatinipositivenews.online/by-elections-registrations-begin-tomorrow/)).<br><br>The commission's own account of the process describes voter registration on an optical-mark-recognition identification system, production of a preliminary register and validation at chiefdom level ([process](https://www.elections.org.sz/2023/electoral-process/)).<br><br>A proposal to make the chief code compulsory at registration, alongside a six-month residency rule, divided residents at Ezulwini and is under commission review ([debate](https://times.co.sz/41170/news/chief-code-rule-divides-ezulwini-residents/)).<br><br>The same code is the one the home affairs ministry asked citizens to correct on their identity documents in August 2026. No registered-voter count is held at any date on this base.</details> | Advanced |
| Ethiopia | The electoral roll and its registration framework rest on the 2019 electoral proclamation, unchanged in the window ([proclamation](https://nebe.org.et/sites/default/files/Ethiopian-Electoral-Proclamtion-No-1162.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The controlling instrument is the 2019 electoral proclamation, which defines the electoral roll and the voter and candidate registration framework and confirms the national election board as the impartial election administration ([proclamation](https://nebe.org.et/sites/default/files/Ethiopian-Electoral-Proclamtion-No-1162.pdf)).<br><br>The voter and candidate registration system and the count of voters registered for the seventh general election are carried on the ledger from April 2026 and nothing in the window moved them.<br><br>The base holds no statement on whether the electoral roll is checked against the civil register or the national digital identity.</details> | No change |
| Gabon | An [electoral-roll verification platform](https://www.interieur.gouv.ga/9-actualites/601-verifiez-votre-inscription-et-consultez-votre-centre-de-vote-en-) is live for the 2025 cycle, keyed on the personal identification number. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The platform was published by the digital agency for the electoral cycle and lets a voter check the roll against the identification number ([verification platform](https://www.interieur.gouv.ga/9-actualites/601-verifiez-votre-inscription-et-consultez-votre-centre-de-vote-en-)).<br><br>No usage figure is published at either end of the window, no register size is held, and the source page is undated so the position carries year precision only.</details> | No change |
| Gambia | [212,095 supplementary registrations were announced against 179,445 records](https://gambiaj.com/news/politics/elections/political-parties-renew-concerns-over-iec-voter-register-discrepancies/) on the data given to parties, with 1,600 objections filed. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The commission attributes the gap to unverified processing outputs; the discrepancy was raised by opposition parties at a stakeholder forum ahead of the December 2026 presidential election, and no reconciled figure has been published ([discrepancy](https://gambiaj.com/news/politics/elections/political-parties-renew-concerns-over-iec-voter-register-discrepancies/)).<br><br>The objections, on eligibility and residency, are being heard by revising court magistrates before the provisional register merges with the 2021 register; the chairman states the register will be credible once the court finishes, that the names removed were multiple registrations covered by an earlier amnesty, and that no eligible registrant has been omitted ([assurance](https://thepoint.gm/africa/gambia/headlines/iec-chair-assures-gambians-of-credible-voter-register-ahead-of-2026-polls), [objections](https://thepoint.gm/africa/gambia/headlines/iec-received-1600-objections-in-voter-registration-njie)).<br><br>Partial digitalisation of the December polls was announced in March 2026, covering polling-station mapping, results transmission and tablets, with no procurement, test or audit arrangement published ([digitalisation](https://standard.gm/iec-introduces-partial-digitalisation-ahead-december-polls)).</details> | Advanced |
