| Ghana | The certified 2024 register held [18,774,159 valid voters, all but 1,870 with biometric data](https://gna.org.gh/2024/11/ec-releases-summary-2024-certified-voters-register/), and a [reform programme was set out in August 2026](https://ghananewsonline.com.gh/ec-outlines-sweeping-reforms-to-strengthen-ghanas-electoral-system/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The certified 2024 register carries [18,774,159 valid voters, 18,640,811 of them with biometric data, 131,487 special voters and 1,870 without biometric data](https://gna.org.gh/2024/11/ec-releases-summary-2024-certified-voters-register/). The commission's page did not render its own table as text; the figures come from a same-day wire account of the release.<br><br>The register is a funded outcome indicator in the commission's budget, [rising from 17.03m in 2023 to 18.77m in 2024](https://mofep.gov.gh/sites/default/files/pbb-estimates/2026/2026-PBB-EC.pdf).<br><br>2026-08-12 - the commission [set out a medium-term reform programme of upgraded biometric registration equipment, an election-technology inventory system, continuous registration procedures and detection of deceased and duplicate records](https://ghananewsonline.com.gh/ec-outlines-sweeping-reforms-to-strengthen-ghanas-electoral-system/). None of it is recorded as delivered.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea | The [permanent biometric electoral file](https://mediaguinee.com/2026/04/fichier-electoral-la-dge-annonce-pres-de-7-millions-delecteurs-pour-les-elections-couplees-du-31-mai/) held 6.95m electors across 23,681 stations for the May 2026 coupled elections. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It stood at 6.77m at the September 2025 referendum ([earlier file](https://mediaguinee.com/2025/08/guinee-67-millions-delecteurs-inscrits-conakry-et-kankan-concentrent-presque-la-moitie/)) and at 6.95m with 23,681 stations by April 2026 ([current file](https://mediaguinee.com/2026/04/fichier-electoral-la-dge-annonce-pres-de-7-millions-delecteurs-pour-les-elections-couplees-du-31-mai/)).<br><br>It is the base's most precisely stated register, and its precision comes from an election timetable rather than from a registry publication.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea-Bissau | The updated electoral register [was handed to the elections commission on 18 August 2026 recording 966,152 voters](https://ang.gw/referendo-30-de-agosto-gtape-entrega-cadernos-eleitorais-a-cne/), unchanged in size from the last update. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08-18 — the electoral register [was handed to the elections commission in both physical and electronic form ahead of the 30 August referendum, recording 966,152 registered voters](https://ang.gw/referendo-30-de-agosto-gtape-entrega-cadernos-eleitorais-a-cne/).<br><br>2026-08-17 — the operation behind it [issued 25,146 replacement voter cards over thirty days from 13 July, worked by 51 brigades covering every region](https://ang.gw/gtape-anuncia-emissao-de-25-146-cartoes-do-eleitor-da-2a-via/).<br><br>The electorate total is unchanged from the previous update, so the operation replaced cards rather than added registrants. No audit, no de-duplication statement and no link between the register and the biometric identity system is published. It is the only register on this ledger that moved in the window.</details> | Advanced |
| Kenya | [KSh 74.8bn was appropriated](https://nation.africa/kenya/news/politics/50-million-cameras-the-election-kenya-did-not-have-in-1992-5552024) for 2027 election technology, while two bills would remove the duty to livestream polling-station results. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Treasury allocated KSh 41.3bn and Parliament approved a further KSh 33.5bn; it is an appropriation and not a spend, with no procurement or equipment breakdown published ([appropriation](https://nation.africa/kenya/news/politics/50-million-cameras-the-election-kenya-did-not-have-in-1992-5552024)).<br><br>The two bills would remove the commission's statutory duty to create a livestreaming mechanism; neither is enacted and the base does not establish how they relate procedurally ([bills](https://www.standardmedia.co.ke/politics/article/2001554383/recipe-for-rigging-battle-looms-over-plan-to-scrap-iebcs-livestreaming-of-election-results)).<br><br>A clause vesting ownership of the election technology's intellectual property and access to the election servers in the commission passed the Senate in December 2024 and has sat in committee with no report since February 2025; it is set against the 2017 refusal to open commission servers hosted abroad and a vendor's 2022 refusal of server access on intellectual-property grounds ([server access](https://nation.africa/kenya/news/politics/who-will-control-elections-tech-and-servers-bill-languishes-as-2027-nears-5569602)).<br><br>Optional iris capture was added to voter-registration kits under a roughly KSh 7bn upgrade, with nothing on file since October 2025 on deployment or enrolment ([iris](https://nation.africa/kenya/news/why-iebc-is-using-iris-identification-technology-5230162)).<br><br>Sector guidance issued in 2024 binds every organisation that touches voter data, from the commission down to civil-society registration groups, with no enforcement action under it on record as unsolicited political messaging spreads ([guidance](https://www.odpc.go.ke/wp-content/uploads/2024/02/ODPC-Guidance-Notes-for-Electoral-Purposes.pdf), [messaging](https://www.the-star.co.ke/news/2026-08-18-political-sms-raise-questions-over-kenyans-personal-data)).<br><br>The commission confirmed an [engagement with the registration bureau on voter data and identity verification](https://www.iebc.or.ke/news/?Press_release_-_Collaboration_with_State_Agencies_on_Managing_the_Register_of_Voters=), with no data-sharing agreement published.</details> | Mixed, the appropriation nearly doubled while two bills would remove the livestreaming duty and one to vest server ownership has stalled |
| Lesotho | A [voter registration strategy was formally endorsed in June 2026](https://www.idea.int/news/lesotho-takes-strategic-step-towards-inclusive-electoral-participation), with constituency readiness assessments under way. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2013 — [the electoral commission’s report describes the constitutional mandate for the general register of electors and the 2011 electoral Act](https://iec.org.ls/storage/2025/07/26-May-2012-National-Assembly-Report-V6-2014.pdf).<br><br>2026-06-15 — [constituency readiness assessments began ahead of registration intensification, with a commissioner deployment framework covering register audits](https://iec.org.ls/storage/2026/06/Press-release-on-constituency-assessment_15June2026.pdf).<br><br>2026-06-25 — [the commission, government and international partners formally endorsed the voter registration strategy](https://www.idea.int/news/lesotho-takes-strategic-step-towards-inclusive-electoral-participation).<br><br>2026-06-04 — [a Bill before the Senate would tie registration and verification to the national identity number](https://senate.parliament.ls/2026/07/13/hansard-4-june-2026/).<br><br>No registered-elector count is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Liberia | The [electoral commission](https://analystliberiaonline.com/nec-plans-tough-measures-for-2029-sets-tougher-party-compliance-measures-biometric-voter-registration-and-higher-candidate-fees/) will assess the 2023 roll first; any renewal would be fully biometric from January 2027. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The commission's chairman [set out in August 2026](https://analystliberiaonline.com/nec-plans-tough-measures-for-2029-sets-tougher-party-compliance-measures-biometric-voter-registration-and-higher-candidate-fees/) that the viability of the 2023 voter roll will be assessed first, and that if a fresh exercise is needed it would be fully biometric with same-day card issue over satellite connectivity, with intensive preparation from January 2027 ahead of the 2029 general elections.<br><br>Nothing has begun. The register in use remains the 2023 roll, and the base holds no procurement, budget line or technical specification for a biometric exercise.</details> | No change, a stated intention with no exercise begun |
| Libya | [Biometric voter verification](https://libyareview.com/47455/libya-implements-biometric-verification-in-municipal-council-elections) was piloted in four municipalities at the 2024 municipal elections and no extension has been published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The commission used biometric verification in four municipalities at the municipal council elections of August 2024 ([pilot](https://libyareview.com/47455/libya-implements-biometric-verification-in-municipal-council-elections)).<br><br>No evaluation, no extension to national elections and no register size have been published since, so the register's coverage and its link to the national identification number cannot be established from this base.</details> | No change |
| Madagascar | A [full reconstitution of the electoral roll](https://2424.mg/election-la-ceni-lance-les-operations-de-recensement-pour-la-refonte-de-la-liste-electorale-avant-les-scrutins-de-2027/) opened in July 2026, running four months and gated on presentation of a national identity card. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The reconstitution began at Talata Volonondry and runs to 24 November 2026 through five registration channels, replacing a roll the commission records as contested ([reconstitution](https://2424.mg/election-la-ceni-lance-les-operations-de-recensement-pour-la-refonte-de-la-liste-electorale-avant-les-scrutins-de-2027/)).<br><br>The commission states that an unregistered citizen may not observe polling in their own place of residence, lodge a complaint or contest results, which makes the identity-card gate a condition on political participation and not only on voting.</details> | Advanced |
| Malawi | [6,500 electoral management devices](https://times.mw/the-year-ict-sector-grappled-with-implementation-woes/) were deployed for voter verification at the September 2025 elections, with offline glitches reported at rural polling stations. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The voter register is held electronically and the devices were deployed for verification at the elections of 16 September 2025 ([deployment](https://times.mw/the-year-ict-sector-grappled-with-implementation-woes/)).<br><br>Two million voters had been registered using offline digital identities in 2024. The commission had refused an observer coalition electronic access to the roll in June 2025 ([refusal](https://africabrief.substack.com/p/mec-rejects-observer-coalitions-electronic)), so the register is digital for the commission's own use and not for scrutiny of it.</details> | Advanced, offline failures recorded |
| Mali | The [annual revision of the electoral lists ran from 1 October to 31 December 2025](https://mali24.info/mali-la-revision-annuelle-des-listes-electorales-de-2025-prevue-du-1er-octobre-au-31-decembre/), and the electoral authority [trained agents on a mobile register application](https://malijet.com/elections-presidentielles-et-legisliatives-au-mali/307835-mali--l-aige-forme-ses-agents-sur-le-logiciel--elector-mobile-.html) in February 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The electoral law of 24 June 2022 is the instrument behind both and its text is held from the official journal ([law](https://sgg-mali.ml/JO/2022/mali-jo-2022-18.pdf)).<br><br>No registered-voter count, revision outturn or relationship to the national identification number is published, so the process is dated and the register's size and quality are not.</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritania | [1,939,343 registered voters](https://conseil-constitutionnel.mr/fr/?p=1711) on the Constitutional Council's proclamation of the 2024 presidential result. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The figure is the roll as it stood at the 2024 presidential election, taken from the Council's own deliberation proclaiming the definitive results ([proclamation](https://conseil-constitutionnel.mr/fr/?p=1711)).<br><br>No later revision, audit or statement of the register's relationship to the national population register is on the record, so the base holds one dated count and no series.</details> | No change, a single count with no earlier figure behind it |
| Mauritius | The register is compiled annually from a [statutory house-to-house inquiry](https://electoral.govmu.org/oec/?p=8503); no digital enrolment route is described. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Representation of the People Act requires a house-to-house inquiry for the compilation of the register, carried out again in 2026 ([inquiry](https://electoral.govmu.org/oec/?p=8503)).<br><br>The electoral commissioner issued a public communique on elector registration in May 2026 ([communique](https://electoral.govmu.org/oec/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Communique-Enregistrement-des-electeurs.pdf)).<br><br>The inquiry is a door-to-door canvass. In a country whose identity card carries a mobile form and whose government runs a credential wallet, the electoral roll is still compiled by knocking on doors.</details> | No change |
| Morocco | [Online registration](https://fesnews.media/315047/2026/01/14) took two of every three new entries in the 2026 revision, and an [organic law](https://www.chambredesrepresentants.ma/ar/RapportLec1-5525) institutionalises internet-based enrolment. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The interior minister's account of the annual revision records new registrations running two to one in favour of the online channel over in-person enrolment, on a roll of about 16.5m voters with about 1.4m entries removed ([revision](https://fesnews.media/315047/2026/01/14)).<br><br>Organic law 55.25 institutionalises internet-based electoral-roll registration for residents and expatriates and bars artificial-intelligence tools and social platforms from publishing referendum poll results; it was adopted in committee across four sessions in November 2025 and companion law 54.25 was constitutionally cleared in December ([committee report](https://www.chambredesrepresentants.ma/ar/RapportLec1-5525), [decision](https://www.cour-constitutionnelle.ma/Decision?Page=Decision&id=2124)).</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | A [new voter register is produced for each election](https://www.eeas.europa.eu/sites/default/files/documents/2025/EU%20EOM%20Mozambique%202024%20Final%20Report%20ENG.pdf) rather than maintained continuously, and the observation mission records a notable lack of confidence in its reliability. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The European Union observation mission on the 9 October 2024 general elections records that a new voter register is produced for each election, which it names as part of what makes elections expensive, and a notable lack of confidence in the register's reliability given discrepancies between it and the statistics institute's population projections ([mission](https://www.eeas.europa.eu/sites/default/files/documents/2025/EU%20EOM%20Mozambique%202024%20Final%20Report%20ENG.pdf)).<br><br>The election administration published no report on the voter registration process, and the mission recommends updating the register from other reliable existing data sources, naming the civil registry ([mission](https://www.eeas.europa.eu/sites/default/files/documents/2025/EU%20EOM%20Mozambique%202024%20Final%20Report%20ENG.pdf)).<br><br>No register size, update cycle or duplicate rate is published by the election administration.</details> | No change |
| Namibia | General registration closed on 1 August 2024 with [1,467,604 voters enrolled, 91 per cent of those eligible](https://www.namibian.com.na/well-done-majority-of-namibians-registered-to-vote/), against [1,358,468 in 2019](https://www.namibian.com.na/well-done-majority-of-namibians-registered-to-vote/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The general registration period for the 2024 presidential and National Assembly elections closed on 1 August 2024 with 1,467,604 voters registered in-country and at diplomatic missions, about 91 per cent of the roughly 1.6 million the statistics agency put as eligible](https://www.namibian.com.na/well-done-majority-of-namibians-registered-to-vote/). [Of these, 763,700 men and 852,100 women registered, two regions reached 97 per cent and the lowest region 83 per cent](https://www.namibian.com.na/well-done-majority-of-namibians-registered-to-vote/). [The 2019 register held 1,358,468 voters, of whom 820,227 voted](https://www.namibian.com.na/well-done-majority-of-namibians-registered-to-vote/).<br><br>The commission publishes supplementary registration and party-registration procedure, including a requirement that a party produce 500 declarations from names on the national register ([procedure](https://www.ecn.na/faq/)).<br><br>No statement of how the roll is deduplicated against the population register, and no post-2024 roll size, is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Niger | The [biometric voter roll rests on the pre-2023 electoral code](https://www.gouv.ne/images/textes-fondamentaux/codeelectoral.pdf), with no successor body or roll update on record. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [electoral code of August 2017, as amended in July 2019](https://www.gouv.ne/images/textes-fondamentaux/codeelectoral.pdf), establishes the biometric electoral file as the unique national voter roll at articles 36 and 37, assembled from communal, regional and embassy lists and managed by the electoral commission's dedicated directorate.<br><br>That is the pre-coup regime. No post-2023 successor electoral body, and no update to the roll, is on file; the 2026 civil-status and population-register reforms are an identity track rather than a rebuilt electoral roll.</details> | No change |
| Nigeria | [Biometric voter accreditation devices failed](https://blog.nigerianbar.org.ng/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/REPORT-OF-THE-OSUN-STATE-ELECTION-2026.pdf) at a state governorship election, with two observer missions calling for a technical audit before the 2027 general election. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The bar association's election working group recorded devices failing to boot and delaying a poll's opening, fingerprint and facial capture failing on some voters — elderly voters recurrently — so that they could not vote at all, slow processing at units of over 1,100 registered voters, an export failure and a complete device stoppage at one unit ([report](https://blog.nigerianbar.org.ng/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/REPORT-OF-THE-OSUN-STATE-ELECTION-2026.pdf)).<br><br>It recommends comprehensive pre-election testing of biometric capture, network connectivity, battery performance and volume handling, and an upgrade before the general election ([upgrade](https://dailytrust.com/osun-poll-nba-calls-for-bvas-upgrade/)); a second observer mission separately called for a full post-election technical audit ([audit](https://dailypost.ng/2026/08/16/osun-election-yiaga-africa-calls-for-bvas-audit-improved-security-ahead-2027-polls/)).<br><br>No commission response, failure rate or audit commitment is on record, and the observations are limited to the units the teams visited.</details> | Regressed |
| Rwanda | The electoral commission reported [more than 9.5 million registered voters](https://www.ktpress.rw/2024/05/the-9-5-million-rwandans-on-voter-list/) ahead of the July 2024 elections. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The commission reported more than 9.5 million people on the voter list by 13 May 2024, ahead of presidential and parliamentary elections held on 15 July ([list](https://www.ktpress.rw/2024/05/the-9-5-million-rwandans-on-voter-list/)), of whom more than two million were registering for the first time ([first-time voters](https://www.ktpress.rw/2024/05/rwanda-decides-electoral-body-says-more-than-two-million-first-time-voters-registered/)).<br><br>Both counts were given during the registration period and neither was restated at its close, so the base holds no final register size for the 2024 elections and nothing since. How the register is built from, or reconciled against, the national identity system is not stated in either account.</details> | No change, a first count with no earlier figure behind it |
| Sao Tome and Principe | The [automatic electoral roll](https://rstp.st/2026/07/31/eleicoes26-cen-lanca-campanha-de-consulta-e-atualizacao-de-dados-com-mais-de-146-mil-eleitores-inscritos/) carries 146,510 voters for the September 2026 elections, added after a process failure that had missed about 1,860 eligible voters. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The reform project closed in September 2025, the new system was ready in February 2026 and automatic registration was inaugurated in April ([project](https://rstp.st/2025/09/02/stp-encerra-o-projeto-de-reforma-do-sistema-eleitoral-financiado-pela-uniao-europeia/), [system](https://jornaleconomico.sapo.pt/noticias/sao-tome-e-principe-pronto-para-um-novo-sistema-de-recenseamento-eleitoral/), [inauguration](https://www.telanon.info/sociedade/2026/04/30/52715/prese-inaugura-recenseamento-automatico-e-poupa-milhoes-ao-estado/)).<br><br>The provisional roll carries 146,510 voters, added after a fix to the process failure ([roll](https://rstp.st/2026/07/31/eleicoes26-cen-lanca-campanha-de-consulta-e-atualizacao-de-dados-com-mais-de-146-mil-eleitores-inscritos/)).<br><br>The finding is what the failure was: the first automatic derivation from the civil registry reproduced that register's own omissions. Automating a derivation inherits the source's gaps, and the electoral commission launched a consultation and update campaign in response. The data-protection authority supervises the interconnection, which is recorded separately.</details> | Mixed, the first automatic roll derived from the civil registry while that derivation reproduced the register's own omissions |
| Senegal | The [2025 ordinary revision completed with cards available from 2 June 2026](https://lesoleil.sn/actualites/politique/revision-ordinaire-des-listes-electorales-de-2025-les-cartes-delecteur-deja-disponibles/), and a [May 2026 law narrowed the grounds for striking a citizen off](https://lesoleil.sn/actualites/politique/le-senegal-reforme-son-code-electoral/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The elections director-general [announced on 2 June 2026 that voter cards arising from registrations, modifications and status changes during the 2025 revision were available at prefectures and sub-prefectures, collection requiring the deposit receipt and the biometric identity card](https://lesoleil.sn/actualites/politique/revision-ordinaire-des-listes-electorales-de-2025-les-cartes-delecteur-deja-disponibles/). No revised national total is stated.<br><br>[Loi 2026-10 of 12 May 2026 narrows the grounds on which a citizen is struck from the roll to offences the text expressly names, and caps the resulting bar at five years from the date a conviction became final](https://lesoleil.sn/actualites/politique/le-senegal-reforme-son-code-electoral/), closing the previous absence of any time limit.<br><br>The last stated size is the 2024 election's: [7,371,890 registered voters of whom 338,040 abroad, held by the interior ministry with the file-automation directorate running the centralised processing and the voter card coupled to the biometric national card](https://www.au.int/sites/default/files/documents/44060-doc-Rapport_final_Election_Presidentielle_-_Senegal_2024.pdf).</details> | Advanced |
| Seychelles | The [2026 register of voters was certified on 31 March 2026](https://www.nation.sc/articles/30109/ecs-certifies-2026-voters-register), after an online registration portal and tablet voter-tagging debuted in 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The provisional register went out for inspection from 27 January to 13 February 2026 under section 8(1) of the Elections Act ([gazette](https://www.gazette.sc/sites/default/files/2026-01/Gazette%20No%202%20-%2012th%20January%202026.pdf)), and the chief registration officer certified the register on 31 March 2026, with certified copies distributed to eight political parties ([certification](https://www.nation.sc/articles/30109/ecs-certifies-2026-voters-register)).<br><br>The Commission's own 2025 report records 73,627 voters certified in March 2025, the register reopened on 14 October 2025 for 1,597 post-election transactions to the end of the year, and an online registration portal and tablet-based voter tagging deployed for the September and October 2025 elections ([report](https://www.nationalassembly.sc/sites/default/files/2026-04/ANNUAL-REPORT-2025%20Electoral%20Commission%20of%20Seychelles.pdf)).<br><br>The 2026 certified total is not stated in what is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Sierra Leone | Voter registration is [under review](https://tscssl.org/tscs-engages-ecsl-on-constitutional-reform-and-implementation-of-tripartite-recommendations/), with a [UNDP feasibility study](https://www.undp.org/sites/g/files/zskgke326/files/2026-04/feasibility_study_on_voter_registration_in_sierra_leone_final.pdf) published in December 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Registration is conducted under the Public Elections Act 2022 ([Act](https://www.parliament.gov.sl/uploads/acts/THE%20PUBLIC%20ELECTIONS%20ACT,%202022.pdf)).<br><br>A feasibility study on voter registration, with analysis and recommendations, was published in December 2025 ([study](https://www.undp.org/sites/g/files/zskgke326/files/2026-04/feasibility_study_on_voter_registration_in_sierra_leone_final.pdf)), and the tripartite technical sub-committee engaged the Electoral Commission in February 2026 on constitutional reform and on implementing the tripartite recommendations ([engagement](https://tscssl.org/tscs-engages-ecsl-on-constitutional-reform-and-implementation-of-tripartite-recommendations/)).<br><br>No registered-voter figure, no decision on the study's recommendations and no link between the electoral roll and the national identity register is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Somalia | The electoral commission [presented digital voter registration equipment](https://nation.africa/africa/news/somalia-s-electoral-commission-unveils-digital-voter-registration-equipment-ahead-of-local-polls-4970512) in March 2025, and nothing has moved in the base since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The equipment was presented in March 2025 ([equipment](https://nation.africa/africa/news/somalia-s-electoral-commission-unveils-digital-voter-registration-equipment-ahead-of-local-polls-4970512)).<br><br>No registration figure, roll-out district, timetable or relationship to the national identity register is published. Equipment presented and then unreported for eighteen months is not evidence of a register.</details> | No change |
| South Africa | Voter registration [opened inside a messaging platform on 4 August 2026](https://techcabal.com/2026/08/07/south-africa-whatsapp-voter-registration/) and carried 1.7m transactions over the final weekend, with 291,016 first-time registrations. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08-04 — the electoral commission [launched voter registration inside a messaging platform three days before the deadline for the November local elections](https://techcabal.com/2026/08/07/south-africa-whatsapp-voter-registration/), running one-time-password authentication, document upload, address verification and voting-station confirmation over it. [The final weekend carried 1.7m registration transactions and 291,016 first-time registrations, of which the online portal alone carried 238,000, and 46% of new registrations were by people under 29](https://techcabal.com/2026/08/07/south-africa-whatsapp-voter-registration/).<br><br>Two absences accompany the launch: no statement on data handling, and none on a fallback if the channel fails. A national register accepting enrolment through a private messaging platform, stood up three days before a deadline, is a delivery achievement and a dependency in the same act.</details> | Advanced |
| South Sudan | An [election date of 22 December 2026](https://www.aa.com.tr/en/africa/south-sudan-sets-dec-22-for-long-awaited-general-elections/3974661) was set with registration to follow; [as at August it had not begun](https://www.africa-newsroom.com/press/south-sudan-president-kiir-urges-national-elections-commission-nec-to-prepare-for-december-polls-as-voter-registration-nears?lang=en). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The National Elections Act 2012 requires the Commission to prepare, revise, update, approve and maintain the General Electoral Register ([Act](https://mojca.gov.ss/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/National-Elections-Act-39-of-2012.pdf)).<br><br>The Commission announced 22 December 2026 as the election date and said a nationwide registration exercise, expected to take about a month, would follow ([date](https://www.aa.com.tr/en/africa/south-sudan-sets-dec-22-for-long-awaited-general-elections/3974661)). After assessment missions across all ten states and the three administrative areas, it said in August 2026 that registration was expected to begin soon ([status](https://www.africa-newsroom.com/press/south-sudan-president-kiir-urges-national-elections-commission-nec-to-prepare-for-december-polls-as-voter-registration-nears?lang=en)).<br><br>A month of registration, four months before the poll, and it had not started. No register of any size exists to update.</details> | Advanced |
| Sudan | The [National Elections Act 2008](https://redress.org/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/National-Elections-Act-2008-English.pdf) still founds the general electoral register, and no operational post-2023 register was found. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act establishes the general electoral register and the commission that prepares, revises, approves and keeps it, repealing the 1998 statute ([Act](https://redress.org/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/National-Elections-Act-2008-English.pdf)).<br><br>No authoritative evidence of an operational register after 2023 was located. The war and the absence of elections leave this an operative but dormant framework.<br><br>That matters for more than voting: an electoral register is one of the few population-wide datasets a state maintains independently of the civil registry, and Sudan's has not been refreshed.</details> | No change |
| Tanzania | [A permanent national register was created by the 2024 elections act](https://www.inec.go.tz/uploads/documents/sw-1714458586-SHERIA%20YA%20UCHAGUZI%20WA%20RAIS,%20WABUNGE%20NA%20MADIWANI%20YA%20MWAKA%202024.pdf) and [carried 37,647,235 voters in 2025](https://www.inec.go.tz/news/takwimu-za-wapiga-kura-na-vituo-vya-kupigia-kura-vitakavyotumika-katika-uchaguzi-mkuu-wa-mwaka-2025). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The 2024 elections act establishes the permanent national voters' register alongside a preliminary register, repealing the 1985 and 1979 election laws](https://www.inec.go.tz/uploads/documents/sw-1714458586-SHERIA%20YA%20UCHAGUZI%20WA%20RAIS,%20WABUNGE%20NA%20MADIWANI%20YA%20MWAKA%202024.pdf).<br><br>[The electoral commission announced receipt of Zanzibar's finalised figures, reconciling the national register at 37,647,235 ahead of the 2025 general election](https://www.inec.go.tz/news/takwimu-za-wapiga-kura-na-vituo-vya-kupigia-kura-vitakavyotumika-katika-uchaguzi-mkuu-wa-mwaka-2025).<br><br>[Its director of elections had earlier explained the register's growth as about 3,113,931 previously eligible but unregistered people now registered, answering an opposition party's dispute over the numbers](https://thechanzo.com/2025/08/04/inec-tanzania-responds-to-concerns-over-voter-register-numbers/).<br><br>No audit of the register, and no statement of whether it draws on the national identity register, is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Togo | The [electoral register](https://www.togofirst.com/fr/politique/0204-16058-elections-municipales-au-togo-la-revision-des-listes-electorales-debute-le-7-avril) was revised nationwide over two weeks in April 2025 under a presidential decree. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The revision ran 7 to 23 April 2025 ([revision](https://www.togofirst.com/fr/politique/0204-16058-elections-municipales-au-togo-la-revision-des-listes-electorales-debute-le-7-avril)).<br><br>It is a register separate from the identity agency's system, and the two must never be summed. Nothing on the record connects the six million identity numbers to electoral registration, and no source states whether the two will be reconciled.</details> | No change |
| Tunisia | [The electoral authority holds and permanently updates the register of electors](https://www.isie.tn/fr/isie/creation-et-mission/) under its founding organic law, with nothing published on its size or upkeep. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The electoral authority's own account of its duties states that it holds and permanently updates the register of electors and finalises and publishes the electoral lists for each election, under its founding organic law of December 2012 as modified by a decree-law of April 2022](https://www.isie.tn/fr/isie/creation-et-mission/).<br><br>Nothing beyond the mandate is held: no registration count, no update cycle, no audit, and no statement of how the register relates to the civil register or the citizen unique identifier.</details> | No change |
| Zambia | The final register [was certified at 8,786,300 voters with polling-station detail across 13,529 stations](https://www.elections.org.zm/wp-content/uploads/rptPDListing20260508.pdf), after [1,615,922 were added](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/node/13018). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-03-06 — [a ministerial statement reported a 48-day mass registration exercise that added 1,615,922 new voters, bringing the provisional register to 8,861,918, followed by inspection and biometric verification](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/node/13018).<br><br>2026-05-02 — [the commission announced certification of the register for the general election on 4 May](https://www.elections.org.zm/?p=5570).<br><br>2026-05-08 — [the final register was published at 8,786,300 registered voters with polling-station-level detail across 13,529 polling stations](https://www.elections.org.zm/wp-content/uploads/rptPDListing20260508.pdf). The difference between the provisional register and the certified one is what inspection and biometric verification removed, and neither account explains it — which for an electoral register is the thing a reader most wants explained.</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | A [third constitutional amendment on the voters roll](https://www.zbcnews.co.zw/registrar-general-to-take-over-voters-roll-under-constitutional-reforms/) has begun implementation, [transferring the roll to the registrar-general](https://www.zbcnews.co.zw/registrar-general-to-take-over-voters-roll-under-constitutional-reforms/) — the office that determines identity and vital status would also determine the electorate. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The justice minister told the Senate implementation had begun, with remaining work described as administrative rather than legislative ([amendment](https://www.zbcnews.co.zw/registrar-general-to-take-over-voters-roll-under-constitutional-reforms/), [transfer](https://www.zbcnews.co.zw/registrar-general-to-take-over-voters-roll-under-constitutional-reforms/)).<br><br>The office that determines identity and vital status would also determine the electorate. That is the finding, and the ledger states it directly rather than leaving it to inference.<br><br>It lands in a unit where the responsible minister says he is unaware of any identity-document backlog and where the national digital identity has never started.</details> | Advanced |

## Tax register

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Benin | The [Identifiant Fiscal Unique has been instituted since Decret 2017-215 of 10 April 2017](https://sgg.gouv.bj/doc/decret-2017-215/download). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Decret 2017-215 of 10 April 2017 institutes the Identifiant Fiscal Unique](https://sgg.gouv.bj/doc/decret-2017-215/download), and [the tax directorate deployed the SIGTAS system from March 2018](https://www.gouv.bj/article/27/lancement-du-systeme-integre-de-gestion-des-taxes-et-assimiles-sigtas-par-le-ministre-de-leconomie-et-des-finances-monsieur-romuald-wadagni/).<br><br>No registered-taxpayer count under the identifier is held, and nothing states whether it is keyed to the national register of natural persons.</details> | No change, a standing position the base holds for the first time |
| Botswana | The [Tax Administration Act 2026 was assented in June](https://www.burs.org.bw/index.php/tax/tax-laws-2026?download=955%3Atax-administration-act-2026), carrying a registration framework the previous law spread across several statutes. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-06-29 — [the Act was assented, its 128 sections covering taxpayer registration, assessment and collection](https://www.burs.org.bw/index.php/tax/tax-laws-2026?download=955%3Atax-administration-act-2026).<br><br>2026-05-29 — [regulations under the VAT Act set the registration and compliance machinery for foreign suppliers of remote services](https://www.burs.org.bw/images/VAT%20Added%20Tax%20Regulations%202026.pdf).<br><br>2026-02-26 — [the finance ministry described a modernisation programme covering real-time taxpayer accounts, automated risk scoring and third-party data integration, and an electronic VAT invoicing solution](https://www.botswanaspeaks.gov.bw/media/COMMITTEE%20OF%20SUPPPLY%20SPEECHES/Ministry%20of%20FInance%20Committee%20of%20Supply%20Speech%2026-02-2026%20-%20Final.pdf).<br><br>No registered-taxpayer count is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Burundi | Registration [exists but is not integrated with filing and payment](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1bdiea2026002.pdf), and the [value-added tax threshold was reset from 1 July 2026](https://www.obr.bi/index.php/communiques/2528-communique-aux-contribuables-ayant-un-chiffre-d-affaires-de-25-millions-de-fbu). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[An international assessment records the register's position: taxpayer registration exists but is not yet integrated with the filing and payment modules, the revenue platform is phased to full operation around mid-2027, and about 13,000 electronic billing machines are deployed against a 40,000 target](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1bdiea2026002.pdf).<br><br>2026-07-01 — [the value-added tax threshold was set at 25,000,000 BIF of taxable turnover under a statute of 30 June 2026, with invoicing from 1 July and monthly declaration by the fifteenth](https://www.obr.bi/index.php/communiques/2528-communique-aux-contribuables-ayant-un-chiffre-d-affaires-de-25-millions-de-fbu). A threshold change is a change in who the register must carry, which is the register question rather than the platform question, and no count of taxpayers on either side of it is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Cape Verde | The tax and customs authority's [first governing board was appointed](https://boe.incv.cv/Bulletins/Download/34129) with effect from 1 August 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [resolution approved in council on 29 July 2026](https://boe.incv.cv/Bulletins/Download/34129) appoints the members of the authority's governing board with effect from 1 August, which is the body constituted rather than the register moved.<br><br>No taxpayer count or registration series is published against it.</details> | Advanced |
| Central African Republic | The [first structured official report evaluating the tax and customs measures](https://www.finances.gouv.cf/sites/default/files/2026-05/RAPPORT%20DES%20MESURES%20NOUVELLES.pdf) describes the online tax platform as the operative system. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-05 - [the first structured official report evaluating the measures describes the online tax platform alongside the customs system as the operative digitalisation](https://www.finances.gouv.cf/sites/default/files/2026-05/RAPPORT%20DES%20MESURES%20NOUVELLES.pdf). The platform was [launched in 2023 with European funding for online enrolment, declaration and payment](http://news.abangui.com/h/88755.html).<br><br>2025-10 - the fiscal risk statement accompanying the budget bill [records tax administration weaknesses among five risk categories to revenue mobilisation](https://finances.gouv.cf/sites/default/files/2025-11/risque%20budgetaire%20plf%202026.pdf).<br><br>No taxpayer count, enrolment figure or online-filing share is published, so the register behind the platform cannot be sized.</details> | Advanced |
| Chad | The tax platform runs [registration, filing and payment online](https://dgi.td/), and [protocols now link tax, treasury, customs and the central bank](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/07/21/tchad-la-dgi-la-dgddi-et-la-beac-signent-des-protocoles-daccord-pour-lechange-de-donnees/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The tax directorate's platform covers tele-registration, tele-declaration and tele-payment, alongside a mobile application and a normalised electronic invoicing system ([platform](https://dgi.td/)).<br><br>A protocol signed on 1 April 2026 with the treasury provides automated real-time exchange of tax issuances, receipts, arrears and payments ([protocol](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/04/01/la-direction-generale-des-impots-et-celle-du-tresor-et-de-la-comptabilite-signent-un-protocole-dechange-de-donnees/)), extended on 21 July 2026 to customs and the central bank ([extension](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/07/21/tchad-la-dgi-la-dgddi-et-la-beac-signent-des-protocoles-daccord-pour-lechange-de-donnees/)).<br><br>No taxpayer count is published on the platform, so the register's size is not established.</details> | Advanced |
| Comoros | The [general tax code builds a declarative system around a single taxpayer file](https://justice.gouv.km/texte/code-general-des-impots-2023/); no registration count is on file. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [code, adopted by loi n 11-07 of 3 May 2011 and promulgated by decret n 11-151 of 23 July 2011, established a modern declarative tax system built around a single taxpayer file, following a refoundation of the tax system worked through in 2009 and 2010](https://justice.gouv.km/texte/code-general-des-impots-2023/).<br><br>What the base holds is the code's landing page, not its operative articles, so what the file must contain and who must be on it cannot be read from it.<br><br>2026-01 - the current fund review [names mobilising domestic revenue through tax and customs administration reforms among its key policy priorities](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1comea2026001-source-pdf.pdf). Fifteen years after the code, no registration count, coverage estimate or cleansing exercise has been published.</details> | No change, a single taxpayer file provided for since 2011 with no coverage figure ever published |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The taxpayer register [gained statutory footing in 2025](https://www.dgi.gouv.ci/assets/documents/EBOOK/BODGI_2025/files/basic-html/page95.html) and [paper filing ends for its smaller tiers from 2027](https://www.dgi.gouv.ci/assets/documents/EBOOK/BODGI_2026/files/basic-html/page152.html). <details><summary>Full record</summary>Before 2025 the administration registered taxpayers by attributing an account number and attaching them to a managing office, [an established practice not expressly provided for in law and so contestable; the 2025 annexe fiscale gives it statutory footing, requiring every taxpayer carrying on a professional activity, lucrative or not, to be attached to the competent office after an account number is issued](https://www.dgi.gouv.ci/assets/documents/EBOOK/BODGI_2025/files/basic-html/page95.html).<br><br>2025-12-19 - the following year's annexe fiscale ends paper filing for the smaller tiers: [microenterprise taxpayers must transmit financial statements and every annexed document electronically only from 1 January 2027, and taxpayers under the entrepreneur state tax from 1 January 2028, on a penalty of FCFA 1,000,000 rising by FCFA 100,000 for each further month of delay](https://www.dgi.gouv.ci/assets/documents/EBOOK/BODGI_2026/files/basic-html/page152.html); the obligation already applied to the large and medium enterprise directorates.<br><br>No register size, coverage rate or filing-compliance figure is published for any tier.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | [No taxpayer register is held](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099012726115520907/pdf/P180696-28ecf2f4-34e9-4559-9695-f14bd83f8798.pdf); the integrated tax system that would carry one collects nothing. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register question turns on a system that does not run. The [share of returns filed and paid through the integrated tax administration system stood at 0% in January 2026 against a 70% target for 2029, with the follow-on project's effectiveness extended a third time to 9 March 2026](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099012726115520907/pdf/P180696-28ecf2f4-34e9-4559-9695-f14bd83f8798.pdf).<br><br>Its predecessor [closed with the tax system non-operational, while customs modernisation succeeded](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099080525142540178/pdf/BOSIB-52d5d49a-e84f-4d03-9ca1-282ab3f38cab.pdf).<br><br>No taxpayer count, registration figure or register instrument is published. The authorities' plan is to [finalise digitalisation and integration of information systems across tax administrations over 2026-30](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2025/english/1djiea2025001-source-pdf.pdf).</details> | Stalled, the system that would hold the register is not operational |
| Egypt | A [new tax work automation system began operating](https://www.eta.gov.eg/ar/news/ttbyq-mnzwmt-myknt-ajraat-alml-aldryby-aljdydt-sap-fy-mntqt-alqahrt-awl-walmamwryat-altabt-lha) from October 2025, on a [unified registration number](https://alberonsy.com/%D9%82%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%88%D9%86-%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85-206-%D9%84%D8%B3%D9%86%D8%A9-2020-%D8%A8%D8%A5%D8%B5%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1-%D9%82%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%88%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A5%D8%AC%D8%B1%D8%A7%D8%A1%D8%A7/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statutory basis is [the 2020 unified tax procedures law, whose article 26 assigns every taxpayer a single unified tax registration number across all taxes](https://alberonsy.com/%D9%82%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%88%D9%86-%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85-206-%D9%84%D8%B3%D9%86%D8%A9-2020-%D8%A8%D8%A5%D8%B5%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1-%D9%82%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%88%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A5%D8%AC%D8%B1%D8%A7%D8%A1%D8%A7/).<br><br>2025-10-01 - [a new tax work automation system began operating at the first Cairo office](https://www.eta.gov.eg/ar/news/ttbyq-mnzwmt-myknt-ajraat-alml-aldryby-aljdydt-sap-fy-mntqt-alqahrt-awl-walmamwryat-altabt-lha).<br><br>2025-11-12 - a finance ministry decree [opened application to a simplified regime for businesses under EGP 20m of annual turnover](https://eta.gov.eg/ar/news/qrar-wzyr-almalyt-rqm-420-lsnt-2025), and a [national electronic receipt specification for education-service returns was issued in August 2026](https://sdk.preprod.invoicing.eta.gov.eg/documents/education-return-receipt-v1-2/). No register size or filing-compliance figure is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Equatorial Guinea | The revenue system's [registration, filing and audit modules were detailed in May 2026](https://minhacienda-gob.com/el-consejo-directivo-del-ministerio-de-hacienda-analiza-las-reformas-estructurales-y-la-modernizacion-de-los-sistemas-de-gestion-economica) as the route to a reliable taxpayer roll. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system was [launched with development bank finance in October 2023, contracted to a supplier and due in the first quarter of 2026](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/imgdb/2023/file/NP%20Lanzamiento%20SIGREF.pdf).<br><br>In [May 2026, after that date, the finance ministry was still setting out its tax registration, filing and audit modules](https://minhacienda-gob.com/el-consejo-directivo-del-ministerio-de-hacienda-analiza-las-reformas-estructurales-y-la-modernizacion-de-los-sistemas-de-gestion-economica). A [single annual trade licence system opened training in February with a 40 per cent tax cut pitched to draw informal traders onto the register](https://minhacienda-gob.com/acto-de-apertura-oficial-del-seminario-de-formacion-sobre-el-patente-tributario-unico-anual/), and a [draft decree would recode the tax identification number itself](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/el_consejo_interministerial_analiza_nuevas_medidas_para_reforzar_el_empleo_la_fiscalidad_y_la_cooperacion_internacional).</details> | Advanced |
| Eswatini | Taxpayer identification number registration is [free but paper-based](https://eswatinitradeportal.com/index.php?id=25&r=searchProcedure%2Fview1), on a form collected from an office or downloaded, with twelve supporting documents. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The procedure is free and administered by the revenue service under the value added tax statute, with the form collected from an office or downloaded and twelve supporting documents required, from a certified certificate of incorporation to evidence of a fixed place of business and the capacity to keep accounting records ([procedure](https://eswatinitradeportal.com/index.php?id=25&r=searchProcedure%2Fview1)).<br><br>There is no online submission route, which sits against company registration in the same ministry running through the government portal ([company](https://eswatinitradeportal.com/index.php?id=23&r=searchProcedure%2Fview1)).<br><br>No count of registered taxpayers is published, and the underlying forms the procedure page links carry dates of April 2020.</details> | No change |
| Ethiopia | A directive materially widened who must enter the value-added tax register, with a 30-day window for those already affected ([directive](https://www.mofed.gov.et/media/filer_public/be/34/be34d1fd-284a-44c4-9fe8-5df5162504cd/1104_latachamaari_eseete_taakese_selamamazegabe_yawathaa_mamariyaa_quthere_1104_2017_250903_101712.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>Directive 1104/2025, issued by the finance ministry in September 2025 under the value-added tax proclamation, expanded who must register: Category A taxpayers, mandatory and voluntary book-keepers, businesses with turnover above two million birr, and specified professional-service providers below that threshold, with a 30-day registration window for existing affected taxpayers ([directive](https://www.mofed.gov.et/media/filer_public/be/34/be34d1fd-284a-44c4-9fe8-5df5162504cd/1104_latachamaari_eseete_taakese_selamamazegabe_yawathaa_mamariyaa_quthere_1104_2017_250903_101712.pdf)).<br><br>The statutory basis is the 2016 federal tax administration proclamation, which requires taxpayer registration and establishes the taxpayer identification number ([proclamation](https://justice.gov.et/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/አዋጅ-ቁጥር-983-2008-እንግሊዘኛ.pdf)).<br><br>The base holds no count of registered taxpayers before or after the widening, so the size of the change is not measurable from it.</details> | Advanced |
| Gambia | The tax administration system was [re-awarded at US$7.27m](https://www.gra.gm/news/4f0f3845-b049-11f0-b086-029254d29bb1) after the first supplier went bankrupt. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [joint press release of October 2025](https://www.gra.gm/news/4f0f3845-b049-11f0-b086-029254d29bb1) records the original award collapsing in the supplier's bankruptcy and a replacement consortium selected at US$7.27m after negotiation. That is a two-year delay to the register's modernisation, stated by the two agencies themselves.<br><br>Around it the revenue stack is running: [digital tax invoicing and excise stamps](https://www.gra.gm/news/26fd4242-17b6-11f1-b086-029254d29bb1) are in the authority's own March 2026 inventory, and a [four-party memorandum of April 2026](https://www.gra.gm/news/7bb085a9-4f96-11f1-b086-029254d29bb1) is to build telecom revenue assurance, with no go-live date or cost given.</details> | Advanced |
| Ghana | The revenue authority [maintains the taxpayer identification number system](https://gra.gov.gh/tin/) under the 2016 revenue administration Act; no register size is published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The authority's own page is the operative description: [the taxpayer identification number system is maintained under sections 10 to 12 of the revenue administration Act, which authorise the commissioner-general to keep it](https://gra.gov.gh/tin/). It carries no register size, no coverage rate and no active-taxpayer count.<br><br>What is moving is the system the register sits in rather than the register itself: the tax administration system's pilot had [more than 400 taxpayers onboarded at one office by June 2026, with a second phase to integrate the companies registrar, the social security trust and the identification authority](https://graphiconline.com/news/general-news/ghana-news-gra-activates-new-tax-administration-platform-over-400-taxpayers-onboarded.html). That integration is where a register's completeness would be tested, and it has not happened yet.</details> | No change, the register's statutory basis is stated and no size or coverage figure is published |
| Guinea-Bissau | The tax identification number is [issued solely through the payment platform](https://kontaktu.mef.gw/instructions) from December 2025, with a [taxpayer census as a reform action](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1gnbea2026002.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The revenue directorate's [order of December 2025 bans the older financial management system and makes the payment platform the sole channel for producing and issuing the tax identification number](https://kontaktu.mef.gw/instructions).<br><br>The register behind it is to be rebuilt: the fund's [eleventh review confirms the revenue administration platform and a taxpayer census among the reform actions](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1gnbea2026002.pdf). No taxpayer count is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Kenya | The revenue authority's [online tax service](https://itax.kra.go.ke) carries the verification checkers the tax identification number and the electronic invoicing regime run on. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The service carries verification checkers for the personal identification number, tax compliance certificates, exemptions and excise licences, withholding certificates and agents, application status, and the control-unit serial and invoice numbers that the electronic invoicing regime turns on ([service](https://itax.kra.go.ke)).<br><br>The held capture is the platform's public landing page. No registered-taxpayer count, filing volume or availability figure is published on it, so the register's size is not established.</details> | No change |
| Lesotho | The taxpayer register is now fed by [mandatory structured electronic transaction data](https://www.rsl.org.ls/sites/default/files/2026-04/VAT%20-%20E-Invoicing%20Regulations%20No.25%20of%202026%20%283%29_0.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2001 — [section 7(1) of the Revenue Authority Act authorises systems for taxpayer identification](https://www.rsl.org.ls/sites/default/files/2024-07/LRA%20ACT%202001%20original.pdf).<br><br>2026-03-27 — [the e-invoicing regulations legally establish structured electronic transaction data in taxpayer records](https://www.rsl.org.ls/sites/default/files/2026-04/VAT%20-%20E-Invoicing%20Regulations%20No.25%20of%202026%20%283%29_0.pdf).<br><br>2026-02-10 — [a bank payment gateway with automatic reconciliation went live](https://www.rsl.org.ls/sites/default/files/2026-02/Press%20Statement%20RSL%20SLB%20Integration%20E%20payments%20Launch%20%281%29.pdf).<br><br>No registered-taxpayer count is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Liberia | [165,608 registered taxpayers in 2024](https://revenue.lra.gov.lr/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/Liberia-DRM-Strategy-2025-2029.pdf), segmented, on a tax system that replaced its predecessor in December 2023. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [resource mobilisation strategy](https://revenue.lra.gov.lr/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/Liberia-DRM-Strategy-2025-2029.pdf) gives the register as 165,608 taxpayers in 2024 across large, medium, government and non-profit, natural-resource, small and micro and real-property segments, with 7,662 inactive, and records the integrated tax administration system replacing its predecessor for transaction processing from 1 December 2023.<br><br>That system is [recorded as integrated with six systems](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099110625073415416/pdf/P177478-9397c8d1-edb6-4a96-a39d-0222e717f2c4.pdf) including the identity registry, which is the only working data exchange the base holds against a service bus carrying none.</details> | Advanced |
| Libya | The tax administration was [entirely paper-based with no register system](https://www.elibrary.imf.org/downloadpdf/view/journals/019/2024/100/019.2024.issue-100-en.pdf) in August 2024; a unified tax system has since gone live. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The fund's [technical assistance report](https://www.elibrary.imf.org/downloadpdf/view/journals/019/2024/100/019.2024.issue-100-en.pdf) of August 2024 is blunt: Libya's tax administration ran entirely on paper with no register system behind it.<br><br>The unified tax system carried on the ledger since December 2025 is the answer to that finding, and the [electronic revenue collection mandate of March 2026](https://lgcc.gov.ly/%d8%b1%d8%a6%d9%8a%d8%b3-%d8%a7%d9%84%d9%88%d8%b2%d8%b1%d8%a7%d8%a1-%d8%b9%d8%a8%d8%af%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%ad%d9%85%d9%8a%d8%af-%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%af%d8%a8%d9%8a%d8%a8%d8%a9-%d9%8a%d8%b5%d8%af%d8%b1/) extends the same logic to collection. No taxpayer count has been published against either.</details> | Advanced |
| Madagascar | A [beneficial owners register was launched](http://central.mef.gov.mg/page_personnalisee/index/news/1781) in June 2026 alongside a new release of the online declaration service. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A tax administration diagnostic mission in October 2022 assessed the integrity of the taxpayer register, the taxpayer number and its online service, the second such assessment ([diagnostic](https://www.tadat.org/content/dam/tadat/en/assessments/Madagascar_2022_TADAT_PAR.pdf)).<br><br>A new version of the online tax declaration service was released alongside the integrated administration system in October 2025 ([release](https://newsmada.com/2025/10/07/gestion-fiscale-safi-et-nouvelle-version-de-le-hetra/)).<br><br>A central register of beneficial owners was launched in June 2026 under the transparency and domestic revenue agenda, against illicit financial flows estimated at USD 330 million in 2024 ([launch](http://central.mef.gov.mg/page_personnalisee/index/news/1781)). The diagnostic's own register-integrity scores are not in the base; only its contents and preface were captured.</details> | Advanced |
| Malawi | About [8,000 landlords were registered for rental income tax](https://malawi24.com/2026/07/20/mras-eis-gains-momentum/) through property-registration exercises across low- and high-density areas. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The drive was announced in February 2026, starting in the three main cities with data collectors recruited ([announcement](https://malawi24.com/2026/02/11/mra-clarifies-electronic-invoicing-system-rental-income-tax-amid-public-skepticism/)), and about 8,000 landlords had registered by July ([progress](https://malawi24.com/2026/07/20/mras-eis-gains-momentum/)).<br><br>It is a registry drive riding on a revenue platform rather than a land registry, which is the only way a property register is being built in this base.</details> | Advanced |
| Mali | The revenue directorate has [published its list of active taxpayers online since 2019](https://www.dgi.gouv.ml/la-liste-des-contribuables-actifs-est-en-ligne/) and [opened three filing options](https://bamada.net/mali-la-direction-generale-des-impots-ouvre-trois-options-pour-la-declaration-fiscale-et-le-depot-des-etats-financiers) in April 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>No filing share, taxpayer count or processing time is published for the three options, and the newest annual report held is for 2022 ([report](https://www.dgi.gouv.ml/wp-content/uploads/2023/05/Rapport-annuel-DGI-Mali-2022-version.pdf), [options](https://bamada.net/mali-la-direction-generale-des-impots-ouvre-trois-options-pour-la-declaration-fiscale-et-le-depot-des-etats-financiers)).<br><br>A cadastral address identifier was announced by the same directorate in January 2026 and is forthcoming rather than in service ([address](https://www.dgi.gouv.ml/du-ninacad-adresse-bientot-disponible-au-mali/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritania | The [tax procedures book](https://impots.gov.mr/DGI/files/doctrine/LPF-Titre1-DC-20191007.pdf) carries the tax identification number and the obligations attaching to it. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The common provisions of the procedures book are what the base holds on the identifier ([provisions](https://impots.gov.mr/DGI/files/doctrine/LPF-Titre1-DC-20191007.pdf)).<br><br>No registered-taxpayer count, coverage figure or link to the business or population registers is published, so the identifier exists in law and its reach is unmeasured.</details> | No change |
| Mauritius | The register extended in two directions inside a year: a [changed VAT threshold](https://www.mra.mu/download/VATReg120925.pdf) and a [tax agent registration regime](https://www.mra.mu/download/GuideTaxAgents.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register is maintained under the Income Tax Act, in force since July 1996 ([Act](https://attorneygeneral.govmu.org/Documents/Laws%20of%20Mauritius/A-Z%20Acts/I/In/Income%20Tax%20Act-I9.pdf)).<br><br>The compulsory value-added tax registration threshold changed following the Finance Act, notified in September 2025 ([communique](https://www.mra.mu/download/VATReg120925.pdf)).<br><br>A registration regime opened for tax agents, with eligibility, procedure and obligations published in June 2026 ([guide](https://www.mra.mu/download/GuideTaxAgents.pdf)). No registered-taxpayer count is published in the sources held.</details> | Advanced |
| Morocco | The tax directorate publishes [two online routes to a tax identifier](https://portail.tax.gov.ma/wps/portal/DGI/Vos-impots-procedures/Autres-demandes), against a [24.6 per cent tax take](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1marea2026001-source-pdf.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [directorate's live procedure page](https://portail.tax.gov.ma/wps/portal/DGI/Vos-impots-procedures/Autres-demandes) shows two online routes to obtaining a Moroccan tax identifier, which is registration as a service rather than a counter visit.<br><br>What the register produces is measured: the fund attributes a [tax take of 24.6 per cent of output in 2025 to the full digitalisation of collection](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1marea2026001-source-pdf.pdf). The directorate is also [piloting artificial-intelligence property valuation in two cities](https://www.lodj.ma/Rabat-et-Casablanca-testent-le-controle-intelligent_a162801.html), which uses the register rather than extending it.</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | A [nine-digit single taxpayer identification number](http://www.at.gov.mz/eng/Internacional-Trade/FAQ-s/NUIT) assigned by the revenue authority is required of natural and legal persons for all income including customs. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The number is assigned by the revenue authority to natural and legal persons regardless of income level and is required for all income including customs, obtained at a tax office against an identity document, passport, birth certificate, voter registration card or driving licence ([guidance](http://www.at.gov.mz/eng/Internacional-Trade/FAQ-s/NUIT)).<br><br>The held record is the revenue authority's own guidance page, retrieved from a web archive after the live site failed to answer. No register size, issuance figure or linkage to the national identity system is published.</details> | No change |
| Namibia | The register reached [1.0 million taxpayers](https://www.namibian.com.na/one-million-taxpayers-carry-the-nation/) by March 2025 and a trader identification number became mandatory on every customs declaration from April 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>1.0 million taxpayers were registered at 31 March 2025, of whom 575,136 were electronic filers, on 1.04 million active accounts ([report](https://www.namibian.com.na/one-million-taxpayers-carry-the-nation/)); the year before the agency recorded 477,164 electronic filers ([2023/24](https://www.namra.org.na/documents/cms/uploaded/namra-annual-report--20234-b0ca93ea50.pdf)).<br><br>From 1 April 2026 a trader identification number is required on every import and export declaration, joining the customs register to the taxpayer register ([notice](https://www.namra.org.na/documents/cms/uploaded/public-notice--mandatory-tin-on-asycudaworld--9-dec-2025-7d1548a64e.pdf)).<br><br>The amnesty programme added 28,157 taxpayers by 30 September 2025 ([amnesty](https://www.namra.org.na/documents/cms/uploaded/namra--newsletter-july--september-2025-f477ca628d.pdf)).<br><br>One million registered taxpayers in a population of about three million. Registration is not the constraint on this base; nothing published says how many of the 1.04 million accounts are active in the sense of filing.</details> | Advanced |
| Niger | A [nationwide rental-housing census and regularisation drive](https://airinfoagadez.com/2026/08/01/niger-campagne-de-regularisation-des-impots-fonciers-les-autorites-misent-sur-le-civisme-fiscal/) was announced in July 2026 to broaden taxpayer identification. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A [ministry communiqué of 27 July 2026](https://airinfoagadez.com/2026/08/01/niger-campagne-de-regularisation-des-impots-fonciers-les-autorites-misent-sur-le-civisme-fiscal/) announced a nationwide census of rental housing and a regularisation drive for landlords, sub-lessors and second-home owners under the general tax code, to broaden identification of taxable property owners; the communiqué is held only in syndicated copies, not on a government site.<br><br>The register itself is long-standing: the tax authority [describes the taxpayer identification number as unique, exclusive and invariable](https://impots.gouv.ne/index.php?Itemid=182&id=52&option=com_content&view=article), issued under a 2023 implementing order that sets out enrolment dossiers for individuals, companies and other filers.<br><br>No public count of registered taxpayers, and no open register, is on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Nigeria | A [central tax identification portal went live on 1 January 2026](https://fctirs.gov.ng/nigerian-tax-id-portal-goes-live/), linking tax identifiers to the national identification number. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [portal went live nationwide on 1 January 2026](https://fctirs.gov.ng/nigerian-tax-id-portal-goes-live/), run jointly by the revenue board and the revenue service, linking tax identifiers to the national identification number for individuals and to corporate registration numbers for businesses, and replacing previously issued taxpayer identification numbers.<br><br>The revenue service [briefed federal bodies in July 2026 on the platform that replaced its predecessor and consolidates taxpayer registration, returns filing, payment and communications on one system](https://fmino.gov.ng/nrs-sensitises-federal-mdas-on-rev360-digital-tax-platform-ehcon-highlights-environmental-health-regulatory-mandate/).<br><br>The statutory duty is the [Tax Administration Act 2025](https://nass.gov.ng/documents/download/11250). No register size, coverage figure or registration outturn is published under any of them.</details> | Advanced |
| Rwanda | The [2023 tax procedures law](https://www.rra.gov.rw/fileadmin/user_upload/Law_n___020_2023_of_31.03.2023_on_tax_procedures.pdf) gives the register its legal basis through a registration duty and a taxpayer identification number. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statute enacted on 31 March 2023 establishes, in its second chapter, the taxpayer registration duty and the taxpayer identification number ([law](https://www.rra.gov.rw/fileadmin/user_upload/Law_n___020_2023_of_31.03.2023_on_tax_procedures.pdf)).<br><br>The order of April 2026 extends the register's reach into online payment: financial institutions facilitating them must integrate with the tax administration's systems ([order](https://www.rra.gov.rw/fileadmin/Folder_for_2025/Ministerial_Order_VAT_on_Online_Services_April_2026.pdf)).<br><br>No count of registered taxpayers is published in anything the base holds, so the register's coverage cannot be stated.</details> | Advanced |
| Sao Tome and Principe | The taxpayer identifier is [required throughout company registration](https://dgrn.gov.st/reg_gue.php), and a [February 2026 memorandum](https://impostos.financas.gov.st/index.php/component/k2/itemlist/category/77-direccao) created a permanent data-sharing mechanism with the tourism directorate. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The company window requires the taxpayer card of the applicant and the identifier of shareholders and companies, and a negative certificate of tax debt to dissolve ([window](https://dgrn.gov.st/reg_gue.php)), which is the identifier in operational use across another register.<br><br>The memorandum creates a permanent mechanism for sharing data on licensed tourism and hospitality businesses, cross-checking that firms are properly registered and tax-compliant and reinforcing the obligation to issue electronic invoices ([memorandum](https://impostos.financas.gov.st/index.php/component/k2/itemlist/category/77-direccao)).<br><br>The credit facility review reports a domestic revenue mobilisation strategy covering VAT administration, large-taxpayer follow-up and digital tax systems ([review](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2025/english/1stpea2025004-source-pdf.pdf)). No register size, coverage of the informal economy or compliance rate is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Senegal | The statistics agency publishes a [quarterly series of new registrations to the enterprise register](https://www.ansd.sn/Indicateur/immatriculation-au-ninea-chiffres-cles-tendances-et-evolutions) that issues the taxpayer identifier. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The bulletin records [26,685 new registrations in the fourth quarter of 2025, up 32.0% on the previous quarter and down 1.1% year on year, 76.0% of them sole traders](https://www.ansd.sn/Indicateur/immatriculation-au-ninea-chiffres-cles-tendances-et-evolutions).<br><br>It is a flow series only: no stock total for the register and no figure for the tax directorate's own taxpayer file is published in it.<br><br>The register the identifier attaches to is administered alongside the [tax directorate's five tele-procedure platforms](https://www.dgid.sn/service/e-services/), none of which publishes a registered-taxpayer count.</details> | Advanced |
| Seychelles | The [2022 business registration regulations](https://www.gazette.sc/sites/default/files/2022-12/SI%20138%202022%20-%20Revenue%20Administration%20%28Business%20Registration%20and%20Declaration%20of%20Operation%29%20Regulations%202022.pdf) govern the taxpayer register and TIN issuance; lodgement acknowledgment went digital in April 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The regulations, in force from 1 January 2023, govern registration with the revenue commission, the issuing of tax identification numbers and active, suspended, dormant and deregistered taxpayer status ([regulations](https://www.gazette.sc/sites/default/files/2022-12/SI%20138%202022%20-%20Revenue%20Administration%20%28Business%20Registration%20and%20Declaration%20of%20Operation%29%20Regulations%202022.pdf)).<br><br>From 1 April 2026 taxpayers receive a digital acknowledgment notice on lodgement in place of a paper assessment letter ([notice](https://src.gov.sc/src-streamlines-tax-lodgement-acknowledgement-process/)), under regulations of November 2025 giving electronic filing its legal basis ([regulations](https://www.nationalassembly.sc/legislation/statutory-instrument-sis/si-65-2025-revenue-administration-electronic-filing-documents)).<br><br>No count of registered taxpayers, or of returns filed electronically, is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Sierra Leone | The [Finance Act 2026](http://webtestcms.nra.gov.sl/uploads/The_Finance_Act_2026_121df05d9b.pdf) extends the tax register to suppliers of digital services with no physical presence in the country. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The register was constituted by the Finance Act 2020, which amended the Income Tax Act 2000 to require the Commissioner-General to keep a register of all persons liable to be assessed and to assign each a system-generated Tax Identification Number ([2020 Act](https://www.mof.gov.sl/wp-content/uploads/2020/02/The-Finance-Act-2020.pdf)).<br><br>The Finance Act 2026, in operation from 1 January 2026, brings within charge a person making a taxable supply of digital services to consumers in Sierra Leone whether or not they have a physical presence, and requires such a supplier to appoint a resident representative ([2026 Act](http://webtestcms.nra.gov.sl/uploads/The_Finance_Act_2026_121df05d9b.pdf)).<br><br>No registered-taxpayer count is published, and no enforcement against a non-resident digital supplier is on record.</details> | Advanced |
| Somalia | Registrations rose by [48,197 between July 2024 and August 2025](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099112625052026899/pdf/P177298-a188364c-8fe9-4a23-a8d1-afb09ca7c17a.pdf), a 76 per cent increase on a corrected baseline of 62,948. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The mid-term review recorded in the November 2025 status report puts the corrected baseline at 62,948 registered taxpayers across the federal government and member states at June 2024, rising by 48,197 new registrations to August 2025, excluding one member state ([report](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099112625052026899/pdf/P177298-a188364c-8fe9-4a23-a8d1-afb09ca7c17a.pdf)).<br><br>The same report notes the integrated tax administration system contracted after the review, with online filing by large taxpayers still at nil.<br><br>The operative framework is the income tax manual of November 2025, implementing the 2025 Act that replaced the 1966 direct taxation law, setting registration on a single form and a taxpayer number ([manual](https://www.mof.gov.so/sites/default/files/Publications/Somalia%20Income%20Tax%20Manual_English%20Version.pdf)).</details> | Advanced |
| South Africa | The Tax Administration Act's 2026 amendment [enables a simplified express customs regime and a voluntary electronic value-added-tax reporting system](https://www.parliament.gov.za/storage/app/media/Acts/2026/Act_4_of_2026_Tax_Administration_Laws_Amendment_Act.pdf), and [a reference-number enquiry service is documented](https://www.sars.gov.za/wp-content/uploads/Ops/Guides/GEN-ENR-01-G08-Tax-Reference-Number-TRN-Enquiry-Services-on-eFiling-External-Guide.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The Tax Administration Act of 2011 is the register's statutory basis](https://www.gov.za/sites/default/files/gcis_document/201409/a282011.pdf). 2026-04-01 — [an amendment Act was enacted creating the enabling framework for a simplified customs regime for door-to-door express imports and exports and for a voluntary value-added-tax electronic reporting system](https://www.parliament.gov.za/storage/app/media/Acts/2026/Act_4_of_2026_Tax_Administration_Laws_Amendment_Act.pdf).<br><br>Voluntary is the operative word: the reporting system the amendment enables carries no obligation, no commencement date and no uptake figure on the record.<br><br>2026-08-14 — [a self-service channel for retrieving and verifying a taxpayer reference number is documented in an external guide](https://www.sars.gov.za/wp-content/uploads/Ops/Guides/GEN-ENR-01-G08-Tax-Reference-Number-TRN-Enquiry-Services-on-eFiling-External-Guide.pdf), a service guide rather than a service report, with no usage figure published.</details> | Advanced |
| South Sudan | A [presidential order routes all government revenue through the electronic collection system](https://www.eyeradio.org/governance-cluster-endorses-policy-measures-on-finance-revenue-collection/), and [registration is being enforced](https://www.eyeradio.org/taxpayers-told-to-clear-arrears-before-may-15/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Taxation Act 2009 requires the revenue authority to run a registration system giving each taxpayer a unique identifying number, with registration mandatory and a certificate issued ([Act](https://cms.nra.gov.ss/uploads/TAXATION_ACT_2021_R_E_CONSOLIDATED_f4f9ed8876.pdf)).<br><br>Presidential Order No. 35 of 2025, signed 3 December 2025, requires every government institution to collect and remit public revenue exclusively through the authority's electronic collection system, and its enforcement was endorsed in March 2026 ([order](https://www.eyeradio.org/governance-cluster-endorses-policy-measures-on-finance-revenue-collection/)).<br><br>The domestic tax division gave taxpayers until 15 May 2026 to clear arrears and warned that unregistered businesses without an identification number are acting illegally ([notice](https://www.eyeradio.org/taxpayers-told-to-clear-arrears-before-may-15/)).<br><br>No registered-taxpayer count is published, so the enforcement has no denominator.</details> | Advanced |
| Sudan | The register runs on a [single tax identification number with monthly commercial-register feeds](https://tax.gov.sd/en/tax-procedures/), and an [expansion initiative](https://sudannewsgazette.com/initiative-to-broaden-tax-base-launched-to-promote-tax-equity-and-support-the-national-economy/) opened in July 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The chamber's own procedural statement sets mandatory registration within 30 days, a single tax identification number covering all tax types including customs, a per-taxpayer account kept for ten years, and monthly data sharing from the commercial register ([procedures](https://tax.gov.sd/en/tax-procedures/)).<br><br>An initiative launched in July 2026, backed by an April ministerial decision forming a cross-agency committee, aims to close the war-widened gap between economic activity and registered taxpayers, citing an estimate that over 60 per cent of activity sits outside the net ([initiative](https://sudannewsgazette.com/initiative-to-broaden-tax-base-launched-to-promote-tax-equity-and-support-the-national-economy/)).<br><br>The electronic invoice system went live from the start of May, linking invoice data to taxpayer accounts ([launch](https://sudanhorizon.com/tax-chamber-announces-official-launch-of-electronic-invoice-system/)).</details> | Advanced |
