| Tanzania | [An integrated revenue administration system went live in February 2026](https://dailynews.co.tz/tra-welcomes-taxpayers-at-the-official-launch-of-the-idras-system/), and [the budget commits to registering all local traders](https://www.mof.go.tz/uploads/text-editor/files/GOVERNMENT%20BUDGET%20%20SPEECH---ENGLISH%20VERSION_1781243659.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The tax administration statute requires taxpayer registration and identifier issuance linked to the national identification number](https://oagmis.oag.go.tz/portal/acts/revised/538/download).<br><br>[The revenue authority completed its integrated domestic revenue administration system with pre-launch verification of migrated taxpayer records ahead of a 9 February 2026 go-live](https://dailynews.co.tz/tra-welcomes-taxpayers-at-the-official-launch-of-the-idras-system/).<br><br>[The 2026/27 budget commits the authority to integrate that system with the property register for direct data exchange, to apply artificial intelligence, big data and blockchain, and to register buildings, owners' identity and tax details and all local traders](https://www.mof.go.tz/uploads/text-editor/files/GOVERNMENT%20BUDGET%20%20SPEECH---ENGLISH%20VERSION_1781243659.pdf).<br><br>No registered-taxpayer count and no migration-error rate is published against any of it.</details> | Advanced |
| Togo | The register reached [100,292 taxpayers for 2024](https://www.otr.tg/index.php/fr/documentation/rapports-de-performance-otr/596-otr-rapport-de-performance-2024/file.html) and became a published verification service in April 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The 2024 performance report puts the taxpayer population at 100,292, of whom 73,239 active, the active population [up 24.0 per cent on end-2023](https://www.otr.tg/index.php/fr/documentation/rapports-de-performance-otr/596-otr-rapport-de-performance-2024/file.html), with 95,535 new taxpayers registered in the year and 30,459 individual numbers issued online, 38.0 per cent of those created.<br><br>On [30 April 2026](https://otr.tg/index.php/fr/blog/avis-communiques/1571-avis-aux-operateurs-economiques-n-022156-2026-otr-cg-ci-dim-relatif-a-la-consultation-du-repertoire-actualise-des-contribuables-actifs.html) the updated register of active taxpayers was put online, with operators directed to check a supplier's active status before transacting under article 196 of the general tax code — a change in what the register does rather than in its size.<br><br>A [communique of 29 January 2026](https://otr.tg/index.php/fr/blog/avis-communiques/1557-communique-n-002-2025-otr-cg-ci-dcf-relatif-a-l-utilisation-de-la-plateforme-du-guichet-unique-de-depot-des-etats-financiers-gudef.html) requires an account on the financial-statements single window before electronic filing.</details> | Advanced |
| Tunisia | [Tax-file and transfer-pricing filings became exclusively electronic in March 2026](https://www.tunisienumerique.com/dgi-depot-obligatoire-des-declarations-fiscales-via-la-plateforme-tej/), on a register whose [identifier procedure dates from 2008](https://www.finances.gov.tn/fr/octroi-dun-identifiant-fiscal). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The finance ministry's own service description has the tax identifier granted by the territorially competent tax-control office after a declaration of existence, under article 56 of the income and corporate tax code and a decree of January 2008](https://www.finances.gov.tn/fr/octroi-dun-identifiant-fiscal).<br><br>[The tax administration made filing of the tax file and transfer-pricing declarations exclusively electronic through its TEJ platform in March 2026, and required taxpayers to regularise omitted filings and update legal-representative identity data on it](https://www.tunisienumerique.com/dgi-depot-obligatoire-des-declarations-fiscales-via-la-plateforme-tej/).<br><br>[A February 2026 ministerial order separately mandates an electronic tax identity card sealed by the national certification agency and issued within one working day through the business register](https://lake.jort.tn/journal-officiel/fr/2026/019.pdf).<br><br>No count of registered taxpayers or of filings made electronically is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Zambia | Register holders [rose to 6,571,699 while active tax accounts rose only from 379,985 to 388,779](https://zambianbusinesstimes.com/only-388k-out-of-6-5-million-tpin-holders-pay-tax-zra/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[A 2023 amendment made obtaining a taxpayer identification number a statutory obligation, effective 1 January 2024](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/sites/default/files/documents/acts/Act%20No.%2022%20of%202023%2C%20The%20Income%20Tax%20%28Amendment%29.pdf).<br><br>2026-07 — [the revenue authority's annual report shows register holders growing from 6,015,533 in 2024 to 6,571,699 in 2025, while active tax accounts rose only from 379,985 to 388,779](https://zambianbusinesstimes.com/only-388k-out-of-6-5-million-tpin-holders-pay-tax-zra/). Six per cent of the register is active. A statutory registration obligation produces a register, and this is what a register produced that way looks like.<br><br>2025-10 — [an amendment bill enabling information exchange among revenue authority divisions and with the entities it establishes was tabled, commencing 1 January 2026](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/sites/default/files/documents/bills/N.A.B%2022%20of%202025.pdf), which is the reform aimed at the gap.</details> | Mixed, the register grew while active accounts barely moved |
| Zimbabwe | The tax register runs on a platform fully implemented in 2024; a new statutory registration category took effect in January 2026 and a compulsory [master data update](https://zimra.co.zw/public-notices?download=4482%3Apublic-notice-18-of-2026-update-of-taxpayer-records) closed in April. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The revenue authority's tax and revenue management system reached full implementation with a fifth-module release on 1 October 2024, adding case management, debt management, investigations and audit and risk and compliance, with taxpayer-facing services through a self-service portal ([notice](https://www.zimra.co.zw/public-notices?download=4079%3Apublic-notice-73-tarms-release-3-go-live)); a second phase integrating third-party stakeholders is being readied.<br><br>The Finance Act 2025 inserted a new Part IIIB into the Income Tax Act with effect from 1 January 2026, creating a compulsory registration category of registrable proprietors, the owners, lessees and sub-lessees of premises let to tenants liable for presumptive tax ([Act](https://zimlii.org/akn/zw/act/2025/7/eng@2025-12-29/source)).<br><br>Every taxpayer was required to confirm and correct the names, contacts and business classifications held for them by 30 April 2026 ([update](https://zimra.co.zw/public-notices?download=4482%3Apublic-notice-18-of-2026-update-of-taxpayer-records)).<br><br>No registered-taxpayer count is published at any date, so neither the statutory expansion nor the cleanse can be measured.</details> | Advanced |

## Land register

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | An [electronic land-title system went nationwide on 13 January 2026](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/public-management/algeria-moves-to-electronic-land-titles-with-amlak-system), with end-to-end traceability. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system did not exist at the window's opening. 2026-01 - it [went nationwide with end-to-end traceability, and no coverage figure is published](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/public-management/algeria-moves-to-electronic-land-titles-with-amlak-system).<br><br>No count of titles issued, parcels covered or transactions processed has followed. It is one of six registers or platforms this country put into service inside the window, all announced as live and none accompanied by a volume figure - which is the shape of the whole govtech position here.</details> | Advanced |
| Angola | A digital platform [linking the cadastre, the tax authority and the property registry targets 40,000 properties legalised by end-2026](https://novojornal.co.ao/politica/detalhe/executivo-promete-legalizar-40-mil-imoveis-ate-ao-final-de-2026-71209.html), with no progress published since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-04-17 — [a digital platform linking the cadastre, the tax authority and the property registry was set a target of 40,000 properties legalised by the end of 2026, aimed at cutting duplication and enabling mortgage access](https://novojornal.co.ao/politica/detalhe/executivo-promete-legalizar-40-mil-imoveis-ate-ao-final-de-2026-71209.html).<br><br>No progress figure against that target has been published in the four months since, and no source states whether the platform is in service, which agency operates it or how the three registries it links exchange data. A target with no reporting against it is the same shape of evidence as the digital transition agenda's project count: a number the government publishes about its own intentions.<br><br>It is the only land or property system on this ledger.</details> | Advanced |
| Benin | The national cadastre [records 445,695 parcels with the registration certificate mandatory for any transaction](https://fraternite.bj/modernisation-de-landf-e-foncier-au-service-de-la-securite-des-investissements/), and [a land governance programme finalised its operations manual](https://andf.bj/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/ANDF-TERRA-RF-006-V1.0_VERSION-FINALE-MOP-TERRA-BENIN-CONSULTANT-ADJIBI-17-OCTOBRE-2025.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-03-26 — [445,695 parcels are registered nationally and a decree makes the cadastre registration certificate mandatory for any transaction, legalising sales without a full title but only through the platform](https://fraternite.bj/modernisation-de-landf-e-foncier-au-service-de-la-securite-des-investissements/), against [491,420 parcels across twelve communes in February 2025](https://lanation.bj/actualites/reforme-du-e-foncier-au-benin-plus-de-491-000-parcelles-enregistrees-dans-12-communes).<br><br>The two figures are not comparable and the base does not net them: the earlier is a commune-level count and the later a national one. What is unambiguous is the legal change — the platform is now the only lawful route to a transaction, which converts a registry into a gate.<br><br>2025-10-17 — [a national land governance programme finalised its operations manual, run by the land agency with World Bank support](https://andf.bj/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/ANDF-TERRA-RF-006-V1.0_VERSION-FINALE-MOP-TERRA-BENIN-CONSULTANT-ADJIBI-17-OCTOBRE-2025.pdf), sitting alongside the cadastre platform rather than replacing it.</details> | Advanced |
| Botswana | The deeds registry [remains substantially manual on a workload of 9,000 to 30,000 transactions a year](https://www.mmegi.bw/business/botswana-registry-faces-backlog/news), with no digitisation programme, budget line or date on the record. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-10-15 — [the deputy registrar described a registry that is substantially manual, carrying 9,000 to 30,000 transactions a year since the 2017-18 land Acts with no added resources](https://www.mmegi.bw/business/botswana-registry-faces-backlog/news). Nothing has moved it since, and no digitisation programme, budget line or date appears.<br><br>[About 70% of the country's land is tribal land, brought into registration by the 2018 Tribal Land Act](https://www.mmegi.bw/business/botswana-registry-faces-backlog/news), so the workload the manual registry carries is the product of a deliberate widening of what must be registered.<br><br>It is the clearest untouched administrative system in this report: every other registry described here is either being built, tested or funded, and this one is not on anybody's list.</details> | No change |
| Burkina Faso | The land and cadastre platform [became the operating layer of a reformed land regime](https://www.sidwaya.info/reorganisation-agraire-et-fonciere-la-nouvelle-loi-un-rempart-contre-les-derives/), with [development now verified from satellite imagery without a site visit](https://www.sidwaya.info/des-innovations-pour-mieux-gerer-le-foncier-conseil-des-ministres/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Land-occupation titles have been issued online since March 2025, with a cadastral extract in a day and a definitive title in sixty](https://www.sidwaya.info/foncier-au-burkina-sycd-pour-une-meilleure-gestion-des-terres/). 2026-06-29 — [an October 2025 law and a March 2026 decree give the platform statutory footing as the operating layer of a reformed land regime, with physical files no longer circulating between administrations and falsified titles rejected by the system](https://www.sidwaya.info/reorganisation-agraire-et-fonciere-la-nouvelle-loi-un-rempart-contre-les-derives/).<br><br>The same decree changes how development is proved: [the tax administration verifies development from satellite imagery without a site visit, instructing a file in a day, and the development period is extended from five to eight years](https://www.sidwaya.info/des-innovations-pour-mieux-gerer-le-foncier-conseil-des-ministres/). Those two sources are the entire satellite-data holding for this country.<br><br>No figure for titles or extracts actually issued through the platform is published. Beside it, [the computerised mining cadastre is being modernised and expanded by its supplier under a World Bank-financed land and mining governance project](https://www.gaf.de/gaf-ag-to-upgrade-burkina-fasos-mining-cadastre-system-emc-under-the-world-bank-funded-pargfm-project/), with no delivery date or contract value stated.</details> | Advanced |
| Burundi | Land titles [are digitised, with about 123,000 holders to be secured](https://burundi-eco.com/le-gouvernement-accelere-t-il-la-digitalisation-des-services-publics/), and [a mandatory electronic-title fee drew objections on cost and deadline](https://www.iwacu-burundi.org/titres-fonciers-securises-une-mesure-couteuse-et-precipitee/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Land services were digitised on an integrated platform in June 2025](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/burundi-s-land-services-go-digital-with-new-integrated-platform), and 2026-06-12 — [the minister's stock-take put about 123,000 title holders as those to be secured digitally](https://burundi-eco.com/le-gouvernement-accelere-t-il-la-digitalisation-des-services-publics/).<br><br>2026-04-01 — [an ordinance making the electronic land title fee mandatory drew objections on its cost and on the shortness of its deadline](https://www.iwacu-burundi.org/titres-fonciers-securises-une-mesure-couteuse-et-precipitee/). No response, revision or enforcement figure has followed.<br><br>It is the most complete register on this ledger and the only one charging the public directly to use it. No count of titles actually converted, no processing time and no dispute figure is published.</details> | Advanced, with the mandatory fee contested |
| Cameroon | A [memorandum of 28 April 2026 covers about 200,000 land files and a digital possession certificate](https://leconomie.info/douala-la-mairie-de-ville-veut-digitaliser-son-cadastre-et-creer-un-certificat-de-possession-numerique/), while the [budget line fell](https://rfp.cm/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/PROJET-DE-LOI-FINANCES-2026_FR_26112025.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-04-28 - a memorandum was signed to [digitise about 200,000 land files at Douala, with a digital possession certificate, and no budget, timeline or start date published](https://leconomie.info/douala-la-mairie-de-ville-veut-digitaliser-son-cadastre-et-creer-un-certificat-de-possession-numerique/).<br><br>The money went the other way. The lands ministry's cadastre modernisation programme was [tabled at FCFA 874,046,000 for FY2026 against FCFA 1,567,500,000 appropriated for FY2025](https://rfp.cm/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/PROJET-DE-LOI-FINANCES-2026_FR_26112025.pdf), a 44.2% cut and below the FY2024 level, at proposed stage only. It is the only digital line outside the sector vote, so the cut is the whole of the state's own cadastre financing story.</details> | Mixed, a Douala cadastre memorandum was signed while the lands ministry's own cadastre line was cut 44.2% |
| Cape Verde | [LMITS is functioning and used for most land transactions on four islands](https://www.mcc.gov/resources/doc/evalbrief-111022-cvp-ii-land-investment/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[An independent evaluation published on 1 November 2022 found the Land Management, Information and Technology System functioning, integrated into the transaction process and used to process most land transactions; it was built under the US$17.5m Land Management for Investment project inside MCC's US$65.6m Cabo Verde II Compact, covering Sal, Boa Vista, Sao Vicente and Maio, and the national land management institute has continued the cadastre work since the compact closed](https://www.mcc.gov/resources/doc/evalbrief-111022-cvp-ii-land-investment/). [The territory institute runs the property cadastre as a service of its own](https://ingt.gov.cv/ingt/Servi%C3%A7os/cadastro-predial/).<br><br>No national cadastral coverage figure is held, and nothing states the position on the six islands the compact did not cover.</details> | No change, a standing position the base holds for the first time |
| Central African Republic | 122 tokenised plots [sold for about US$38,308 by November 2025](https://globalinitiative.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/Behind-the-blockchain-Cryptocurrency-and-criminal-capture-in-the-Central-African-Republic-Central-Africa-Observatory-on-Organized-Crime-and-Violence-December-2025.pdf), while the cadastre carries [FCFA 625,000 for the mining register and FCFA 650,000 for a petroleum database](http://www.finances.gouv.cf/sites/default/files/2026-03/Cadre%20des%20D%C3%A9penses%20%C3%A0%20Moyen%20Terme%202026.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Tokenised land concessions opened in June 2025 under presidential decree over 1,700 hectares](https://itweb.africa/article/car-offers-land-ownership-using-cryptocurrency/Olx4z7kaAGbq56km), and [122 plots had sold for about US$38,308 by November 2025 at a fixed price of about US$314 each, with a flagged expansion to mineral rights not recorded as happening](https://globalinitiative.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/Behind-the-blockchain-Cryptocurrency-and-criminal-capture-in-the-Central-African-Republic-Central-Africa-Observatory-on-Organized-Crime-and-Violence-December-2025.pdf). The terms are unreconciled between the two accounts, which agree on location but not on pricing.<br><br>The registry underneath has not been built. [A US$1.6m agreement of April 2024 covered a second phase of public-administration digitalisation including an electronic cadastre](https://oubanguimedias.com/2024/04/14/le-pnud-et-le-gouvernement-centrafricain-signent-un-accord-de-1-6-millions-de-dollars-pour-le-projet-de-digitalisation/), no cadastre delivery record is held, and [the 2026 framework carries FCFA 625,000 for updating the mining cadastre and FCFA 650,000 for a petroleum database](http://www.finances.gouv.cf/sites/default/files/2026-03/Cadre%20des%20D%C3%A9penses%20%C3%A0%20Moyen%20Terme%202026.pdf) — FCFA 1,275,000 for the year between them.<br><br>2026-07-31 — [a memorandum with a Gulf counterparty adds land-administration modernisation to the stated programme](https://www.journaldebangui.com/rca-bangui-renforce-son-partenariat-avec-les-emirats-arabes/), with no commitment figure or governance arrangement.</details> | Mixed, land was tokenised and sold while the cadastre it would rest on has no delivery record |
| Chad | The [1967 land law](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/cha38421.pdf) still governs; [261 state reserves were registered](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/08/21/foncier-vaste-operation-pour-securiser-les-reserves-de-letat/) in August 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The 1967 law is the operative instrument: registration is the procedure by which ownership is established, the titre foncier is the registered title, and custody of deeds and inscription of transfers sit with the conservateur ([law](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/cha38421.pdf)); unregistered land is presumed state-owned.<br><br>An interministerial committee reported 261 state reserves recovered, enumerated and registered in August 2026, the only in-window registration count found, and ordered clearance of the eighty-metre strips and the green belt within three months ([committee](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/08/21/foncier-vaste-operation-pour-securiser-les-reserves-de-letat/)).<br><br>A joint arrete of 17 August 2026 instituted a coordination mechanism putting cadastral and technical land data in front of the judges hearing land disputes ([mechanism](https://www.alwihdainfo.com/tchad-un-nouveau-mecanisme-conjoint-pour-renforcer-le-traitement-des-dossiers-fonciers/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Comoros | The [land directorate is mandated for cadastre, digitisation and a digital land database](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/com225911.pdf); a [new director general took it in September 2025](https://lagazettedescomores.com/soci%C3%A9t%C3%A9/nominations-deux-nouvelles-directions-voient-le-jour-.html). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [decree of 2 May 2023 creates the national land and property directorate with nationwide scope and island offices, and assigns it at article 4 title registration, land conservation, cadastre and topography, the digitisation and electronic archiving of land documents, and the digital land database under the national property registration programme](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/com225911.pdf).<br><br>2025-09 - the president [appointed a former regional director of taxes and domains as director general by decree of 19 September 2025, a large land-sector reform announced as forthcoming without its text or funding being named](https://lagazettedescomores.com/soci%C3%A9t%C3%A9/nominations-deux-nouvelles-directions-voient-le-jour-.html).<br><br>A mandate and a reorganisation are not a register. Nothing states how many titles exist, how many are digitised, or whether the database has been built.</details> | No change, a new director general over the same mandate and no digitisation figure |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The [urban land system now records and tracks every land application in real time](https://www.capital-foncier.com/en/blog/sigfu-and-idufci-how-cadastral-digitalization-is-transforming-ivorian-land-management), and the [first electronic rural land certificates were signed](https://www.abidjaneconomie.net/2026/03/15/dematerialisation-du-foncier-rural-les-premiers-certificats-fonciers-electroniques-signes-dans-le-moronou/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The urban system was created by decret n 2021-862 of 15 December 2021 and is now [the central platform on which every land application - definitive concession order, subdivision, transfer - is recorded and tracked in real time, holding each parcel's history and connecting the tax, land and urban-planning administrations, with an electronic signature in use since January 2024](https://www.capital-foncier.com/en/blog/sigfu-and-idufci-how-cadastral-digitalization-is-transforming-ivorian-land-management), [reported again in a land and urban-planning programme](https://www.economie-ivoirienne.ci/en/node/362). Its parcel identifier was [instituted by decret n 2019-221 of 13 March 2019](https://civlii.laws.africa/en/akn/ci/act/decree/2019/221/fra@2023-08-09).<br><br>2026-03-12 - the [first five electronic rural land certificates were signed by the prefect of the Moronou region](https://www.abidjaneconomie.net/2026/03/15/dematerialisation-du-foncier-rural-les-premiers-certificats-fonciers-electroniques-signes-dans-le-moronou/).<br><br>The [rural land information system created by ordonnance n 2025-85 of 12 February 2025](https://civlii.laws.africa/en/akn/ci/act/ord/2025/85/fra@2025-06-04) has nothing on file on what has been built, and no count of parcels or files processed is published for either system.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | The [digitised land books are the register’s operative legal form](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-n006-an-18-8eme-l-modifiant-et-completant-la-loi-n177-an-91-2eme-l-du-10-octobre-1991-portant-organisation-de-la-propriete-fonciere/); a [national land policy was validated in December 2025](https://igad.int/djibouti-advances-its-national-land-policy-for-inclusive-and-sustainable-land-governance/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The standing position is a 2018 amendment to the 1991 property law, which [gives legal effect to digitised land books and a centralised electronic database as the operative form of the register, requires every registered private parcel to be mapped and recorded, and lets the register cross-check identity documents against the national population database](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-n006-an-18-8eme-l-modifiant-et-completant-la-loi-n177-an-91-2eme-l-du-10-octobre-1991-portant-organisation-de-la-propriete-fonciere/).<br><br>2025-12-06 - a [validation workshop at Tadjourah reviewed the draft national land policy, covering tenure security, land-use planning and access for women, youth and vulnerable groups](https://igad.int/djibouti-advances-its-national-land-policy-for-inclusive-and-sustainable-land-governance/).<br><br>No count of parcels registered or digitised is published, so how much of the territory the register covers is not established.</details> | Advanced |
| DR Congo | [Loi 25-062 on the land and property regime came into force in December 2025](https://dbz-avocats.com/uploads/98ddd346fa7efb0ada6d0a8985cf8830/J.O.S-du-20.01.2026_-RDC-LOI-REGIME-FONCIER-ET-IMMO.pdf), with a [national land plan to 2040](https://www.affairesfoncieres.gouv.cd/actualites/rdc-le-gouvernement-adopte-le-plan-foncier-national-2026-2040-tournant-historique-pour-la-gestion-des-terres). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Loi 25-062 on the land and property regime was enacted on 30 December 2025](https://dbz-avocats.com/uploads/98ddd346fa7efb0ada6d0a8985cf8830/J.O.S-du-20.01.2026_-RDC-LOI-REGIME-FONCIER-ET-IMMO.pdf). [A national land plan for 2026 to 2040 followed in June 2026](https://www.affairesfoncieres.gouv.cd/actualites/rdc-le-gouvernement-adopte-le-plan-foncier-national-2026-2040-tournant-historique-pour-la-gestion-des-terres), [a national land and property register went to Senate reform discussion](https://www.affairesfoncieres.gouv.cd/actualites/senat-se-mme-oneige-nsele-reaffirme-les-innovations-et-les-garanties-de-securite-juridique-portees-par-la-reforme-fonciere), and [digitalisation of the land registry was reported in August 2026](https://apanews.net/dr-congo-land-registry-begins-digitalisation-process/).<br><br>No parcel count, coverage figure or completion date for the digitalisation is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Egypt | The [state-land platform has cleared a small fraction of its intake](https://www.youm7.com/story/2026/8/4/%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B5%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%88%D8%B7%D9%86%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D8%AA%D9%84%D9%82%D9%89-302-%D8%A3%D9%84%D9%81-%D8%B7%D9%84%D8%A8-%D8%AA%D9%82%D9%86%D9%8A%D9%86-%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B1%D8%A7%D8%B6%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AF%D9%88%D9%84%D8%A9/7503179), and the [property identifier law has no implementing decree](https://invest-gate.me/features/egypt-moves-forward-with-property-id-law-to-digitize-real-estate-sector). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The platform records [more than 302,000 applications received against about 1,700 contracts drawn up, 350 plots recovered and compensation collected on about 300 applications, with governorate committees still doing inspection, survey and measurement, while on the older statutory track the committee reports more than 124,000 regularisation contracts delivered, over 95%](https://www.youm7.com/story/2026/8/4/%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B5%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%88%D8%B7%D9%86%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D8%AA%D9%84%D9%82%D9%89-302-%D8%A3%D9%84%D9%81-%D8%B7%D9%84%D8%A8-%D8%AA%D9%82%D9%86%D9%8A%D9%86-%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B1%D8%A7%D8%B6%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AF%D9%88%D9%84%D8%A9/7503179).<br><br>The [property identifier law was approved by the House on 27 April 2025, mandating a unique digital identifier for every property tied to a unified digital base map and making it a prerequisite for registry or notary registration and for utility connections](https://invest-gate.me/features/egypt-moves-forward-with-property-id-law-to-digitize-real-estate-sector); no decree naming the overseeing authority, no executive regulations and no commencement date are on record fifteen months later.<br><br>One survey section's [title register was deferred by a year to 1 July 2027](https://www.youm7.com/story/2026/8/4/%D9%88%D8%B2%D9%8A%D8%B1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%AF%D9%84-%D9%8A%D8%A4%D8%AC%D9%84-%D8%AA%D8%B7%D8%A8%D9%8A%D9%82-%D9%86%D8%B8%D8%A7%D9%85-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B3%D8%AC%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86%D9%89-%D8%A8%D9%85%D8%AF%D9%8A%D9%86%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B4%D8%A3%D8%A9-%D9%81%D9%89/7502518), the country moving section by section under a [1964 statute](https://metropolitiques.eu/Securing-Land-Tenure-in-Egypt-Who-Needs-Registered-Titles.html). Ministers [met on 20 August 2026 to take forward registry digitisation](https://www.youm7.com/story/2026/8/20/%D9%88%D8%B2%D9%8A%D8%B1%D8%A7-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%AF%D9%84-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%AE%D8%B7%D9%8A%D8%B7-%D9%8A%D8%A8%D8%AD%D8%AB%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85%D9%86%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%87%D8%B1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D9%82%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%89-%D9%88%D8%AA%D9%88%D8%B3%D9%8A%D8%B9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AE%D8%AF%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D9%83%D9%86%D9%88%D9%84%D9%88%D8%AC%D9%8A%D8%A9/7519133) with [no protocol, timetable or service list published](https://sis.gov.eg/en/media-center/news/justice-investment-ministries-sign-pact-to-digitize-investor-services/).</details> | Mixed, a state-land regularisation platform took over 302,000 applications while clearing under 1% of them, the property identifier law has no implementing decree and one title-register section was deferred a year |
| Equatorial Guinea | The [first house-to-house cadastral census began at Bata](https://mofuen.com/2026/06/16/ordenacion-de-tierras-pone-en-marcha-en-bata-la-primera-fase-del-censo-catastral/) in June 2026 under the 2025 cadastre law. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Title has rested on the [2009 land law's concession basis, with registration of customary tenure optional](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/eqg158220.pdf), and on an 1906 survey statute that the [cadastre bill approved by deputies in May 2025 replaces](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/aprobacion_del_proyecto_de_ley_sobre_la_creacion_y_establecimiento_del_catastro_nacional_de_tierras).<br><br>Field work started in [June 2026 at Bata, house to house](https://mofuen.com/2026/06/16/ordenacion-de-tierras-pone-en-marcha-en-bata-la-primera-fase-del-censo-catastral/). The consequences are already reaching households: [residents along one highway were given a year to regularise title as the register forces case-by-case checks](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/el_gobierno_otorga_un_a%C3%B1o_a_los_residentes_de_la_autopista_malabo-sipopo_para_regularizar_sus_titulos_de_propiedad).</details> | Advanced |
| Eritrea | Land registration rests on the 1997 proclamation establishing the cadastral office, with no statement of its operational extent ([proclamation](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/eri11855.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The 1997 land registration proclamation established the Cadastral Office under the land, water and environment ministry, responsible for registering all land, rights over land and transfers of property, following the 1994 change of tenure under which all land belongs to the state with allocable private rights ([proclamation](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/eri11855.pdf)).<br><br>The proclamation establishes the office and not the register's reach. The base holds no parcel count, no coverage figure and no statement that the register is held digitally.</details> | No change |
| Eswatini | The [deeds registry is still manual](https://lands.go.ke/eswatini-delegation-returns-kenya-benchmarking-lands-digitization), with the registrar stating an intention to move to a fully electronic system after two study missions in five months. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A delegation from the deeds registry and the science and technology park made a five-day study visit to a neighbouring land registry in August 2025, the second such mission in five months, the first having been led by the principal secretary for natural resources and energy ([mission](https://lands.go.ke/eswatini-delegation-returns-kenya-benchmarking-lands-digitization)).<br><br>The registrar stated that registration and storage of deeds remain manual and that the country is working to upgrade them into a fully electronic system ([position](https://lands.go.ke/eswatini-delegation-returns-kenya-benchmarking-lands-digitization)).<br><br>No system, procurement, budget line or date for that upgrade is on record, and no parcel or title count is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Ethiopia | A [cloud-hosted land services platform](https://land.et) integrated with the national identity and payment systems is in service, with nothing published about its reach. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The platform carries document upload, electronic signature and online payment, is integrated with the national identity and payment systems, and offers a mobile interface for citizens and officials ([platform](https://land.et)).<br><br>Its own site is the held record, retrieved from a web archive after the domain failed to resolve. No parcel count, city coverage or operator is established, and a land platform whose domain does not resolve is itself the finding.</details> | No change |
| Gabon | A [proposal for an integrated urban land management system](https://www.gabonreview.com/modernisation-fonciere-la-digitalisation-au-service-dun-titre-foncier-pour-chaque-gabonais/) was submitted in December 2025, against about 1,200 cession decisions signed that year. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The proposal was submitted with drone purchases announced alongside it, and no system, contract award or delivery date is held ([cadastre proposal](https://www.gabonreview.com/modernisation-fonciere-la-digitalisation-au-service-dun-titre-foncier-pour-chaque-gabonais/), [modernisation](http://gouvernement.ga/2025/11/24/foncier-et-numerique-le-gabon-engage-la-modernisation-de-son-cadastre-avec-lign-fi)).<br><br>Ministers told public television on 30 November 2025 that about 1,200 cession decisions were signed in 2025, with about 3,000 beneficiaries owing about FCFA 14bn in unpaid fees ([cession figures](https://www.gabonreview.com/un-gabonais-un-titre-foncier-1-200-decisions-de-cession-en-2024-et-14-milliards-de-fcfa-dimpayes/)).<br><br>A proposal was received rather than awarded, so no engagement, signature or value is established, and the cession figures are a television statement rather than a register return.</details> | Advanced |
| Gambia | The [first unified national land policy](https://policies.gov.gm/f/6d4b253b-ef9d-11f0-b086-029254d29bb1) mandates a digitalised land administration system and a centralised land information system. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The policy for 2026 to 2035, published in August 2026, also requires digitised land registration alongside new certificates of customary ownership, backed by a costed ten-year implementation roadmap ([policy](https://policies.gov.gm/f/6d4b253b-ef9d-11f0-b086-029254d29bb1)).<br><br>It is a policy mandate rather than a build: no supplier, budget line, custodian or start date for either system is stated, and no data-protection provision for the land record is reported despite a data-protection statute enacted the year before.</details> | Advanced |
| Ghana | A [cadastre programme expected to start in 2023](https://lc.gov.gh/landconference/materials/NLC-Fit-for-purpose%20cadastral.pdf) has no dated progress on file, while a planning system is being rebuilt inside a GHS 36,454,968 sub-programme. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The cadastre date is a year-precision proxy off an undated slide deck and nothing has followed ([cadastre](https://lc.gov.gh/landconference/materials/NLC-Fit-for-purpose%20cadastral.pdf)).<br><br>The land use planning and management information system is being redeveloped into a web-based platform for spatial planning, development control and street addressing, with interoperability against existing government systems consulted on; it sits inside a spatial-planning sub-programme appropriated GHS 36,454,968 for 2026 from which no digital share is separable, runs with donor support that is not costed in the estimates, and has no launch date, contractor or district count published ([planning system](https://mofep.gov.gh/sites/default/files/pbb-estimates/2026/2026-PBB-MLGCRA.pdf)).</details> | No change |
| Guinea | A [first phase of the Systeme d'Information Foncier covering Conakry and thirteen towns was agreed in October 2025](https://guineejet.info/lancement-du-sif-le-ministre-mory-conde-engage-la-guinee-vers-une-gouvernance-fonciere-moderne/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The urbanism minister and Geo Consult International set the terms of the first implementation phase of the Systeme d'Information Foncier, covering Conakry and thirteen other towns, on five pillars: high-resolution aerial imagery, a structured parcel plan for Conakry, a multi-actor land survey campaign, the system itself, and the supporting technical infrastructure and training; financing for a national phase was under discussion with the Islamic Development Bank](https://guineejet.info/lancement-du-sif-le-ministre-mory-conde-engage-la-guinee-vers-une-gouvernance-fonciere-moderne/).<br><br>[Separately, mapping of State agricultural land began in Boke, Conakry, Kankan and Kindia on 6 October 2025 under the agricultural land reform, centralising areas and coordinates in a national database connected to a future e-Foncier rural platform](https://www.agronewsguinee.net/2025/10/reforme-fonciere-lancement-de-la-cartographie-des-terres-agricoles-en-guinee/).<br><br>Both are first phases: no parcel count, coverage figure or completion date is held for either.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea-Bissau | Eight states [approved a draft directive harmonising land registration](https://croissanceafrique.com/uemoa-directive-sur-lharmonisation-des-regles-regissant-limmatriculation-et-la-propriete-fonciere-au-sein-de-lunion/) in June 2026; no domestic register is described. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The base holds no account of a Guinean land register, cadastre or titling system.<br><br>The movement is regional and prospective: eight member states [approved a draft directive harmonising land registration and titling rules across the union](https://croissanceafrique.com/uemoa-directive-sur-lharmonisation-des-regles-regissant-limmatriculation-et-la-propriete-fonciere-au-sein-de-lunion/) in June 2026. No domestic transposition or register is recorded.</details> | Advanced |
| Kenya | The [land platform has been unavailable since 1 September 2026](https://eastleighvoice.co.ke/news/396368/lsk-demands-urgent-restoration-of-ardhisasa-platform-amid-land-transaction-delays), stalling searches, transfers, registrations and valuations, with no cause and no restoration timeline given. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The platform went down on 1 September 2026 and the advocates' professional body demanded its restoration, saying conveyancing and land transactions had stalled with no adequate communication on the cause or on when services would resume; it asked for an explanation of the outage's nature and extent, a restoration timeline, contingency measures for time-sensitive transactions, an assurance that users incur no penalties or loss of priority, and an incident-notification protocol ([outage](https://eastleighvoice.co.ke/news/396368/lsk-demands-urgent-restoration-of-ardhisasa-platform-amid-land-transaction-delays)).<br><br>The body notes that the State migrated essential land services to the platform while in some instances restricting manual alternatives, so the outage has no fallback ([outage](https://eastleighvoice.co.ke/news/396368/lsk-demands-urgent-restoration-of-ardhisasa-platform-amid-land-transaction-delays)).<br><br>No county-rollout update has been on file since July 2025 and no national coverage count is held ([rollout](https://lands.go.ke/isiolo-county-onboarded-ardhisasa-more-counties-embrace-platform)).</details> | Regressed |
| Lesotho | The land authority is [taking register services into the districts](https://www.lena.gov.ls/laa-brings-services-closer-to-communities/), with new offices planned. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2010-06-15 — [the Land Administration Authority Act charges the authority with maintaining a complete land-holding database](https://lesotholii.org/akn/ls/act/2010/9/eng@2010-06-15).<br><br>2025-08-05 — [district outreach expanded access to register services, with new district offices planned](https://www.lena.gov.ls/laa-brings-services-closer-to-communities/).<br><br>2025-08-27 — [the authority put its 2026-2029 strategic plan out to tender, prioritising technology adoption for registration services](https://selibeng.com/call-for-bids-strategic-planning-exercise/).<br><br>No parcel count, coverage figure or registration time is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Liberia | The land authority took a [national spatial data infrastructure](https://www.liberianobserver.com/news/lla-takes-liberia-s-land-modernization-drive-to-un-geospatial-forum/article_6395fd70-5dfc-496a-bc1c-944a19aff053.html) to a United Nations geospatial session in August 2026, seeking partnerships and technical support. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It would build a coordinated geospatial framework for land administration, mapping and national planning ([approach](https://www.liberianobserver.com/news/lla-takes-liberia-s-land-modernization-drive-to-un-geospatial-forum/article_6395fd70-5dfc-496a-bc1c-944a19aff053.html)).<br><br>It is an approach for support rather than a programme: no funding, custodian, standard or timetable is stated, and no land register, title count or digitisation figure appears anywhere in the base.</details> | Advanced |
| Libya | [Benghazi resumed limited registry transactions in April 2026](https://almarsad.co/2026/04/26/%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%aa%d8%b3%d8%ac%d9%8a%d9%84-%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%b9%d9%82%d8%a7%d8%b1%d9%8a-%d9%81%d9%8a-%d8%a8%d9%86%d8%ba%d8%a7%d8%b2%d9%8a-%d9%8a%d8%b4%d8%b1%d8%b9-%d8%aa%d8%af%d8%b1%d9%8a%d8%ac%d9%8a/), against a suspension standing since 2011. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registration authority has [issued certificates only, with substantive registration suspended since 2011](https://aladel.gov.ly/home/%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%B3%D8%AC%D9%8A%D9%84%E2%80%AD-%E2%80%AC%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D9%82%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%8A%E2%80%AD-%E2%80%AC%D8%A8%D9%8A%D9%86%E2%80%AD-%E2%80%AC%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%82%D8%B9/), and [cadastral mapping covers two-thirds of the country on a fragmented spatial data infrastructure](https://unhabitat.org/sites/default/files/2024/02/land_admininistration_and_land_rights_for_peace_and_development_in_libya.pdf).<br><br>In [April 2026 the Benghazi office resumed limited administrative transactions including mortgage and ownership transfer](https://almarsad.co/2026/04/26/%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%aa%d8%b3%d8%ac%d9%8a%d9%84-%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%b9%d9%82%d8%a7%d8%b1%d9%8a-%d9%81%d9%8a-%d8%a8%d9%86%d8%ba%d8%a7%d8%b2%d9%8a-%d9%8a%d8%b4%d8%b1%d8%b9-%d8%aa%d8%af%d8%b1%d9%8a%d8%ac%d9%8a/) despite the suspension. Municipalities are building their own holdings alongside: [a mayor reviewed registry records for a state-property inventory](https://lana.gov.ly/post.php?id=355492&lang=ar) that month.</details> | Advanced |
| Madagascar | A [national diagnostic of 524 communes](https://moov.mg/article/115878-gestion-fonciere-plus-de-911-milles-certificats-recenses-a-madagascar) in 2026 found 911,744 certificates recorded and 65 per cent of offices still on paper. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The national land programme records, as at February 2024, 35 land services, 594 communes with a decentralised land office, 699,816 land titles and 1,518,000 land certificates, and states that no integrated national land information system yet exists ([programme](https://observatoire-territoire.mg/publications/programme-national-foncier-ii/)).<br><br>A ministry diagnostic run from February to May 2026 across 524 communes recorded 911,744 certificates nationally, with 65 per cent of offices still paper-based or off-grid, an average processing time of 3.5 months and 51 per cent of certificate disputes reaching the courts ([diagnostic](https://moov.mg/article/115878-gestion-fonciere-plus-de-911-milles-certificats-recenses-a-madagascar)).<br><br>An urban development project approved in 2026 carries registry components ([project](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2026/07/06/madagascar-to-boost-urban-resilience-and-jobs-in-antananarivo-and-toamasina)).</details> | Advanced |
| Malawi | The [Land Information Management System](https://itweb.africa/article/malawi-reviews-digital-land-workflows/rW1xLv5ngAp7Rk6m) has been live since February 2024, and which transactions remain on paper is still unpublished. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The web-based system links lands, surveys and physical planning, allowing parcel viewing, document submission, notifications and payment of ground rent and application fees; the scope of remaining manual transactions is not published ([system](https://itweb.africa/article/malawi-reviews-digital-land-workflows/rW1xLv5ngAp7Rk6m)).<br><br>The ministry convened a workflow review in August 2026 to design digital procedures for transactions still on paper; which transactions remain manual, when revised workflows will be implemented and current processing times all remain unpublished.</details> | No change |
| Mali | A [land information system and one-stop land office](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/mali-unveils-digital-platforms-for-payments-land-management) launched in March 2025 and no transaction figure has been published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system and the one-stop office launched together on 18 March 2025 ([launch](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/mali-unveils-digital-platforms-for-payments-land-management)).<br><br>No parcel count, transaction figure, coverage area or link to the payment service launched the same day is published, so the base records that the office exists and not what passes through it.</details> | No change |
| Mauritania | A [decree of 27 August 2026](https://www.ami.mr/archives/267317) creates a national digital cadastral reference with a unique identifier for every parcel, where no integrated cadastral system existed. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The government's cadastre digitisation was publicly criticised as a route to silent dispossession in a country where land is at the heart of social conflict ([critique](https://kassataya.com/mauritanie-le-numerique-comme-instrument-de-depossession-silencieuse-du-foncier/)). The critique is one outlet's; what the base does not hold is any safeguard, appeal route or consultation record attached to the digitisation.<br><br>The Council of Ministers adopted a decree on 27 August 2026 establishing a national digital reference for land and cadastral data as the state's authoritative source, with a permanent, unique, non-duplicable identifier for every parcel independent of administrative divisions, the land minister stating that no integrated cadastral survey system meeting these requirements has existed ([decree](https://www.ami.mr/archives/267317)). The decree states expressly that neither the reference nor the cadastral certificate creates or extinguishes any real property right.</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritius | A [disaster recovery solution](https://registrar.govmu.org/rgd/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Communique-20.03.2026.pdf) was implemented for the electronic registry in March 2026, with deed e-submission in the budget annex. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registry operates under a statute of 1864 ([Act](https://lawsofmauritius.govmu.org/portal/viewlegislationdocument/web/?docnumber=&doctitle=VHJhbnNjcmlwdGlvbiBhbmQgTW9ydGdhZ2UgQWN0&doctype=act)), and the department reported on its performance for 2024-2025 ([report](https://mof.govmu.org/Documents/2026/Annual%20Report%20on%20Performance%20for%20Ministries%20and%20Department/7-6%20Registrar%20Generals%20Dept/RGD%20Annual%20Report%202024%20-%202025%20Final%20AMENDED.pdf)).<br><br>A disaster recovery solution was implemented for the electronic registry system in March 2026 ([communique](https://registrar.govmu.org/rgd/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Communique-20.03.2026.pdf)).<br><br>Electronic submission of land deeds is carried in the 2026-2027 budget annex ([annex](https://nationalbudget2026.govmu.org/documents/2026_27_annexBudget.pdf)). The registry is digital and resilient; the statute beneath it is 162 years old.</details> | Advanced |
| Morocco | The interior ministry began a [nationwide screening of collective land community rolls](https://www.hespress.com/الداخلية-تطلق-عملية-واسعة-لـغربلة-لو-1794573.html) in August 2026, on a single methodology for the first time. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Circulars went to community delegates through local authority officials from 22 August 2026, beginning in two regions and open to extension nationwide ([screening](https://www.hespress.com/الداخلية-تطلق-عملية-واسعة-لـغربلة-لو-1794573.html)).<br><br>Each listed member is to be verified for actual affiliation, legal majority and residence under law 62.17 and its implementing decree, applying one methodology so that criteria stop varying from one community to another. No count of communities, members or corrections is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | The [land information system](https://www.fnds.gov.mz/edocman/sigit-review-and-audit-final-report.pdf) had recorded 563,765 land-use right requests, 77 per cent processed and 68 per cent delivered to beneficiaries. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system digitises land-use right application processing, with 563,765 requests recorded, 77 per cent processed and 68 per cent delivered ([system](https://www.fnds.gov.mz/edocman/sigit-review-and-audit-final-report.pdf)).<br><br>The figures come from a 2019 review report and nothing later is held, so the country's only quantified registry position is seven years old and the gap between processed and delivered is the part worth watching.</details> | Advanced |
| Namibia | The deeds register runs under a [1937 statute](https://www.lac.org.na/laws/2021/7561.pdf) restored by court order, and a Land Bill tabled in September 2025 proposes the largest restructuring of the framework. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A government notice of June 2021 restored the Deeds Registries Act of 1937 as the operative law after a High Court order ([notice](https://www.lac.org.na/laws/2021/7561.pdf)).<br><br>The agriculture minister tabled a Land Bill in the National Assembly in September 2025, the largest proposed restructuring of the register framework on this base ([statement](https://d4f7y6nbupj5z.cloudfront.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/10/FINAL_Land_Bill_MINISTERIAL_STATEMENT_ON_LAND_BILL_BY_HON_INGE_I.pdf)).<br><br>A gazette of June 2026 exempted qualifying townships and subdivisions from standard lodging periods, an operational change inside the existing regime ([gazette](https://www.lac.org.na/laws/2026/8949.pdf)).<br><br>Business and intellectual property registration is offered online with no transaction figure published. Nothing on the base states whether the deeds register itself is digitised.</details> | Advanced |
| Niger | A [national steering committee for the electronic cadastre was installed](https://ansi.ne/?p=1143) in December 2025, on a decentralised rural register dating from 1993. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The information society agency [announced the committee's installation and its own role as delegated technical lead in December 2025](https://ansi.ne/?p=1143), presenting the unique parcel identifier platform as the infrastructure layer for a progressive national cadastre. The [ministerial meeting of 16 September 2025](https://www.gouv.ne/index.php/actualite-des-ministeres/879-compte-rendu-de-la-reunion-presidee-par-le-ministre-de-l-urbanisme-et-de-l-habitat) that preceded it recommended accelerating cadastral digitisation as a tool for land security.<br><br>The base register underneath is the [rural code of March 1993](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/ner4660.pdf), which institutes in each commune a land commission and a rural file — a rights map and individual rights sheets, opposable to third parties without being a land title.<br><br>Regionally, eight member states [approved a draft land registration harmonisation directive](https://croissanceafrique.com/uemoa-directive-sur-lharmonisation-des-regles-regissant-limmatriculation-et-la-propriete-fonciere-au-sein-de-lunion/) in June 2026, which is an approved draft rather than a transposed rule.</details> | Advanced |
| Nigeria | [More than 90 per cent of land is unregistered](https://www.thisdaylive.com/2025/03/20/with-300-billion-in-dead-capital-fg-plans-digitisation-of-land-information-system-nationwide), locking an estimated US$300bn as dead capital, while a [mortgage registry](https://fmino.gov.ng/housing-sector-stakeholders-converge-to-validate-national-housing-data-reform-policies/) is described as ready for deployment. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The national land registration and documentation programme is under way against more than 90 per cent of land unregistered, on the housing minister's figure ([programme](https://www.thisdaylive.com/2025/03/20/with-300-billion-in-dead-capital-fg-plans-digitisation-of-land-information-system-nationwide)).<br><br>A national mortgage registry is described as ready for deployment with no launch date, custodian arrangement or data-protection basis stated, and its governing policy is still a draft under stakeholder validation ([registry](https://fmino.gov.ng/housing-sector-stakeholders-converge-to-validate-national-housing-data-reform-policies/)).<br><br>One state has a live compensation and valuation land system ([state system](https://www.thisdaylive.com/2025/02/04/kaduna-unveils-software-to-improve-land-administration)).</details> | Advanced |
| Rwanda | [Electronic titles replaced physical deeds](https://www.environment.gov.rw/news-detail/rwanda-launched-electronic-land-registration-certificate-system), and a [district correction exercise](https://realrwanda.rw/en/kayonza-land-correction-drive-issues-over-28000-titles-cuts-boundary-wrangles) concluded in June 2026 with the fee waived. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The e-Title system issues titles electronically on the registrar's approval, for download from the land portal, discontinuing physical deeds; the Rwf 5,000 printing fee is now payable only where an owner asks in writing for a printed copy, and non-notarial transactions run on the national services portal ([e-Title](https://www.environment.gov.rw/news-detail/rwanda-launched-electronic-land-registration-certificate-system)).<br><br>The registry beneath it holds the 11 million parcels registered over 26,000 square kilometres under the 2009 to 2013 regularisation programme, against a prior state in which more than 90 per cent of land lacked documentation ([case study](https://www.un.org/globalgeospatial/sites/default/files/2026-04/rwanda_land_administration_case_study_unggkic.pdf)).<br><br>The district exercise concluded on 17 June 2026, part of a national correction in which 222,423 boundary errors were corrected across 825 villages with the fee waived ([exercise](https://realrwanda.rw/en/kayonza-land-correction-drive-issues-over-28000-titles-cuts-boundary-wrangles), [disputes](https://allafrica.com/stories/202602180018.html)).<br><br>The scale of the correction is the finding: a digitised cadastre that needed errors fixed at that volume was carrying them before the exercise, and nothing on file states how they arose. The registry's capacity rests on a twinning arrangement with a foreign cadastral agency running 2022 to 2027, with nothing published on delivery ([twinning](https://d-portal.iatistandard.org/ctrack.html#view=act&aid=SE-0-SE-6-15037)).</details> | Advanced |
| Sao Tome and Principe | The property registry system held [105,535 entries at March 2024](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099112024053534841/pdf/P16212912eb16e03d183301d6b1e658ec19.pdf) against a year-end target of 133,151, of an estimated 200,000 records to be catalogued. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The status report names the new information system, records 105,535 real estate and vehicle registries entered as at 15 March 2024 against a December 2024 target of 133,151, and states that the registries are estimated to hold about 200,000 records, 162,000 property and 38,000 vehicle, with at least 65 per cent to be digitalised and cataloguing ongoing ([status report](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099112024053534841/pdf/P16212912eb16e03d183301d6b1e658ec19.pdf)).<br><br>It also flags a risk that the IT investments financed may not be sustainable without more skilled staff ([status report](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099112024053534841/pdf/P16212912eb16e03d183301d6b1e658ec19.pdf)).<br><br>The directorate's own page lists the registrable acts on buildings and land and the certificates the registry issues, with fees by turnaround ([services](https://dgrn.gov.st/reg_pred_cid.php)). No later count is held, so whether the December 2024 target was met is unestablished.</details> | Advanced |
| Senegal | [832,526 parcels were surveyed at 30 June 2026](https://procasef.com/comite-de-pilotage-du-procasef-des-resultats-historiques-salues-et-un-ptba-2026-revise-adopte/), against 673,000 mapped at the November 2025 supervision. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The steering committee puts the register at [832,526 parcels surveyed, 634,861 carrying a cadastral identifier, 550,072 deliberations validated and 441,315 parcels approved at 30 June 2026, with more than 70,000 women holding an occupancy title](https://procasef.com/comite-de-pilotage-du-procasef-des-resultats-historiques-salues-et-un-ptba-2026-revise-adopte/).<br><br>That is up from the supervision mission of November 2025, which recorded [673,000 parcels mapped, 469,000 assigned a cadastral identifier and 351,899 occupancy titles recorded in the land information system against 174,680 at 31 May 2025 — and zero municipal land bureaux yet equipped and operational with system access against a target of 120](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099122125122530674/pdf/P172422-ab8683a5-9e88-4886-8cab-aa59ce4e604d.pdf).<br><br>Two dated deliveries followed: [60 further communes signed second-phase conventions in May 2026 under a $40 million additional financing](https://procasef.com/financement-additionnel-du-procasef-des-rencontres-dinformation-avec-60-communes-preselectionnees-pour-consolider-la-securisation-fonciere-au-senegal/), and one commune [received 10,318 occupancy titles approved in the identifier system on 9 July 2026](https://procasef.com/vallee-du-fleuve-senegal-et-ferlo-la-commune-de-dodel-franchit-une-etape-majeure-dans-la-securisation-fonciere-avec-la-remise-officielle-de-10-318-titres-daffectation/).</details> | Advanced |
| Seychelles | The land register runs on the [1967 Act](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/sey116490.pdf); a [land search system](https://registrationdivision.gov.sc/exciting-update-new-land-searchsystem-coming-soon-to-seychelles/) is announced as coming soon. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Land Registration Act of 1967, consolidated to 30 June 2014 and still the operative framework, establishes the Land Registry, the Land Register and the office of Land Registrar and governs registration, dispositions, searches and fees ([Act](https://faolex.fao.org/docs/pdf/sey116490.pdf)).<br><br>The lands and ICT departments tendered in November 2024 for a Land Management Administration Information System interfacing with the National Population Database, the digital identity, Immigration and the Registration Division, on a nine-month delivery from award ([tender](https://www.pou.gov.sc/tenders/consultancies/1275-design-and-development-of-the-land-management-administration-information-system-lmais)).<br><br>The Registration Division announced a new land search system as coming soon in 2026 ([announcement](https://registrationdivision.gov.sc/exciting-update-new-land-searchsystem-coming-soon-to-seychelles/)). No award, commissioning or completion statement for either is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Sierra Leone | [Customary land registration](https://sllap.molhcp.gov.sl/customary-land-registration-commences-in-bekeh-loko-chiefdom/) has begun chiefdom by chiefdom, under the [National Land Commission Act 2022](https://www.parliament.gov.sl/uploads/acts/THE%20NATIONAL%20LAND%20COMMISSION%20ACT,%202022.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>Registration commenced in Bekeh Loko chiefdom ([registration](https://sllap.molhcp.gov.sl/customary-land-registration-commences-in-bekeh-loko-chiefdom/)), parcel maps were validated by Rogbere Section 1 landowners in Maforki ([validation](https://sllap.molhcp.gov.sl/field-update-rogbere-section-1-landowners-validate-parcel-maps-in-maforki-chiefdom/)), and land committees in both chiefdoms were pre-sensitised with the commission and FAO in May 2026 ([orientation](https://sllap.molhcp.gov.sl/sierra-leone-land-administration-project-national-land-commission-and-fao-conduct-pre-sensitization-orientation-for-land-committees-in-maforki-and-bekeh-loko-chiefdoms/)).<br><br>The statutory basis is the 2022 Act establishing the National Land Commission ([Act](https://www.parliament.gov.sl/uploads/acts/THE%20NATIONAL%20LAND%20COMMISSION%20ACT,%202022.pdf)), and the project's implementation status report of December 2025 is the supervision record ([report](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099121525063029143/txt/P177031-b3b42e13-eea4-4c5b-b39d-cc8c986128c4.txt)).<br><br>Two chiefdoms is the extent of what is registered. No parcel count, no national register, and no timetable for extension beyond the pilot chiefdoms is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Somalia | No national register or cadastre exists. The responsible ministry's own [five-year plan](https://mpwr.gov.so/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/mpwr-5years-Strategic-Plan.pdf) treats cadastral system development as a future priority. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The plan cover-dated October 2024 sets out the ministry's land-zoning and land-management mandate under a 1962 decree law and two later statutes, and treats cadastral system development as a priority still to be undertaken ([plan](https://mpwr.gov.so/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/mpwr-5years-Strategic-Plan.pdf)) — the clearest federal statement that no operational register exists.<br><br>A first bi-annual land sector coordination meeting in Mogadishu in September 2025 revised terms of reference for a national coordination platform ([meeting](https://igad.int/igad-concludes-an-inaugural-land-sector-actors-coordination-meeting-in-somalia/)).<br><br>What exists instead is subnational: geographic-information property and land systems supported across 59 districts ([report](https://mptf.undp.org/sites/default/files/documents/2024-07/jplg_iii_integrated_final_report_02.2024.pdf)).</details> | No change |
| South Africa | Sections of the deeds amendment [came into operation in September 2025](https://www.gov.za/sites/default/files/gcis_document/202509/53274pr286.pdf) and [the fee schedule was replaced in February 2026](https://www.gov.za/sites/default/files/gcis_document/202603/54225gon7180.pdf), on [a 2019 electronic registration statute](https://www.gov.za/sites/default/files/gcis_document/201910/42744gon1293.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The Electronic Deeds Registration Systems Act of 2019 is the statute creating an electronic deeds register](https://www.gov.za/sites/default/files/gcis_document/201910/42744gon1293.pdf). 2025-09-01 — [named sections of the 2024 amendment came into operation](https://www.gov.za/sites/default/files/gcis_document/202509/53274pr286.pdf), moving the amended registration framework into effect.<br><br>2026-02-27 — [the schedule of deeds office fees was replaced, setting a R52 lodgement fee and revised transfer-fee bands effective one month after publication](https://www.gov.za/sites/default/files/gcis_document/202603/54225gon7180.pdf).<br><br>Seven years after the electronic statute, no source states how much of the register is actually electronic. The one piece of evidence that back-scanning had been completed anywhere came from a fire, reported under rural registry offices.</details> | Advanced |
| South Sudan | No [digital land register](https://www.onecitizendaily.com/index.php/2025/06/18/digital-land-register-key-in-fighting-fraud-expert/) programme, instrument or system is held; the record is one expert's advocacy for a central database. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The advocacy was published in June 2025 in the context of land fraud ([advocacy](https://www.onecitizendaily.com/index.php/2025/06/18/digital-land-register-key-in-fighting-fraud-expert/)).<br><br>A personal identity card is required for land transactions, so the register that would record those transactions does not exist while the credential to conduct them does.</details> | No change |
| Sudan | Offices are [reopening](https://www.sudanakhbar.com/1770191) and the electronic system is [being restored](https://www.sudanakhbar.com/1673321), while a [six-month freeze](https://tryagnews.com/77832) blocks dealings in reconstituted parcels. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The registrar general states the electronic system across state and locality offices was disrupted by the war, offices reverted to paper, and restoration is under way with field preparation for renewed network linkage ([statement](https://www.sudanakhbar.com/1673321)).<br><br>In March 2026 he ordered a six-month freeze on sales and ownership transfers for parcels whose registers had just been reconstituted, after Khartoum offices were burned and forgers were found selling absent owners' land ([order](https://tryagnews.com/77832)).<br><br>The Jabal Awliya office reopened in April under a 2025 settlement law and a chief justice regulation covering record reconstruction ([reopening](https://www.sudanakhbar.com/1770191)). Registration has sat with the judiciary since 1909, across 106 branches ([administration](http://sj.gov.sd/ar/content/page/%D8%A5%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D8%B3%D8%AC%D9%8A%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%A7%D8%B6%D9%8A)).</details> | Mixed, offices reopening under a freeze on transfers |
| Tanzania | [Digital payment proof became mandatory for land transfers from 1 July 2026](https://dailynews.co.tz/tanzania-announces-july-1-2026-as-proof-of-digital-payment-for-major-assets-land-buildings-motor-vehicles/), with [3.13 million citizens using digital land services](https://dailynews.co.tz/akwilapo-government-to-strengthen-land-ownership-nationwide/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The consolidated land registration statute requires a land registry and a land register and provides for a Registrar of Titles](https://oagmis.oag.go.tz/portal/acts/revised/438/download).<br><br>[The lands minister's 2026/27 budget presentation reports continued nationwide expansion of the digital land system and 3.13 million citizens benefiting from land and housing registration services](https://dailynews.co.tz/akwilapo-government-to-strengthen-land-ownership-nationwide/).<br><br>[From 1 July 2026 verified digital-payment records became a mandatory condition for land, building and motor-vehicle ownership transfers processed by the lands ministry, the revenue authority and the business registration agency](https://dailynews.co.tz/tanzania-announces-july-1-2026-as-proof-of-digital-payment-for-major-assets-land-buildings-motor-vehicles/).<br><br>The 3.13 million is a services figure from a budget speech, not a count of registered parcels, and no parcel count is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Togo | Eight member states [approved a draft land registration harmonisation directive](https://croissanceafrique.com/uemoa-directive-sur-lharmonisation-des-regles-regissant-limmatriculation-et-la-propriete-fonciere-au-sein-de-lunion/) in June 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [directive approved at Ouagadougou](https://croissanceafrique.com/uemoa-directive-sur-lharmonisation-des-regles-regissant-limmatriculation-et-la-propriete-fonciere-au-sein-de-lunion/) harmonises land registration and titling rules across the union and covers the establishment of urban land information systems.<br><br>It is a draft approved by ministers, not a transposed rule, and nothing in the base records Togolese implementation of it.</details> | Advanced |
| Tunisia | [Compulsory registration opens in six governorates from November 2026](https://lake.jort.tn/journal-officiel/ar/2026/084.pdf), and [processing time fell from 40 days to 8](https://www.tuniscope.com/article/367118/business/services/reduction-des-delais-d-examen-des-demandes-d-enregsitrement-des-operations-foncieres-345013). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Compulsory land registration runs under a 1964 decree-law dividing roles between the land tribunal, the survey and cadastre office for surveying and plans, and the national land property office for inscription on the livre foncier](https://www.otc.nat.tn/if/obligatory).<br><br>[Six ministerial decisions gazetted in August 2026 open compulsory cadastral survey and registration of unregistered unbuilt property, from 1 November 2026, in named zones of the Kasserine, Mahdia, Jendouba, Medenine, Sfax and Gabes governorates](https://lake.jort.tn/journal-officiel/ar/2026/084.pdf).<br><br>[The land property office reported its second-generation processing system networked across all 22 regional directorates, average request processing cut from 40 days to 8, and about 120,000 online service requests received since March 2022](https://www.tuniscope.com/article/367118/business/services/reduction-des-delais-d-examen-des-demandes-d-enregsitrement-des-operations-foncieres-345013).<br><br>What share of national territory is registered at all is not held.</details> | Advanced |
| Zambia | The [integrated land administration system](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/sites/default/files/images/publication_docs/MINISTERIAL%20STATEMENT%20-%20Ministry%20of%20Lands.pdf) went live in May 2023, and the minister told Parliament the predecessor ran with no maintenance contract from March 2022, creating a conduit for corruption. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system went live after data migration from the predecessor ([system](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/sites/default/files/images/publication_docs/MINISTERIAL%20STATEMENT%20-%20Ministry%20of%20Lands.pdf), [clearance](https://corpus.co.zm/unlocking-efficiency-the-digital-clearance-process/), [gateway](https://www.szi.gov.zm/?p=3792), [expansion](https://techafricanews.com/2026/07/08/smart-zambia-expands-digital-infrastructure-to-reach-every-corner-of-the-country/)).<br><br>The minister's statement that the predecessor ran unmaintained for over a year, creating a conduit for corruption, is a government account of its own prior failure and is the most specific such statement in this ledger.<br><br>The denominator is now on record and it is the finding: [fewer than 450,000 of an estimated 1.2 million numbered properties hold registered title deeds, and about 45,000 owners pay ground rent, yielding roughly K157 million a year against more than K1.2 billion if compliance improved](https://www.pdu.gov.zm/blog/fixing-land-records-is-central-to-zambias-development-agenda). That 1.2 million excludes customary land entirely, so the untitled share of the country is larger than the ratio suggests.</details> | No change |
| Zimbabwe | A [live cadastre portal](http://www.zimcadastre.co.zw/) carries property search and cadastral services, and a [two-week validation pilot](https://www.newzimbabwe.com/legal-discussions-with-vengai-madzima-title-deed-validation-and-reissuance-pilot-programme-of-zimbabwe/) with sixteen law firms ended with no result on file. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Lawyers publicly described the presidential deeds digitisation programme as a scam, naming a businessman as its promoter ([report](https://www.masvingomirror.com/presidential-deeds-digitisation-programme-a-scam-lawyers/)). The allegation is untested and the base holds no government response to it.<br><br>The cadastre portal is live, carrying property search, electronic maps, cadastral and geodetic services and account registration, with surveyor modules behind a requested profile; the page carries no date of its own ([portal](http://www.zimcadastre.co.zw/)).<br><br>The instrument text on securitised deeds is not held; all three accounts of it are law-firm-authored and one firm has a commercial interest in validation volume ([instrument](https://www.newzimbabwe.com/legal-discussions-with-vengai-madzima-title-deed-validation-and-reissuance-pilot-programme-of-zimbabwe/), [guide](https://nehandaradio.com/2025/08/01/avoid-the-rush-avoid-the-risk-how-to-secure-your-property-in-zimbabwe-with-the-new-digital-deed-system), [discussion](https://www.newzimbabwe.com/legal-discussions-with-vengai-madzima-digitization-of-the-deeds-registry-in-zimbabwe)).<br><br>The pilot ran two weeks in April 2026 and is reported by one of the sixteen selected firms, with no result on file ([pilot](https://www.newzimbabwe.com/legal-discussions-with-vengai-madzima-title-deed-validation-and-reissuance-pilot-programme-of-zimbabwe/)).<br><br>Naming the commercial interest of the only sources describing a deeds-validation regime is what a reader needs to weigh them. A public portal exists; what it has issued does not.</details> | Mixed, the cadastre platform advanced while the deeds digitisation programme drew public allegations of fraud |
